01 · 范围与运行快照

本文审计 TW-75 当前正式训练，供 TW-81 逐项审阅。目标：稳定走路与站立中的手部表达，暂不做蹲坐切换。实际配置、实现限制和未来建议分开说明。本次只生成文档，不修改或重启训练。

快照时间 2026-10-03T15:13:17+08:00；代码基线 80a531820894bb8f469577103ff232859dcff16e。状态不是实时看板。

种子状态累计迭代 / 目标日志ETA
42training31961 / 3559601:02:47
123training31424 / 3559601:14:27

两张4090分别跑seed42/GPU0、seed123/GPU1，每组1024环境；模型、优化器独立，无梯度同步。completed表示训练脚本中的训练、回放和视频结束，不等于任务验收通过。

本阶段范围与交付目标是否准确？

02 · 完整流程与控制目标

人体NPY/文本/官方划分 → 类别与来源审计 → C++重定向 → 28到30坐标映射 → 质量审核与时间拉伸 → Isaac FK → 类别平衡manifest → PPO接触动力学训练 → 留出动作回放与视频。

策略读取当前参考状态与机器人状态，输出30维关节位置动作，经目标限速与PD执行。训练机器人受模拟重力、惯性和地面接触影响。离线FK/人体对照视频属于运动学检查，不能替代动力学验收。文本仅用于离线选数据；当前是参考条件跟踪器，不是文本动作生成器，无独立的风格判别器。

是否还需要自主生成动作或显式动作切换？

03 · 数据与划分

扫描29228数组=14614原始+镜像；镜像不作为独立样本。选取走路/站立上肢相关类别的全部候选，无top-N上限；不是整个HumanML3D所有类别。源NPY：/root/gpufree-data/datasets/practice9/humanml3d_rebuild/staging-v1/HumanML3D/new_joints；文本与官方划分在 /root/gpufree-data/datasets/HumanML3D-official/HumanML3D。

阶段规则
文本优先walk/march/pace；否则arm/hand/wave/gesture/clap/point/salute或stand/stationary/idle归站立上肢。合并所有描述后判断。
排除跑跳、舞蹈、坐蹲跪爬躺、踢拳、楼梯/椅子/游泳等关键词；混合描述可能被整体排除。
数组[T,22,3]，至少40帧，数值有限，源20Hz。
站立几何根水平路径≤.30m，高度变化≤.12m，脚水平跨度≤.20m。
来源隔离沿用官方划分；去掉410条与val/test原始录制来源相同的train片段。未额外证明val/test彼此来源隔离。
候选5900：train3090/1548、val218/100、test644/300（走路/站立）。
膝弯曲standing_upper双膝角集合p90≤40°；排除126条，5774条送重定向。不是每帧直立保证。
最终质量/FK后5361条；实际划分如下。
集合走路站立总数参考帧数
train2910129942094981348
val21084294371075
test6072518581041585

训练4209条、4981348帧=约27.67小时处理后50Hz参考，含减速拉伸，不能当作原始录制总时长。标签为启发式，可能误分或漏掉动作。

类别覆盖、过滤规则、来源隔离是否足够？

04 · 重定向与几何

采用指定tw_retargeting原生C++ QP方法：stream模式、iterations=1、原生90Hz输出，再线性重采样50Hz。求解器几何从修正后urdf0711_training_30dof.urdf派生；关节限位与肢段长度来自训练模型，修正chest坐标框架和任务朝向，保留原方法。

q_training=sign×q_native+offset；28求解器关节映射30自由度，两侧foot_roll参考为0。坐标契约tw44_table_v2。派生模型100随机姿态FK矩阵等价误差记录为0；这不是人体重定向零误差。QP KKT残差需有限且≤1e-6。

  "name": "previous_head_axis",

上述position-scale/posture-bias/acceleration-cost是离线重定向参数，不是RL奖励。mean-distance-mm=5不是全部动作通过5mm认证。完整原生实现与映射表见附录。

几何、零位、坐标与重定向目标权重是否正确？

05 · 参考数据处理

源[x,y,z]转机器人[z,x,y]，初始髋横向对齐−Y并保留后续转向。根相对第一帧平移缩放0.28963，名义高度0.28m；双髋方向恢复yaw，根参考未恢复完整roll/pitch。28→30映射后按URDF限位裁剪并记录比例；本轮要求clipped_fraction=0。

s=max(1, joint_p99_ratio/.9, joint_peak_ratio/1.2, root_xy_p99/.6, root_xy_peak/.8, root_yaw_p99/2, root_yaw_peak/3)

每片时间拉伸最多6倍，超过需求上限则质量失败。关节、根平移、展开的yaw同步线性插值，输出50Hz，再求新时间轴导数。控制50Hz不表示人体原速；本轮没有逐渐加速课程。

根缩放、倾斜简化和动作节奏是否合理？

06 · 质量门槛与FK

检查实际规则
近限位距边界≤max(.001rad,关节范围1%)；任一关节占比>5%或连续>.25s为严格失败。
关节速度超速元素占比≤.5%，每关节p99速度比例≤1，峰值≤1.2。
根速度水平p99≤.6、峰值≤.8m/s；yaw p99≤2、峰值≤3rad/s。
当前例外政策用户视觉审阅后仅near_limit_fraction/run允许提示，保留strict_quality_pass、原始原因及acceptance_policy。按本轮政策quality_pass不等于原严格全通过。
FKIsaac写入参考状态、批量128求身体位姿速度，非动力学接触验证。
地面以整段双脚碰撞包围角点最低值计算一个固定Z偏移，使最低处=1mm；非逐帧贴地，仍可能悬空或滑脚。
FK门槛base/root状态误差≤1e-5；速度与差分的一致性p95线速度≤.05m/s，角速度≤.2rad/s。

政策tw75_user_review_20261003_near_limits_advisory。FK batch1与128关节角速度相同、身体位置最大差1.62e-6m；启动记录83项测试通过。门槛不保证完整支撑时序或语义正确。

哪些质量提示需要收紧？

07 · 采样与episode

类别重置先验走路/站立各50%，类内片段等权；weight=.5/类片段数/(frames−1)，抵消长片段起始帧数偏置。adaptive_uniform_ratio=1，本轮不混失败驱动采样。

随机片段、随机起始帧，用参考根/关节状态初始化再加扰动。参考每策略步前进1帧；片尾终止重置，不无缝接另一个动作。训练episode上限30秒，runner初始episode长度随机化。50/50是重置概率，不保证实际帧数或时间占比各半。curriculum=None，固定混合训练。

需不需要类内困难样本、完整起步或动作切换？

08 · 动力学与执行器

30自由度浮动基座、平地重力接触、convex_hull碰撞、自碰撞关闭、固定关节合并；位置/速度求解迭代8/4。质量取URDF约0.841kg；资产初始Z=.264m，episode重置由参考覆盖。电机未选定，以下是仿真假设。

组KpKd力矩N·m速度rad/sarmature动作尺度rad
主要腿含ankle_yaw2014030.0010.5
ankle_pitch/foot_roll100.51530.0010.375
腰100.52020.0010.5
臂50.25820.00050.4
颈30.15320.00020.25

ImplicitActuator由物理引擎执行PD，无指定电机电流环、热限制、真实力矩转速曲线或通信延迟。地面静/动摩擦1/1、multiply组合；soft_joint_pos_limit_factor=1。USD force_usd_conversion=False允许缓存，URDF哈希不等于重新认证已加载USD。

电机假设、自碰撞关闭与碰撞体是否合适？

09 · 控制频率与动作

物理dt=.005s=200Hz，decimation4；策略/观测/目标50Hz，目标保持4物理步。不是硬件电流环频率。

q_raw = default_joint_position + action_scale * action
delta = clamp(q_raw-q_previous_target, ±velocity_limit*.02*.5)
q_target = q_previous_target + delta
PD + physics -> actual state

tau=0无指数低通，目标变化率限制启用；腿/脚每步最多.03rad，腰/臂/颈.02rad，即1.5/1rad/s。不是实际速度保证或参考统一减半。重置记忆从实际角初始化。动作相对默认姿态，不是参考角残差；clip_actions=null，变化率限制不等于绝对幅度裁剪。

目标限速是否压制拟人快速动作？

10 · 观测与随机化

Actor171维、Critic291维，已核对日志；无RNN、历史堆叠或未来多帧参考。

Actor项维度含义均匀噪声
motion_command60参考30角+30角速度无
motion_anchor_pos_b3参考chest相对实际chest位置无
motion_anchor_ori_b6相对旋转矩阵前两列±.03
base_lin_vel3基座局部线速度±.10m/s
base_ang_vel3基座局部角速度±.15rad/s
anchor_lin_vel_error_b3chest局部参考减实际线速度无
anchor_ang_vel_error_b3chest局部参考减实际角速度无
joint_pos_rel30实际角减默认角±.01rad
joint_vel_rel30实际速度减默认速度±.30rad/s
last_action30上一步动作无

Critic含command60、锚点位置3/朝向6、14刚体相对位置42/朝向84、基座速度各3、关节角/速度各30、动作30，无上述显式噪声。

阶段范围
启动一次静摩擦.7–1.3、动摩擦.6–1.1、恢复0–.1，共32桶；默认关节角±.005rad；chest质心各轴±.002m。
重置根姿态x/y±.02m，z±.005m，roll/pitch±.05rad，yaw±.10rad。
重置根速度x/y±.25m/s，z±.10m/s，roll/pitch±.25rad/s，yaw±.40rad/s。
重置关节参考角±.05rad再clip，参考关节速度。
未启用当前EventCfg无持续推力、延迟、全身质量或电机强度随机化。

评估关闭观测噪声仍保留材质/重置随机性；实机需要可获得这些状态与参考。

观测可用性和随机化是否充分？

11 · 完整奖励函数

指数项r=exp(−E/σ²)，E为平方误差或多刚体/关节均方；朝向为四元数旋转差。σ为敏感度尺度而非硬阈值；位置m、角度rad、速度m/s或rad/s。奖励按策略步长加权积分。

实际项权重σ实现
motion_joint_vel_cost-0.2—motion_joint_velocity_error_l2
joint_acc-2.0e-06—joint_acc_l2
joint_torque-0.0002—joint_torques_l2
action_rate_l2-0.2—action_rate_l2
joint_limit-5.0—joint_pos_limits
foot_roll_default-0.02—joint_deviation_l1
motion_global_anchor_pos1.00.15motion_global_anchor_position_error_exp
motion_global_anchor_ori0.750.4motion_global_anchor_orientation_error_exp
motion_core_pos1.00.15motion_relative_body_position_error_exp
motion_leg_pos1.50.12motion_relative_body_position_error_exp
motion_foot_pos0.750.08motion_relative_body_position_error_exp
motion_arm_pos0.60.18motion_relative_body_position_error_exp
motion_body_ori0.750.5motion_relative_body_orientation_error_exp
motion_body_lin_vel0.750.75motion_global_body_linear_velocity_error_exp
motion_body_ang_vel0.52.0motion_global_body_angular_velocity_error_exp
motion_joint_pos0.350.5motion_joint_position_error_exp
feet_slide-0.2—feet_slide
undesired_contacts-0.2—undesired_contacts

anchor=chest；core=base_link/chest；leg=双侧thigh/mid_leg/foot，foot重复单独加奖；arm=上臂/前臂/手腕。joint_pos跟踪28参考关节，不含foot_roll；joint_vel_cost是30关节速度误差均方。

相对身体奖励对齐参考水平位置和yaw到实际chest，保留参考高度与相对形态，全局anchor奖另约束整体；身体速度用世界坐标比较。joint_acc/torque为平方和；action_rate为原始动作差平方和，不等于限速后角变化；joint_limit软限位外偏差；foot_roll_default默认角L1。脚接触力历史>1N时惩罚水平滑速；非脚部>1N接触计罚。

拟人性审阅：上肢.6权重/.18m尺度比腿1.5/.12m宽松；平均可能掩盖手腕不足。关节角尺度.5rad≈28.6°。减加速度/动作变化可能压制合理快速动作，需看实际各项奖励贡献，不能只比较不同量纲权重。无独立风格判别、参考脚步接触时序、手腕方向专用奖或站立漂移专用奖。

需要提高哪些模仿权重、收紧尺度或分组平滑？

12 · 终止与指标

条件实际实现
time_out训练30s，play极长episode，timeout截断
motion_end到片尾，正常终止/timeout
anchor_poschest参考与实际Z差>.12m
anchor_ori参考/实际chest坐标中重力向量Z分量差>.8；不是.8rad
ee_body_pos任意脚或手腕参考对齐位置与实际Z差>.12m

异常终止不都等于摔倒，尤其ee可能是手腕。训练随机帧起步，motion_end比例不等于完整片段成功率。reward高、位置误差低或episode长均不能单独证明拟人。

仅Z终止是否忽略水平漂移？

13 · PPO与续训

项目配置
算法RSL-RL PPO / OnPolicyRunner
采样1024环境×24步=24576 transitions/update；24步=.48s
更新5 epochs、4 minibatches，每batch约6144条
网络Actor/Critic各512→256→128、ELU；Gaussian init_std=1、scalar配置
归一化Actor/Critic obs_normalization=false；无RND/对称增强
学习率配置.001，adaptive，desired_KL=.01，运行中非固定
回报gamma=.99、GAE lambda=.95
损失clip=.2，value系数1、clipped value loss=true，entropy=.005
优化器Adam，max_grad_norm=1
恢复S2 long_velocity/model_15596.pt，actor/critic/optimizer/iteration一并恢复
规模每组新增20000轮，目标累计35596；500轮保存一次

每组理论新增491520000条transition，并非独立人体帧；共享同一旧模型起点，不是从零独立训练。runner初始episode长度随机化。完整agent.yaml和control_runtime.json见附录。

网络、训练量、起点和实验对照设计是否合理？

14 · 自动验证与视频

每模型取val中每类前3条：走路000039/000139/000211，站立000016/000124/000346；每条seed42/123/2026，关闭观测噪声，记录JSON/NPZ。只有6条、18次回放，不是完整294条val、858条test或5种子验收。

固定动作从第0帧开始，步数max(1000,frames+1)；异常后可能重置重跑，必须按episode统计首轮完整完成，不能把总时长当作成功。test保持留出。

每条视频seed42、1000步约20s；侧面eye=(0,−1.2,.15)，lookat=(0,0,−.03)，跟随tau=.5。需验证实际编码、帧数、姿态与镜头。视频按模拟时间输出，慢动作需检查参考拉伸。

完整验收需动作完成率、终止原因、手腕/全身跟踪、2–8Hz逐关节抖动、滑脚/接触冲击与限幅。旧S2推荐模型28回放有3异常、未满足原5种子验收，不能替代新扩大数据模型验证。

应扩大哪些未见动作、种子、噪声及原速条件？

15 · 后续提议，尚未实施

先完成当前轮，不中途改配置。之后速度课程可按每片拉伸s_i设r_i=1+α(s_i−1)，α由0到1恢复原时间尺度；需连续相位、关节/根插值、四元数插值、所有速度同步、片尾和随机起始逻辑调整。

依据未见动作跟踪/接触/稳定性推进阶段；先核查目标限速和执行器约束，保持50/200Hz，频率另做实验。可单独对照手腕跟踪、分组平滑、站立漂移支撑和接触时序，避免数据/PD/奖励/速度一次混改。

请标记优先级，本文不会自动实施。

SHA256 66b52936b8b61543cae995d37877a856f7d4b3bbd0aff62c5438dc6786b3923b

   1  """Audit all rebuilt HumanML3D sources; select scoped categories without a cap."""
   2  import csv,json,re,collections
   3  from pathlib import Path
   4  import numpy as np
   5  DATA=Path('/root/gpufree-data/datasets')
   6  ROOT=DATA/'HumanML3D-official/HumanML3D'
   7  JOINTS=DATA/'practice9/humanml3d_rebuild/staging-v1/HumanML3D/new_joints'
   8  OUT=DATA/'practice9/tw75_dataset_v1';OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
   9  WALK=re.compile(r'\b(walk\w*|march\w*|pac(?:e|es|ing))\b',re.I)
  10  ARM=re.compile(r'\b(wav\w*|gestur\w*|clap\w*|point\w*|salut\w*|arm\w*|hand\w*)\b',re.I)
  11  STAND=re.compile(r'\b(stand\w*|stationary|idle)\b',re.I)
  12  EXCLUDE=re.compile(r'\b(run\w*|jog\w*|jump\w*|hop\w*|danc\w*|sit\w*|seat\w*|squat\w*|crouch\w*|kneel\w*|crawl\w*|lying|lie|lying|kick\w*|punch\w*|stairs?|climb\w*|chair|ladder|swim\w*|cartwheel\w*|somersault\w*)\b',re.I)
  13  family={Path(r['new_name']).stem:r['source_path'] for r in csv.DictReader(open(DATA/'HumanML3D-official/index.csv'))}
  14  splits={}
  15  for split in ['train','val','test']:
  16   for id in (ROOT/(split+'.txt')).read_text().split():splits[id]=split
  17  heldout={family[id.lstrip('M')] for id,s in splits.items() if s!='train' and id.lstrip('M') in family}
  18  counts=collections.Counter();rows=[];reject=collections.Counter();sources=sorted(JOINTS.glob('*.npy'))
  19  for source in sources:
  20   id=source.stem;counts['available_arrays']+=1
  21   if id.startswith('M'):counts['mirror_arrays']+=1;continue
  22   counts['original_arrays']+=1
  23   textpath=ROOT/'texts'/(id+'.txt')
  24   if not textpath.exists():reject['missing_caption']+=1;continue
  25   captions=[s.split('#')[0] for s in textpath.read_text().splitlines() if s.strip()];text=' '.join(captions)
  26   if EXCLUDE.search(text):reject['outside_scope_caption']+=1;continue
  27   category='walking' if WALK.search(text) else ('standing_upper' if ARM.search(text) or STAND.search(text) else None)
  28   if category is None:reject['no_scoped_caption']+=1;continue
  29   try:
  30    x=np.load(source,mmap_mode='r',allow_pickle=False)
  31    if x.ndim!=3 or x.shape[1:]!=(22,3) or len(x)<40 or not np.isfinite(x).all():reject['invalid_or_short']+=1;continue
  32    root=x[:,0];xy=root[:,[0,2]];path=float(np.linalg.norm(np.diff(xy,axis=0),axis=1).sum());height=float(np.ptp(root[:,1]));foot=float(np.max(np.ptp(x[:,[7,8,10,11]][:,:,[0,2]],axis=0)))
  33    if category=='standing_upper' and (path>.30 or height>.12 or foot>.20):reject['standing_geometry_moving_or_height']+=1;continue
  34    split=splits.get(id)
  35    if split is None:reject['missing_official_split']+=1;continue
  36    group=family.get(id)
  37    if group is None:reject['missing_source_family']+=1;continue
  38    if split=='train' and group in heldout:reject['train_source_overlaps_heldout']+=1;continue
  39    rows.append(dict(id=id,source=str(source),category=category,split=split,source_family=group,captions=captions,frames=len(x),root_path_m=path,root_height_range_m=height,foot_xy_range_m=foot))
  40   except (ValueError,OSError) as e:reject['array_read_error']+=1
  41  for cat in ['walking','standing_upper']:
  42   chosen=[r for r in rows if r['category']==cat]
  43   (OUT/(cat+'.inputs.txt')).write_text(''.join(r['source']+'\n' for r in chosen))
  44   for split in ['train','val','test']:
  45    subset=[r for r in chosen if r['split']==split];counts[cat+'_'+split]=len(subset)
  46    (OUT/(cat+'_'+split+'.ids.txt')).write_text(''.join(r['id']+'\n' for r in subset))
  47  summary=dict(counts=counts,rejections=reject,total_caption_files=len(list((ROOT/'texts').glob('*.txt'))),selected=len(rows),method='Full available-array scan; no top-N cap. Conservative caption+geometry candidates, not quality-approved. Mirror arrays excluded as augmentation duplicates. Official splits retained and overlapping original source families removed from train. Caption keywords are imperfect; manual samples and retarget/support audits required.')
  48  (OUT/'selection.json').write_text(json.dumps(rows,indent=2)+'\n');(OUT/'audit.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))
/root/gpufree-data/projects/HUMANOID_TW/scripts/practice9/prepare_tw75_training.py

SHA256 3a070165eed28c97737ffc452b202682ff05d60eab33d54ddb493de14c20c820

   1  #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2  """Full scoped corpus, user-reviewed near-limit policy, resumable Isaac FK shards."""
   3  import argparse,json,os,subprocess,sys
   4  from pathlib import Path
   5  import numpy as np
   6  ROOT=Path('/root/gpufree-data/datasets/practice9/tw75_dataset_v1')
   7  OUT=Path('/root/gpufree-data/datasets/practice9/tw75_train_v1')
   8  POLICY='tw75_user_review_20261003_near_limits_advisory'
   9  ADVISORY={'joint_near_limit_fraction','joint_near_limit_run'}
  10  
  11  def eligible(record):
  12      quality=record.get('quality',{})
  13      reasons=set(quality.get('reasons',[]))
  14      return (record.get('quality_pass') is True or (bool(reasons) and reasons<=ADVISORY)) and record.get('clipped_fraction',1)==0
  15  
  16  def standing_upright(row):
  17      if row['category']!='standing_upper':return True
  18      a=np.load(row['source']);angles=[]
  19      for hip,knee,ankle in [(1,4,7),(2,5,8)]:
  20          u=a[:,knee]-a[:,hip];v=a[:,ankle]-a[:,knee]
  21          cosine=(u*v).sum(1)/(np.linalg.norm(u,axis=1)*np.linalg.norm(v,axis=1)).clip(1e-8)
  22          angles.append(np.degrees(np.arccos(np.clip(cosine,-1,1))))
  23      return float(np.percentile(angles,90))<=40
  24  
  25  def main(worker):
  26      OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True);status=OUT/f'prepare_{worker}.status';status.write_text('running')
  27      rows=json.load(open(ROOT/'selection.json'));kept=[r for r in rows if standing_upright(r)]
  28      rejected=[r['id'] for r in rows if r not in kept]
  29      (OUT/f'selection_{worker}.json').write_text(json.dumps({'kept':len(kept),'excluded_non_upright_ids':rejected}))
  30      batches=[kept[i:i+500] for i in range(0,len(kept),500)]
  31      for i,records in enumerate(batches):
  32          if i%2!=worker:continue
  33          dest=OUT/'batches'/f'{i:03d}';dest.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  34          if (dest/'manifest.json').exists():continue
  35          inp=dest/'inputs.txt';inp.write_text(''.join(r['source']+'\n' for r in records))
  36          raw=dest/'retarget_manifest.json'
  37          if not raw.exists():
  38              with open(dest/'retarget.log','w') as log:
  39                  subprocess.run([sys.executable,'scripts/practice9/retarget_humanml3d.py','--input',str(inp),'--output-dir',str(dest/'retargeted'),'--retarget-root',str(ROOT/'native_training_geometry'),'--allow-quality-failures','--continue-on-error'],stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,check=True)
  40          payload=json.load(open(raw));accepted=[];rejections=[]
  41          for record in payload['motions']:
  42              if eligible(record):
  43                  r=dict(record);r['strict_quality_pass']=record['quality_pass'];r['quality_pass']=True;r['acceptance_policy']=POLICY;accepted.append(r)
  44              else:rejections.append({'id':record['id'],'quality':record['quality']})
  45          payload['motions']=accepted;payload['acceptance_policy']=POLICY
  46          (dest/'training_eligible.json').write_text(json.dumps(payload,indent=2));(dest/'rejected.json').write_text(json.dumps(rejections,indent=2))

  50          print('completed',i,'retarget eligible',len(accepted),flush=True)
  51      status.write_text('completed')

  56      except BaseException:

   1  """Build split/category-balanced manifests from audited FK outputs only."""

SHA256 ce46b8786ce3d2b69abc5c0d2a0ae8e14dfd3256d28671c7901e0a1053009ba2

   1  """Equal category mass, then equal clip mass, independent of clip duration."""
   2  def balance(records, category_shares=None):
   3      shares=category_shares or {'walking':.5,'standing_upper':.5}
   4      groups={c:[r for r in records if r['category']==c] for c in shares}
   5      if any(not group for group in groups.values()):
   6          raise ValueError('Both categories must have eligible motions; cannot silently train one category')
   7      result=[]
   8      for category,items in groups.items():
   9          for item in items:
  10              frames=int(item['frames'])
  11              if frames<2:raise ValueError('At least two frames required')
  12              # MotionLibrary weights each bin by its number of startable frames.
  13              result.append(dict(item,weight=shares[category]/len(items)/(frames-1)))
  14      return result

SHA256 9451f97ed7d4d4b15a6a0fa8e6b0abc381de0ac852dda6f524794c4d3bf674fb

   1  #!/usr/bin/env python3
   2  """Derive an isolated 28-coordinate native solver from the training geometry.
   3  
   4  The two added foot-roll DoFs are held at zero, as in existing retarget output.
   5  No training URDF or original native source is modified.
   6  """
   7  import argparse, hashlib, json, shutil, subprocess
   8  import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
   9  from pathlib import Path
  10  import numpy as np
  11  from audit_tw75_kinematics import fk
  12  from retarget_humanml3d import DEFAULT_ROOT, DEFAULT_TRAINING_URDF, MAPPING, CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES, map_28_to_30
  13  from native_retarget import NATIVE_JOINT_NAMES
  14  
  15  
  16  def build(output, foot_orientation_weight=1.0, knee_reference=0.0):
  17      if not np.isfinite(knee_reference) or not -.5 <= knee_reference <= 0:
  18          raise ValueError("Diagnostic knee reference must be in [-.5,0]")
  19      if not np.isfinite(foot_orientation_weight) or not 0 <= foot_orientation_weight <= 1:
  20          raise ValueError("Foot orientation weight must be in [0,1]")
  21      output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  22      for directory in ['src', 'include', 'tests', 'configs']:
  23          shutil.copytree(DEFAULT_ROOT/directory, output/directory, dirs_exist_ok=True)
  24      shutil.copy2(DEFAULT_ROOT/'CMakeLists.txt', output/'CMakeLists.txt')
  25      tree = ET.parse(DEFAULT_TRAINING_URDF)
  26      root = tree.getroot()
  27      links = {}
  28      for side in ['left', 'right']:
  29          links.update({f'{side}_mid_leg': f'{side}_calf', f'{side}_calf': f'{side}_ankle',
  30                        f'{side}_ankle': f'{side}_foot', f'{side}_foot': f'{side}_sole',
  31                        f'{side}_wrist': f'{side}_hand', f'{side}_hand': f'{side}_hand_tip'})
  32      for link in root.findall('link'):
  33          link.set('name', links.get(link.get('name'), link.get('name')))
  34          # Solver consumes kinematics only; do not carry unresolved mesh paths.
  35          for tag in ['visual', 'collision']:
  36              for node in list(link.findall(tag)):
  37                  link.remove(node)
  38      for joint in root.findall('joint'):
  39          for tag in ['parent','child']:
  40              node=joint.find(tag); node.set('link', links.get(node.get('link'), node.get('link')))
  41          name=joint.get('name')
  42          if name in MAPPING and MAPPING[name][0] is not None:
  43              native, sign, offset=MAPPING[name]
  44              if offset != 0:
  45                  raise ValueError('Nonzero coordinate offsets require explicit origin composition')
  46              joint.set('name',native)
  47              axis=joint.find('axis'); axis.set('xyz',' '.join(str(sign*float(x)) for x in axis.get('xyz').split()))
  48              limit=joint.find('limit')
  49              lo,hi=sorted(float(limit.get(k))/sign for k in ['lower','upper'])
  50              limit.set('lower',str(lo)); limit.set('upper',str(hi))
  51          elif joint.get('type') != 'fixed':
  52              if name not in ['left_foot_roll','right_foot_roll']:
  53                  raise ValueError('Unexpected unfixed DoF '+name)
  54              joint.set('type','fixed')
  55      destination=output/'assets/urdf/Assembly.urdf'
  56      destination.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  57      tree.write(destination,encoding='utf-8',xml_declaration=True)
  58      q=np.zeros(28); q[16]=-np.pi/2; q[21]=np.pi/2
  59      neutral=fk(destination,dict(zip(NATIVE_JOINT_NAMES,q)))
  60      lengths={}
  61      for side in ['left','right']:
  62          pairs=[('thigh','calf'),('calf','foot'),('upper_arm','force_arm'),('force_arm','hand')]
  63          lengths[side]=[float(np.linalg.norm(neutral[f'{side}_{b}'][:3,3]-neutral[f'{side}_{a}'][:3,3])) for a,b in pairs]
  64      head=float(np.linalg.norm(neutral['head'][:3,3]-neutral['neck_linkage'][:3,3]))
  65      source=(output/'src/robot.cpp').read_text()
  66      anchor='        if(i==16) p->ref[index]=static_cast<float>(-1.57079632679);'
  67      if source.count(anchor)!=1:
  68          raise ValueError('Native reference posture changed')
  69      source=source.replace(anchor,f'        if(i==3 || i==9) p->ref[index]={knee_reference:.17g};\n'+anchor)
  70      replacements={
  71        'p->model.lowerPositionLimit[index]=limits[i][0];':'// Limits are loaded from the derived training URDF.',
  72        'p->model.upperPositionLimit[index]=limits[i][1];':'',
  73        'double(limits[i][0])':'p->model.lowerPositionLimit[p->indices[i]]',
  74        'double(limits[i][1])':'p->model.upperPositionLimit[p->indices[i]]',
  75        'double(limits[i][0]*factor)':'double(p->model.lowerPositionLimit[p->indices[i]]*factor)',
  76        'double(limits[i][1]*factor)':'double(p->model.upperPositionLimit[p->indices[i]]*factor)',
  77        '*.022487329':f'*{head:.17g}',
  78      }
  79      for index,old in enumerate(['*.0832','*.1105','*.07579','*.04739']):
  80          replacements[old]=f'*(side ? {lengths["right"][index]:.17g} : {lengths["left"][index]:.17g})'
  81      for old,new in replacements.items():
  82          if source.count(old)!=1:
  83              raise ValueError('Upstream source changed: '+old)
  84          source=source.replace(old,new)
  85      anchor='wr=(k==7||k==8)?1.:(k==9?5.:0.);'
  86      if source.count(anchor)!=1:
  87          raise ValueError('Foot orientation task changed')
  88      source=source.replace(anchor,f'wr=(k==7||k==8)?{foot_orientation_weight:.17g}:(k==9?5.:0.);')
  89      old_neutral=fk(DEFAULT_ROOT/'assets/urdf/Assembly.urdf',dict(zip(NATIVE_JOINT_NAMES,np.array([(-np.pi/2 if i==16 else np.pi/2 if i==21 else 0.) for i in range(28)]))))
  90      corrections=[]
  91      for task,link in [(0,'chest'),(7,'left_foot'),(8,'right_foot'),(9,'head')]:
  92          rotation=old_neutral[link][:3,:3].T @ neutral[link][:3,:3]
  93          entries=','.join(f'{v:.17g}' for v in rotation.ravel())
  94          corrections.append(f'if(k=={task}){{ M3 frame_offset;frame_offset<<{entries};rot=rot*frame_offset; }}')
  95      anchor='            pinocchio::SE3 goal(rot,pos);'
  96      if source.count(anchor)!=1:
  97          raise ValueError('Upstream orientation task changed')
  98      source=source.replace(anchor,'\n'.join(corrections)+'\n'+anchor)
  99      (output/'src/robot.cpp').write_text(source)
 100      rng=np.random.default_rng(42); error=0.
 101      for _ in range(100):
 102          q=rng.uniform(-.5,.5,28)
 103          native=fk(destination,dict(zip(NATIVE_JOINT_NAMES,q)))
 104          mapped=map_28_to_30(q[None],NATIVE_JOINT_NAMES)[0]
 105          training=fk(DEFAULT_TRAINING_URDF,dict(zip(CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES,mapped)))
 106          for name,T in training.items():
 107              error=max(error,float(np.max(np.abs(native[links.get(name,name)]-T))))
 108      if error>1e-10:
 109          raise ValueError(f'Geometry equivalence failed: {error}')
 110      metadata={'upstream_commit':subprocess.check_output(['git','-C',str(DEFAULT_ROOT),'rev-parse','HEAD'],text=True).strip(),
 111                'training_urdf_sha256':hashlib.sha256(DEFAULT_TRAINING_URDF.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
 112                'derived_urdf_sha256':hashlib.sha256(destination.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
 113                'foot_orientation_weight':foot_orientation_weight,'knee_reference_rad':knee_reference,
 114                'fixed_joints':{'left_foot_roll':0,'right_foot_roll':0},'link_renames':links,
 115                'segment_lengths_m':lengths,'head_length_m':head,'fk_100_pose_max_matrix_error':error}
 116      (output/'derived_model.json').write_text(json.dumps(metadata,indent=2)+'\n')
 117      print(json.dumps(metadata,indent=2))
 118  
 119  if __name__=='__main__':
 120      parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(); parser.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True)
 121      parser.add_argument('--foot-orientation-weight',type=float,default=1.,help='Diagnostic ablation; 1 preserves selected method')
 122      parser.add_argument('--knee-reference',type=float,default=0.,help='Diagnostic knee initial/prior pose in radians')
 123      args=parser.parse_args(); build(args.output,args.foot_orientation_weight,args.knee_reference)

SHA256 cf7204b493ae3d4f45ea00c3c55e68d23c154e68aba189a4f9b06eed33dec089

   1  """Adapter for tw_retargeting's selected native C++ backend; no legacy solver."""
   2  from __future__ import annotations
   3  import hashlib
   4  import json
   5  import os
   6  import re
   7  import subprocess
   8  import tempfile
   9  import sysconfig
  10  from pathlib import Path
  11  import numpy as np
  12  
  13  NATIVE_FPS = 90.0
  14  NATIVE_JOINT_NAMES = [
  15      'left_hip_pitch_joint', 'left_hip_roll_joint', 'left_hip_yaw_joint',
  16      'left_knee_pitch_joint', 'left_ankle_yaw_joint', 'left_ankle_pitch_joint',
  17      'right_hip_pitch_joint', 'right_hip_roll_joint', 'right_hip_yaw_joint',
  18      'right_knee_pitch_joint', 'right_ankle_yaw_joint', 'right_ankle_pitch_joint',
  19      'waist_yaw_joint', 'waist_pitch_joint', 'waist_roll_joint',
  20      'left_shoulder_pitch_joint', 'left_shoulder_roll_joint', 'left_shoulder_yaw_joint',
  21      'left_elbow_pitch_joint', 'left_wrist_yaw_joint',
  22      'right_shoulder_pitch_joint', 'right_shoulder_roll_joint', 'right_shoulder_yaw_joint',
  23      'right_elbow_pitch_joint', 'right_wrist_yaw_joint',
  24      'neck_yaw_joint', 'neck_roll_joint', 'neck_pitch_joint',
  25  ]
  26  DEFAULT_ROOT = Path(os.environ.get('GPUFREE_DATA_ROOT', '/root/gpufree-data')) / 'projects/tw_retargeting/IKRetargeting_V3'
  27  
  28  
  29  def resample_native(values, output_fps):
  30      values = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.float64)
  31      if values.ndim != 2 or values.shape[1] != 28 or len(values) < 2 or not np.isfinite(values).all():
  32          raise ValueError(f'Native output must be finite [T>=2,28], got {values.shape}')
  33      if not np.isfinite(output_fps) or output_fps <= 0:
  34          raise ValueError('Output FPS must be finite and positive')
  35      duration = (len(values) - 1) / NATIVE_FPS
  36      count = int(np.floor(duration * output_fps + 1e-8)) + 1
  37      if count < 3:
  38          raise ValueError('At least three output frames required for derivatives')
  39      source_t = np.arange(len(values)) / NATIVE_FPS
  40      target_t = np.arange(count) / output_fps
  41      return np.column_stack([np.interp(target_t, source_t, values[:, joint]) for joint in range(28)])
  42  
  43  
  44  class NativeRetargeter:
  45      def __init__(self, root=DEFAULT_ROOT, binary=None, timeout=120, profile=None):
  46          self.root = Path(root).resolve()
  47          self.binary = Path(binary).resolve() if binary else self.root / 'build/native/tw_retarget'
  48          self.urdf = self.root / 'assets/urdf/Assembly.urdf'
  49          config = Path(profile).resolve() if profile is not None else self.root / 'configs/selected_head_axis.json'
  50          if not self.binary.is_file():
  51              raise FileNotFoundError(f'Build the native backend first: {self.binary}')
  52          self.timeout = float(timeout)
  53          if not np.isfinite(self.timeout) or self.timeout <= 0:
  54              raise ValueError('Native timeout must be positive')
  55          self.flags = json.loads(config.read_text())['flags']
  56          if not isinstance(self.flags, list) or not all(isinstance(x, str) for x in self.flags):
  57              raise ValueError('Selected flags must be a list of strings')
  58          source = (self.root / 'src/robot.cpp').read_text()
  59          match = re.search(r'static const std::vector<std::string> names\s*=\s*\{(.*?)\};', source, re.S)
  60          if match is None or re.findall(r'"([^"]+)"', match.group(1)) != NATIVE_JOINT_NAMES:
  61              raise ValueError('Upstream joint order changed; update adapter explicitly')
  62          derived_path = self.root / 'derived_model.json'
  63          derived = json.loads(derived_path.read_text()) if derived_path.exists() else None
  64          commit = derived['upstream_commit'] if derived else subprocess.run(
  65              ['git', '-C', str(self.root), 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'],
  66              check=True, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=10).stdout.strip()
  67          self.provenance = {
  68              'backend': 'tw_retargeting_cpp', 'upstream_commit': commit,
  69              'native_fps': NATIVE_FPS, 'native_joint_names': NATIVE_JOINT_NAMES,
  70              'binary_sha256': hashlib.sha256(self.binary.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
  71              'selected_config_sha256': hashlib.sha256(config.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
  72              'solver_urdf_sha256': hashlib.sha256(self.urdf.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
  73              'selected_flags': self.flags,
  74              'solver_source_sha256': hashlib.sha256((self.root / 'src/robot.cpp').read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
  75              'derived_model': derived,
  76          }
  77          self.environment = os.environ.copy()
  78          libraries = [self.root / 'build/deps/cmeel.prefix/lib',
  79                       Path(sysconfig.get_paths()['purelib']) / 'cmeel.prefix/lib']
  80          self.environment['LD_LIBRARY_PATH'] = ':'.join(str(p) for p in libraries) + ':' + self.environment.get('LD_LIBRARY_PATH', '')
  81  
  82      def process(self, joints, output_fps):
  83          joints = np.asarray(joints, dtype=np.float64)
  84          if joints.ndim != 3 or joints.shape[1] < 22 or joints.shape[2] != 3 or len(joints) < 9:
  85              raise ValueError('Input must be [T>=9,J>=22,3] at 20 Hz')
  86          if not np.isfinite(joints).all():
  87              raise ValueError('Input contains NaN or Inf')
  88          tmp_root = Path(os.environ.get('GPUFREE_DATA_ROOT', '/root/gpufree-data')) / 'tmp/tw56_native'
  89          tmp_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  90          with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(dir=tmp_root) as tmp:
  91              source = Path(tmp) / 'motion.npy'
  92              output = Path(tmp) / 'out'
  93              np.save(source, np.ascontiguousarray(joints), allow_pickle=False)
  94              command = [str(self.binary), '--urdf', str(self.urdf), '--input', str(source),
  95                         '--out', str(output), '--mode', 'stream', '--iterations', '1'] + self.flags
  96              result = subprocess.run(command, env=self.environment, capture_output=True,
  97                                      text=True, timeout=self.timeout)
  98              if result.returncode:
  99                  raise RuntimeError(f'Native retarget failed ({result.returncode}): {result.stderr[-3000:]}')
 100              values = np.load(output / 'motion.npy', allow_pickle=False)
 101              timing = json.loads((output / 'timing.json').read_text())
 102              residual = float(timing.get('max_kkt', float('inf')))
 103              if not np.isfinite(residual) or residual > 1e-6:
 104                  raise ValueError(f'Native QP residual exceeds contract: {residual}')
 105              return resample_native(values, output_fps), timing

  44      "left_mid_leg_pitch": ("left_knee_pitch_joint", -1.0, 0.0),
  45      "left_calf_yaw": ("left_ankle_yaw_joint", -1.0, 0.0),
  46      "left_ankle_pitch": ("left_ankle_pitch_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  47      "left_foot_roll": (None, 0.0, 0.0),
  48      "right_hip_linkage_pitch": ("right_hip_pitch_joint", -1.0, 0.0),
  49      "right_thigh_roll": ("right_hip_roll_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  50      "right_knee_linkage_yaw": ("right_hip_yaw_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  51      "right_mid_leg_pitch": ("right_knee_pitch_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  52      "right_calf_yaw": ("right_ankle_yaw_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  53      "right_ankle_pitch": ("right_ankle_pitch_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  54      "right_foot_roll": (None, 0.0, 0.0),
  55      "waist_yaw": ("waist_yaw_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  56      "gearbox_roll": ("waist_roll_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  57      "chest_pitch": ("waist_pitch_joint", -1.0, 0.0),
  58      "left_shoulder_linkage_pitch": ("left_shoulder_pitch_joint", -1.0, 0.0),
  59      "left_upper_arm_roll": ("left_shoulder_roll_joint", 1.0, math.pi / 2.0),
  60      "left_elbow_linkage_pitch": ("left_shoulder_yaw_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  61      "left_force_arm_yaw": ("left_elbow_pitch_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  62      "left_wrist_pitch": ("left_wrist_yaw_joint", -1.0, 0.0),
  63      "right_shoulder_linkage_pitch": ("right_shoulder_pitch_joint", -1.0, 0.0),
  64      "right_upper_arm_roll": ("right_shoulder_roll_joint", 1.0, -math.pi / 2.0),
  65      "right_elbow_linkage_pitch": ("right_shoulder_yaw_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  66      "right_force_arm_yaw": ("right_elbow_pitch_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  67      "right_wrist_pitch": ("right_wrist_yaw_joint", -1.0, 0.0),
  68      "neck": ("neck_yaw_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  69      "neck_linkage_roll": ("neck_roll_joint", 1.0, 0.0),
  70      "head_pitch": ("neck_pitch_joint", -1.0, 0.0),
  71  }
  72  
  73  
  74  # Compose old IK -> CAD with the user's CAD -> aligned coordinate table.
  75  from joint_coordinates import COORDINATES, CONTRACT_VERSION, rename
  76  
  77  CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES = [rename(name) for name in CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES]
  78  MAPPING = {
  79      rename(name): (old, COORDINATES.get(name, (name, 1, 0.0))[1] * sign,
  80                     COORDINATES.get(name, (name, 1, 0.0))[1] * offset
  81                     + COORDINATES.get(name, (name, 1, 0.0))[2])
  82      for name, (old, sign, offset) in MAPPING.items()
  83  }
  84  
  85  
  86  def load_limits(training_urdf: Path) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
  87      root = ET.parse(training_urdf).getroot()
  88      by_name = {joint.attrib["name"]: joint for joint in root.findall("joint")}
  89      lower, upper, velocity = [], [], []
  90      for name in CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES:
  91          limit = by_name[name].find("limit")
  92          if limit is None:
  93              raise ValueError(f"Missing limit for {name}")
  94          lower.append(float(limit.attrib["lower"]))
  95          upper.append(float(limit.attrib["upper"]))
  96          velocity.append(float(limit.attrib["velocity"]))
  97      return np.asarray(lower), np.asarray(upper), np.asarray(velocity)
  98  
  99  
 100  def validate_humanml3d(joints: np.ndarray, path: Path) -> np.ndarray:
 101      joints = np.asarray(joints, dtype=np.float64)
 102      if joints.ndim != 3 or joints.shape[1] < 22 or joints.shape[2] != 3:
 103          raise ValueError(f"{path}: expected [T,J>=22,3], got {joints.shape}")
 104      if joints.shape[0] < 9:
 105          raise ValueError(f"{path}: at least 9 frames are required, got {joints.shape[0]}")
 106      if not np.isfinite(joints).all():
 107          raise ValueError(f"{path}: contains NaN or Inf")
 108      return joints
 109  
 110  
 111  def map_28_to_30(old_dof: np.ndarray, old_names: list[str]) -> np.ndarray:
 112      lookup = {name: index for index, name in enumerate(old_names)}
 113      output = np.zeros((old_dof.shape[0], len(CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES)), dtype=np.float64)
 114      for target_index, new_name in enumerate(CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES):
 115          old_name, sign, offset = MAPPING[new_name]
 116          if old_name is not None:
 117              output[:, target_index] = sign * old_dof[:, lookup[old_name]] + offset
 118      return output
 119  
 120  
 121  def restore_root_trajectory(
 122      joints: np.ndarray, output_frames: int, root_scale: float, nominal_height: float, output_fps: float = 50.0
 123  ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
 124      # Same coordinates as IK_V2: HumanML3D [x,y,z] -> robot [z,x,y].
 125      world = joints[..., [2, 0, 1]]
 126      pelvis = world[:, 0]
 127      lateral = world[:, 2] - world[:, 1]  # right hip - left hip
 128      heading = np.unwrap(np.arctan2(lateral[:, 1], lateral[:, 0]))
 129      heading_delta = heading - heading[0]
 130  
 131      # Normalize the initial lateral direction to robot -Y, retain subsequent turns.
 132      align = -math.pi / 2.0 - heading[0]
 133      c, s = math.cos(align), math.sin(align)
 134      rotation = np.array([[c, -s], [s, c]], dtype=np.float64)
 135      relative = pelvis - pelvis[0]
 136      relative_xy = relative[:, :2] @ rotation.T
 137  
 138      source_t = np.arange(joints.shape[0]) / 20.0
 139      output_t = np.arange(output_frames) / output_fps
 140      root_pos = np.empty((output_frames, 3), dtype=np.float64)
 141      root_pos[:, 0] = np.interp(output_t, source_t, relative_xy[:, 0] * root_scale)
 142      root_pos[:, 1] = np.interp(output_t, source_t, relative_xy[:, 1] * root_scale)
 143      root_pos[:, 2] = nominal_height + np.interp(output_t, source_t, relative[:, 2] * root_scale)
 144      yaw = np.interp(output_t, source_t, heading_delta)
 145  
 146      root_quat_xyzw = np.zeros((output_frames, 4), dtype=np.float64)
 147      root_quat_xyzw[:, 2] = np.sin(0.5 * yaw)
 148      root_quat_xyzw[:, 3] = np.cos(0.5 * yaw)
 149      return root_pos, root_quat_xyzw
 150  
 151  
 152  def _gradient(values: np.ndarray, fps: float) -> np.ndarray:
 153      return np.gradient(values, 1.0 / fps, axis=0, edge_order=2)
 154  
 155  
 156  def _yaw_from_quat_xyzw(quat: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
 157      return np.unwrap(2.0 * np.arctan2(quat[:, 2], quat[:, 3]))
 158  
 159  
 160  def _quat_xyzw_from_yaw(yaw: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
 161      quat = np.zeros((len(yaw), 4), dtype=np.float64)
 162      quat[:, 2] = np.sin(0.5 * yaw)
 163      quat[:, 3] = np.cos(0.5 * yaw)
 164      return quat
 165  
 166  
 167  def _max_true_run_seconds(mask: np.ndarray, fps: float) -> np.ndarray:
 168      result = np.zeros(mask.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)
 169      for joint in range(mask.shape[1]):
 170          best = current = 0
 171          for active in mask[:, joint]:
 172              current = current + 1 if active else 0
 173              best = max(best, current)
 174          result[joint] = best / fps
 175      return result
 176  
 177  
 178  def _dynamic_metrics(
 179      joint_pos: np.ndarray,
 180      root_pos: np.ndarray,
 181      root_quat: np.ndarray,
 182      velocity_limits: np.ndarray,
 183      fps: float,
 184  ) -> dict[str, np.ndarray | float]:
 185      joint_vel = _gradient(joint_pos, fps)
 186      speed_ratio = np.abs(joint_vel) / velocity_limits[None, :]
 187      root_xy_speed = np.linalg.norm(_gradient(root_pos[:, :2], fps), axis=1)
 188      yaw_rate = np.abs(_gradient(_yaw_from_quat_xyzw(root_quat), fps))
 189      return {
 190          "speed_ratio": speed_ratio,
 191          "speed_ratio_p99": np.quantile(speed_ratio, 0.99, axis=0),
 192          "speed_ratio_max": np.max(speed_ratio, axis=0),
 193          "speed_violation_fraction": float(np.mean(speed_ratio > 1.0)),
 194          "root_xy_speed_p99": float(np.quantile(root_xy_speed, 0.99)),
 195          "root_xy_speed_max": float(np.max(root_xy_speed)),
 196          "root_yaw_rate_p99": float(np.quantile(yaw_rate, 0.99)),
 197          "root_yaw_rate_max": float(np.max(yaw_rate)),
 198      }
 199  
 200  
 201  def _resample_time(
 202      joint_pos: np.ndarray,
 203      root_pos: np.ndarray,
 204      root_quat: np.ndarray,
 205      stretch: float,
 206  ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
 207      if stretch <= 1.0 + 1.0e-6:
 208          return joint_pos, root_pos, root_quat
 209      output_frames = int(math.ceil((len(joint_pos) - 1) * stretch)) + 1
 210      source_u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, len(joint_pos))
 211      output_u = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, output_frames)
 212      joint_out = np.column_stack(
 213          [np.interp(output_u, source_u, joint_pos[:, index]) for index in range(joint_pos.shape[1])]
 214      )
 215      root_out = np.column_stack(
 216          [np.interp(output_u, source_u, root_pos[:, index]) for index in range(3)]
 217      )
 218      yaw_out = np.interp(output_u, source_u, _yaw_from_quat_xyzw(root_quat))
 219      return joint_out, root_out, _quat_xyzw_from_yaw(yaw_out)
 220  
 221  
 222  def audit_and_time_scale(
 223      joint_pos: np.ndarray,
 224      root_pos: np.ndarray,
 225      root_quat: np.ndarray,
 226      lower: np.ndarray,
 227      upper: np.ndarray,
 228      velocity_limits: np.ndarray,
 229      fps: float,
 230      auto_time_scale: bool,
 231      max_time_scale: float,
 232  ) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray, dict[str, object]]:
 233      initial = _dynamic_metrics(joint_pos, root_pos, root_quat, velocity_limits, fps)
 234      requested = max(
 235          1.0,
 236          float(np.max(initial["speed_ratio_p99"])) / 0.9,
 237          float(np.max(initial["speed_ratio_max"])) / 1.2,
 238          float(initial["root_xy_speed_p99"]) / 0.6,
 239          float(initial["root_xy_speed_max"]) / 0.8,
 240          float(initial["root_yaw_rate_p99"]) / 2.0,
 241          float(initial["root_yaw_rate_max"]) / 3.0,
 242      )
 243      applied = min(requested, max_time_scale) if auto_time_scale else 1.0
 244      joint_pos, root_pos, root_quat = _resample_time(joint_pos, root_pos, root_quat, applied)
 245  
 246      margin = np.maximum(1.0e-3, 0.01 * (upper - lower))
 247      near_limit = ((joint_pos - lower[None, :]) <= margin[None, :]) | (
 248          (upper[None, :] - joint_pos) <= margin[None, :]
 249      )
 250      near_fraction = near_limit.mean(axis=0)
 251      near_run_s = _max_true_run_seconds(near_limit, fps)
 252      final = _dynamic_metrics(joint_pos, root_pos, root_quat, velocity_limits, fps)
 253  
 254      reasons = []
 255      if float(np.max(near_fraction)) > 0.05:
 256          reasons.append("joint_near_limit_fraction")
 257      if float(np.max(near_run_s)) > 0.25:
 258          reasons.append("joint_near_limit_run")
 259      if float(final["speed_violation_fraction"]) > 0.005:
 260          reasons.append("joint_speed_violation_fraction")
 261      if float(np.max(final["speed_ratio_p99"])) > 1.0:
 262          reasons.append("joint_speed_p99")
 263      if float(np.max(final["speed_ratio_max"])) > 1.2:
 264          reasons.append("joint_speed_max")
 265      if float(final["root_xy_speed_p99"]) > 0.6 or float(final["root_xy_speed_max"]) > 0.8:
 266          reasons.append("root_xy_speed")
 267      if float(final["root_yaw_rate_p99"]) > 2.0 or float(final["root_yaw_rate_max"]) > 3.0:
 268          reasons.append("root_yaw_rate")
 269      if requested > max_time_scale + 1.0e-6:
 270          reasons.append("required_time_scale_exceeds_cap")
 271  
 272      quality = {
 273          "quality_pass": not reasons,
 274          "reasons": reasons,
 275          "time_scale_requested": requested,
 276          "time_scale_applied": applied,
 277          "near_limit_fraction_by_joint": dict(zip(CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES, near_fraction.tolist())),
 278          "near_limit_max_run_s_by_joint": dict(zip(CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES, near_run_s.tolist())),
 279          "velocity_ratio_p99_by_joint": dict(zip(CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES, final["speed_ratio_p99"].tolist())),
 280          "velocity_ratio_max_by_joint": dict(zip(CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES, final["speed_ratio_max"].tolist())),
 281          "speed_violation_fraction": float(final["speed_violation_fraction"]),
 282          "root_xy_speed_p99": float(final["root_xy_speed_p99"]),
 283          "root_xy_speed_max": float(final["root_xy_speed_max"]),
 284          "root_yaw_rate_p99": float(final["root_yaw_rate_p99"]),
 285          "root_yaw_rate_max": float(final["root_yaw_rate_max"]),
 286      }
 287      return joint_pos, root_pos, root_quat, quality
 288  
 289  
 290  def iter_inputs(path: Path, pattern: str, limit: int | None) -> list[Path]:
 291      if path.is_file() and path.suffix == '.txt':
 292          files = [Path(line.strip()).expanduser() for line in path.read_text().splitlines()
 293                   if line.strip() and not line.lstrip().startswith('#')]
 294          files = [file if file.is_absolute() else path.parent / file for file in files]
 295      else:
 296          files = [path] if path.is_file() else sorted(path.glob(pattern))
 297      if limit is not None:
 298          files = files[:limit]
 299      if len({file.stem for file in files}) != len(files):
 300          raise ValueError('Input clip stems must be unique')
 301      if any(not file.is_file() for file in files):
 302          raise FileNotFoundError('Input list contains missing files')
 303      if not files:
 304          raise FileNotFoundError(f"No HumanML3D clips found at {path} with pattern {pattern}")
 305      return files
 306  
 307  
 308  def main() -> None:
 309      parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
 310      parser.add_argument("--input", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_INPUT)
 311      parser.add_argument("--pattern", default="*.npy")
 312      parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_OUTPUT)
 313      parser.add_argument("--retarget-root", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_ROOT)
 314      parser.add_argument("--retarget-binary", type=Path)
 315      parser.add_argument("--native-timeout", type=float, default=120)
 316      parser.add_argument("--retarget-profile", type=Path, help="Explicit experimental native flag profile; default unchanged")
 317      parser.add_argument("--training-urdf", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_TRAINING_URDF)
 318      parser.add_argument("--output-fps", type=int, default=50)
 319      parser.add_argument("--root-scale", type=float, default=0.28963)
 320      parser.add_argument("--nominal-root-height", type=float, default=0.28)
 321      parser.add_argument("--max-time-scale", type=float, default=6.0)
 322      parser.add_argument("--auto-time-scale", action=argparse.BooleanOptionalAction, default=True)
 323      parser.add_argument("--allow-quality-failures", action="store_true")
 324      parser.add_argument("--limit", type=int)
 325      parser.add_argument("--continue-on-error", action="store_true")
 326      args = parser.parse_args()
 327  
 328      if args.output_fps <= 0:
 329          raise ValueError("--output-fps must be positive")
 330      if args.max_time_scale < 1.0:
 331          raise ValueError("--max-time-scale must be at least 1")
 332      args.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 333      lower, upper, velocity_limits = load_limits(args.training_urdf)
 334      backend = NativeRetargeter(args.retarget_root, args.retarget_binary, args.native_timeout, profile=args.retarget_profile)
 335      old_names = NATIVE_JOINT_NAMES
 336      inputs = iter_inputs(args.input, args.pattern, args.limit)
 337  
 338      motions, failures = [], []
 339      for index, path in enumerate(inputs, start=1):
 340          motion_id = path.stem
 341          print(f"[{index}/{len(inputs)}] retarget {motion_id}", flush=True)
 342          try:
 343              joints = validate_humanml3d(np.load(path, allow_pickle=False), path)
 344              old_dof, native_timing = backend.process(joints, args.output_fps)
 345              joint_pos = map_28_to_30(old_dof, old_names)
 346              unclipped = joint_pos.copy()
 347              joint_pos = np.clip(joint_pos, lower, upper)
 348              clipped_fraction = float(np.mean(np.abs(unclipped - joint_pos) > 1.0e-7))
 349              root_pos, root_quat = restore_root_trajectory(
 350                  joints, joint_pos.shape[0], args.root_scale, args.nominal_root_height, args.output_fps
 351              )
 352              joint_pos, root_pos, root_quat, quality = audit_and_time_scale(
 353                  joint_pos,
 354                  root_pos,
 355                  root_quat,
 356                  lower,
 357                  upper,
 358                  velocity_limits,
 359                  args.output_fps,
 360                  args.auto_time_scale,
 361                  args.max_time_scale,
 362              )
 363              if not quality["quality_pass"] and not args.allow_quality_failures:
 364                  raise ValueError(f"quality gate failed: {quality['reasons']}")
 365  
 366              output = args.output_dir / f"{motion_id}.retarget.npz"
 367              np.savez_compressed(
 368                  output,
 369                  schema_version=np.asarray([1], dtype=np.int32),
 370                  joint_coordinate_contract=np.asarray([CONTRACT_VERSION]),
 371                  fps=np.asarray([args.output_fps], dtype=np.float32),
 372                  source_fps=np.asarray([20], dtype=np.float32),
 373                  source_id=np.asarray([motion_id]),
 374                  joint_names=np.asarray(CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES),
 375                  joint_pos=joint_pos.astype(np.float32),
 376                  root_pos=root_pos.astype(np.float32),
 377                  root_quat_xyzw=root_quat.astype(np.float32),
 378                  mapping_method=np.asarray(["tw_retargeting_cpp_28dof_to_aligned30"]),
 379                  backend_provenance_json=np.asarray([json.dumps(backend.provenance, sort_keys=True)]),
 380                  native_timing_json=np.asarray([json.dumps(native_timing, sort_keys=True)]),
 381                  clipped_fraction=np.asarray([clipped_fraction], dtype=np.float32),
 382                  quality_pass=np.asarray([quality["quality_pass"]], dtype=np.bool_),
 383                  quality_json=np.asarray([json.dumps(quality, sort_keys=True)]),
 384              )
 385              motions.append({
 386                  "id": motion_id,
 387                  "file": str(output.resolve()),
 388                  "weight": 1.0,
 389                  "source_file": str(path.resolve()),
 390                  "frames": int(joint_pos.shape[0]),
 391                  "clipped_fraction": clipped_fraction,
 392                  "quality_pass": bool(quality["quality_pass"]),
 393                  "quality": quality,
 394              })
 395          except Exception as exc:
 396              failures.append({"id": motion_id, "file": str(path), "error": str(exc)})
 397              traceback.print_exc()
 398              if not args.continue_on_error:
 399                  raise
 400  
 401      manifest = {
 402          "schema_version": 1,
 403          "stage": "retargeted_joint_motion",
 404          "backend": backend.provenance,
 405          "joint_coordinate_contract": CONTRACT_VERSION,
 406          "target_fps": args.output_fps,
 407          "joint_names": CUSTOM_JOINT_NAMES,
 408          "quality_gate_version": 1,
 409          "motions": motions,
 410          "failures": failures,
 411      }
 412      manifest_path = args.output_dir.parent / "retarget_manifest.json"
 413      manifest_path.write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
 414      print(f"Wrote {len(motions)} motions and {len(failures)} failures to {manifest_path}")
 415      if not motions:
 416          raise RuntimeError("No clips passed the retarget quality gate; see manifest failures")
 417  
 418  
 419  if __name__ == "__main__":
 420      main()

  62  from isaaclab.utils.math import axis_angle_from_quat, quat_conjugate, quat_mul
  63  from unitree_rl_lab.assets.robots.custom_humanoid import (
  64      CUSTOM_HUMANOID_30DOF_CFG as ROBOT_CFG,
  65  )
  66  from unitree_rl_lab.assets.robots.custom_humanoid import (
  67      CUSTOM_HUMANOID_30DOF_JOINT_NAMES,
  68  )
  69  
  70  
  71  @configclass
  72  class ReplaySceneCfg(InteractiveSceneCfg):
  73      robot: ArticulationCfg = ROBOT_CFG.replace(prim_path="{ENV_REGEX_NS}/Robot")
  74  
  75  
  76  def _rpy_matrix(rpy: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
  77      roll, pitch, yaw = rpy
  78      cr, sr = math.cos(roll), math.sin(roll)
  79      cp, sp = math.cos(pitch), math.sin(pitch)
  80      cy, sy = math.cos(yaw), math.sin(yaw)
  81      rx = np.array([[1, 0, 0], [0, cr, -sr], [0, sr, cr]], dtype=np.float64)
  82      ry = np.array([[cp, 0, sp], [0, 1, 0], [-sp, 0, cp]], dtype=np.float64)
  83      rz = np.array([[cy, -sy, 0], [sy, cy, 0], [0, 0, 1]], dtype=np.float64)
  84      return rz @ ry @ rx
  85  
  86  
  87  def load_link_collision_corners(
  88      urdf_path: Path, link_names: list[str]
  89  ) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
  90      root = ET.parse(urdf_path).getroot()
  91      links = {link.attrib["name"]: link for link in root.findall("link")}
  92      result: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
  93      signs = np.asarray(list(itertools.product((-1.0, 1.0), repeat=3)))
  94      for name in link_names:
  95          corners = []
  96          for collision in links[name].findall("collision"):
  97              box = collision.find("geometry/box")
  98              if box is None:
  99                  raise ValueError(f"{urdf_path}: {name} collision must be a box")
 100              size = np.fromstring(box.attrib["size"], sep=" ", dtype=np.float64)
 101              origin = collision.find("origin")
 102              xyz = np.zeros(3) if origin is None else np.fromstring(
 103                  origin.attrib.get("xyz", "0 0 0"), sep=" ", dtype=np.float64
 104              )
 105              rpy = np.zeros(3) if origin is None else np.fromstring(
 106                  origin.attrib.get("rpy", "0 0 0"), sep=" ", dtype=np.float64
 107              )
 108              corners.append(xyz + (signs * (0.5 * size)) @ _rpy_matrix(rpy).T)
 109          if not corners:
 110              raise ValueError(f"{urdf_path}: {name} has no collision boxes")
 111          result[name] = np.concatenate(corners, axis=0)
 112      return result
 113  
 114  
 115  def _quat_wxyz_matrix(quat: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
 116      quat = quat / np.linalg.norm(quat, axis=-1, keepdims=True).clip(min=1.0e-8)
 117      w, x, y, z = np.moveaxis(quat, -1, 0)
 118      return np.stack(
 119          [
 120              1 - 2 * (y * y + z * z), 2 * (x * y - z * w), 2 * (x * z + y * w),
 121              2 * (x * y + z * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + z * z), 2 * (y * z - x * w),
 122              2 * (x * z - y * w), 2 * (y * z + x * w), 1 - 2 * (x * x + y * y),
 123          ],
 124          axis=-1,
 125      ).reshape(quat.shape[:-1] + (3, 3))
 126  
 127  
 128  def compute_sole_heights(
 129      body_pos: np.ndarray,
 130      body_quat: np.ndarray,
 131      body_names: list[str],
 132      collision_corners: dict[str, np.ndarray],
 133  ) -> np.ndarray:
 134      heights = []
 135      for name, corners in collision_corners.items():
 136          body_index = body_names.index(name)
 137          rotation = _quat_wxyz_matrix(body_quat[:, body_index])
 138          corners_w = body_pos[:, body_index, None, :] + np.einsum(
 139              "tij,cj->tci", rotation, corners
 140          )
 141          heights.append(np.min(corners_w[..., 2], axis=1))
 142      return np.stack(heights, axis=1)
 143  
 144  
 145  def so3_derivative(rotations_wxyz: torch.Tensor, dt: float) -> torch.Tensor:
 146      if rotations_wxyz.shape[0] < 3:
 147          return torch.zeros((rotations_wxyz.shape[0], 3), device=rotations_wxyz.device)
 148      previous, following = rotations_wxyz[:-2], rotations_wxyz[2:]
 149      relative = quat_mul(following, quat_conjugate(previous))
 150      omega = axis_angle_from_quat(relative) / (2.0 * dt)
 151      return torch.cat([omega[:1], omega, omega[-1:]], dim=0)
 152  
 153  
 154  def load_retarget(path: Path, device: torch.device):
 155      with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as data:
 156          fps = float(np.asarray(data["fps"]).reshape(-1)[0])
 157          stored_contract = str(np.asarray(data["joint_coordinate_contract"]).reshape(-1)[0]) if "joint_coordinate_contract" in data else None
 158          stored_names = [str(name) for name in np.asarray(data["joint_names"]).tolist()]
 159          joint_pos = np.asarray(data["joint_pos"], dtype=np.float32)
 160          root_pos = np.asarray(data["root_pos"], dtype=np.float32)
 161          root_quat_xyzw = np.asarray(data["root_quat_xyzw"], dtype=np.float32)
 162  
 163      from joint_coordinates import convert_motion
 164  
 165      stored_names, joint_pos, _ = convert_motion(
 166          stored_names, joint_pos, np.zeros_like(joint_pos), CUSTOM_HUMANOID_30DOF_JOINT_NAMES, stored_contract
 167      )
 168      lookup = {name: index for index, name in enumerate(stored_names)}
 169      missing = [name for name in CUSTOM_HUMANOID_30DOF_JOINT_NAMES if name not in lookup]
 170      if missing:
 171          raise ValueError(f"{path}: missing joints {missing}")
 172      indexes = [lookup[name] for name in CUSTOM_HUMANOID_30DOF_JOINT_NAMES]
 173      joint_pos = joint_pos[:, indexes]
 174  
 175      length = joint_pos.shape[0]
 176      if length < 2 or root_pos.shape != (length, 3) or root_quat_xyzw.shape != (length, 4):
 177          raise ValueError(f"{path}: inconsistent retarget shapes")
 178      if not all(np.isfinite(value).all() for value in (joint_pos, root_pos, root_quat_xyzw)):
 179          raise ValueError(f"{path}: contains NaN or Inf")
 180  
 181      root_quat_xyzw /= np.linalg.norm(root_quat_xyzw, axis=-1, keepdims=True).clip(min=1.0e-8)
 182      for frame in range(1, length):
 183          if np.dot(root_quat_xyzw[frame - 1], root_quat_xyzw[frame]) < 0.0:
 184              root_quat_xyzw[frame] *= -1.0
 185  
 186      dt = 1.0 / fps
 187      joint_vel = np.gradient(joint_pos, dt, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
 188      root_lin_vel = np.gradient(root_pos, dt, axis=0).astype(np.float32)
 189  
 190      root_quat_wxyz = root_quat_xyzw[:, [3, 0, 1, 2]]
 191      root_quat_t = torch.tensor(root_quat_wxyz, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
 192      root_ang_vel = so3_derivative(root_quat_t, dt)
 193  
 194      return (
 195          fps,
 196          torch.tensor(joint_pos, dtype=torch.float32, device=device),
 197          torch.tensor(joint_vel, dtype=torch.float32, device=device),
 198          torch.tensor(root_pos, dtype=torch.float32, device=device),
 199          root_quat_t,
 200          torch.tensor(root_lin_vel, dtype=torch.float32, device=device),
 201          root_ang_vel,
 202      )
 203  
 204  
 205  def convert_clip(
 206      sim: SimulationContext,
 207      scene: InteractiveScene,
 208      input_path: Path,
 209      output_path: Path,
 210      motion_id: str,
 211  ) -> dict[str, object]:
 212      robot = scene["robot"]
 213      joint_indexes, resolved_names = robot.find_joints(
 214          CUSTOM_HUMANOID_30DOF_JOINT_NAMES, preserve_order=True
 215      )
 216      if resolved_names != CUSTOM_HUMANOID_30DOF_JOINT_NAMES:
 217          raise ValueError(f"Simulator joint order mismatch: {resolved_names}")
 218      body_names = list(robot.body_names)
 219  
 220      (
 221          fps,
 222          joint_pos,
 223          joint_vel,
 224          root_pos,
 225          root_quat,
 226          root_lin_vel,
 227          root_ang_vel,
 228      ) = load_retarget(input_path, sim.device)
 229  
 230      log: dict[str, list[np.ndarray]] = {
 231          "joint_pos": [],
 232          "joint_vel": [],
 233          "root_state_w": [],
 234          "body_pos_w": [],
 235          "body_quat_w": [],
 236          "body_lin_vel_w": [],
 237          "body_ang_vel_w": [],
 238      }
 239      batch = scene.num_envs
 240      for frame in range(0, joint_pos.shape[0], batch):
 241          count = min(batch, joint_pos.shape[0] - frame)
 242          ix = torch.arange(frame, frame + batch, device=sim.device).clamp(max=joint_pos.shape[0]-1)
 243          root_state = robot.data.default_root_state.clone()
 244          root_state[:, :3] = root_pos[ix] + scene.env_origins
 245          root_state[:, 3:7] = root_quat[ix]
 246          root_state[:, 7:10] = root_lin_vel[ix]
 247          root_state[:, 10:13] = root_ang_vel[ix]
 248          robot.write_root_state_to_sim(root_state)
 249          robot.write_joint_state_to_sim(joint_pos[ix], joint_vel[ix], joint_ids=joint_indexes)
 250          sim.render()
 251          scene.update(sim.get_physics_dt())
 252          actual_pos = robot.data.joint_pos[:, joint_indexes]
 253          actual_vel = robot.data.joint_vel[:, joint_indexes]
 254          actual_root = robot.data.root_state_w
 255          quat_error = torch.minimum(torch.linalg.vector_norm(actual_root[:,3:7]-root_state[:,3:7],dim=1),
 256                                     torch.linalg.vector_norm(actual_root[:,3:7]+root_state[:,3:7],dim=1)).max()
 257          error = max(float((actual_pos-joint_pos[ix]).abs().max()),float((actual_vel-joint_vel[ix]).abs().max()),
 258                      float((actual_root[:,:3]-root_state[:,:3]).abs().max()),float(quat_error),
 259                      float((actual_root[:,7:]-root_state[:,7:]).abs().max()))
 260          if error > args_cli.state_tolerance:
 261              raise ValueError(f'{motion_id}: simulator readback error {error} at frame {frame}')
 262          log['joint_pos'].extend(actual_pos[:count].cpu().numpy().copy())
 263          log['joint_vel'].extend(actual_vel[:count].cpu().numpy().copy())
 264          normalized_root = actual_root[:count].clone()
 265          normalized_root[:,:3] -= scene.env_origins[:count]
 266          log['root_state_w'].extend(normalized_root.cpu().numpy().copy())
 267          for field in ('body_pos_w','body_quat_w','body_lin_vel_w','body_ang_vel_w'):
 268              value=getattr(robot.data,field)[:count].clone()
 269              if field=='body_pos_w': value-=scene.env_origins[:count,None,:]
 270              log[field].extend(value.cpu().numpy().copy())
 271  
 272      arrays = {name: np.stack(values).astype(np.float32) for name, values in log.items()}
 273      root_state_np = arrays.pop("root_state_w")
 274      joint_pos_np = arrays.pop("joint_pos")
 275      joint_vel_np = arrays.pop("joint_vel")
 276      root_pos_np = root_state_np[:, :3].copy()
 277      root_quat_np = root_state_np[:, 3:7]
 278  
 279      collision_corners = load_link_collision_corners(
 280          args_cli.training_urdf, ["left_foot", "right_foot"]
 281      )
 282      sole_z = compute_sole_heights(
 283          arrays["body_pos_w"], arrays["body_quat_w"], body_names, collision_corners
 284      )
 285      shift_z = args_cli.ground_clearance - float(np.min(sole_z))
 286      root_pos_np[:, 2] += shift_z
 287      arrays["body_pos_w"][:, :, 2] += shift_z
 288      sole_z += shift_z
 289  
 290      base_index = body_names.index("base_link")
 291      base_pos_error = float(
 292          np.max(np.abs(arrays["body_pos_w"][:, base_index] - root_pos_np))
 293      )
 294      base_quat_error = float(
 295          np.max(
 296              np.minimum(
 297                  np.linalg.norm(arrays["body_quat_w"][:, base_index] - root_quat_np, axis=1),
 298                  np.linalg.norm(arrays["body_quat_w"][:, base_index] + root_quat_np, axis=1),
 299              )
 300          )
 301      )
 302      if max(base_pos_error, base_quat_error) > args_cli.state_tolerance:
 303          raise ValueError(
 304              f"{motion_id}: base/root FK mismatch pos={base_pos_error:.6g}, "
 305              f"quat={base_quat_error:.6g}"
 306          )
 307  
 308      dt = 1.0 / fps
 309      body_lin_fd = np.gradient(arrays["body_pos_w"], dt, axis=0)
 310      lin_velocity_error_p95 = float(
 311          np.quantile(np.linalg.norm(body_lin_fd - arrays["body_lin_vel_w"], axis=-1), 0.95)
 312      )
 313      body_quat_contiguous = arrays["body_quat_w"].copy()
 314      for frame in range(1, len(body_quat_contiguous)):
 315          flip = np.sum(body_quat_contiguous[frame - 1] * body_quat_contiguous[frame], axis=-1) < 0
 316          body_quat_contiguous[frame, flip] *= -1
 317      quat_tensor = torch.tensor(body_quat_contiguous, device=sim.device)
 318      body_ang_fd = torch.stack(
 319          [so3_derivative(quat_tensor[:, index], dt) for index in range(len(body_names))],
 320          dim=1,
 321      ).cpu().numpy()
 322      ang_velocity_error_p95 = float(
 323          np.quantile(np.linalg.norm(body_ang_fd - arrays["body_ang_vel_w"], axis=-1), 0.95)
 324      )
 325      fk_quality_pass = (
 326          lin_velocity_error_p95 <= args_cli.body_linear_velocity_p95_tolerance
 327          and ang_velocity_error_p95 <= args_cli.body_angular_velocity_p95_tolerance
 328      )
 329  
 330      output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 331      np.savez_compressed(
 332          output_path,
 333          joint_coordinate_contract=np.asarray([CONTRACT_VERSION]),
 334          schema_version=np.asarray([1], dtype=np.int32),
 335          fps=np.asarray([fps], dtype=np.float32),
 336          motion_id=np.asarray([motion_id]),
 337          joint_names=np.asarray(CUSTOM_HUMANOID_30DOF_JOINT_NAMES),
 338          body_names=np.asarray(body_names),
 339          joint_pos=joint_pos_np,
 340          joint_vel=joint_vel_np,
 341          root_pos=root_pos_np,
 342          root_quat_wxyz=root_quat_np,
 343          **arrays,
 344      )
 345      return {
 346          "id": motion_id,
 347          "file": str(output_path.resolve()),
 348          "weight": 1.0,
 349          "frames": int(joint_pos.shape[0]),
 350          "ground_shift_z": shift_z,
 351          "sole_min_z": float(np.min(sole_z)),
 352          "sole_p05_z": float(np.quantile(sole_z, 0.05)),
 353          "base_pos_error_max": base_pos_error,
 354          "base_quat_error_max": base_quat_error,
 355          "body_linear_velocity_error_p95": lin_velocity_error_p95,
 356          "body_angular_velocity_error_p95": ang_velocity_error_p95,
 357          "fk_quality_pass": fk_quality_pass,
 358          "source_file": str(input_path.resolve()),
 359      }
 360  
 361  
 362  def main() -> None:
 363      payload = json.loads(args_cli.input_manifest.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 364      motions = payload.get("motions", [])
 365      if not motions:
 366          raise ValueError(f"No motions in {args_cli.input_manifest}")
 367      args_cli.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
 368      output_manifest = args_cli.output_manifest or args_cli.output_dir / "manifest.json"
 369  
 370      sim_cfg = sim_utils.SimulationCfg(device=args_cli.device)
 371      sim_cfg.dt = 1.0 / float(payload.get("target_fps", 50))
 372      sim = SimulationContext(sim_cfg)
 373      scene = InteractiveScene(ReplaySceneCfg(num_envs=args_cli.fk_batch_size, env_spacing=1.0))
 374      sim.reset()
 375  
 376      converted = []
 377      failures = []
 378      for index, item in enumerate(motions, start=1):
 379          motion_id = str(item["id"])
 380          input_path = Path(item["file"]).expanduser()
 381          if not input_path.is_absolute():
 382              input_path = args_cli.input_manifest.parent / input_path
 383          input_path = input_path.resolve()
 384          source_quality_pass = item.get("quality_pass") is True
 385          output_path = args_cli.output_dir / f"{motion_id}.npz"
 386          try:
 387              if not source_quality_pass and not args_cli.allow_quality_failures:
 388                  raise ValueError(
 389                      f"{motion_id}: retarget record is not marked quality_pass=true"
 390                  )
 391              print(f"[{index}/{len(motions)}] FK {motion_id}", flush=True)
 392              record = convert_clip(sim, scene, input_path, output_path, motion_id)
 393              record["weight"] = float(item.get("weight", 1.0))
 394              record["retarget_quality"] = item.get("quality", {})
 395              record["acceptance_policy"] = item.get("acceptance_policy", "strict_original")
 396              record["strict_retarget_quality_pass"] = item.get("strict_quality_pass", source_quality_pass)
 397              record["quality_pass"] = bool(source_quality_pass and record["fk_quality_pass"])
 398              if not record["quality_pass"] and not args_cli.allow_quality_failures:
 399                  output_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
 400                  raise ValueError(f"{motion_id}: FK or retarget quality gate failed")
 401              converted.append(record)
 402          except Exception as exc:
 403              output_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
 404              failures.append({"id": motion_id, "file": str(input_path), "error": str(exc)})
 405              traceback.print_exc()
 406              if not args_cli.continue_on_error:
 407                  raise
 408  
 409      result = {
 410          "joint_coordinate_contract": CONTRACT_VERSION,
 411          "backend": payload.get("backend"),
 412          "schema_version": 1,
 413          "target_fps": float(payload.get("target_fps", 50)),
 414          "robot": "urdf0711_training_30dof",
 415          "joint_names": CUSTOM_HUMANOID_30DOF_JOINT_NAMES,
 416          "motions": converted,
 417          "failures": failures,
 418      }
 419      output_manifest.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
 420      print(
 421          f"Wrote {len(converted)} clips and {len(failures)} failures to {output_manifest}"
 422      )
 423      if not converted:
 424          raise RuntimeError("No clips passed FK conversion")
 425  
 426  
 427  if __name__ == "__main__":
 428      try:
 429          main()
 430      finally:
 431          # This is a non-interactive batch converter with no Replicator work.
 432          # Full Kit cleanup can spin indefinitely after all validated outputs
 433          # are closed; the documented immediate shutdown releases the GPU.
 434          simulation_app.close(
 435              wait_for_replicator=False, skip_cleanup=True
 436          )

   4  source /opt/conda/etc/profile.d/conda.sh

  10  mkdir -p "$OUT"
  11  trap 'echo failed > "$OUT/status"' ERR
  12  export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=$GPU OMP_NUM_THREADS=1
  13  export PYTHONPATH="$PWD/source/unitree_rl_lab:${PYTHONPATH:-}" LD_LIBRARY_PATH="$DATA/isaacsim:${LD_LIBRARY_PATH:-}"
  14  echo waiting_for_data > "$OUT/status"
  15  while :; do
  16   A=$(cat "$STATE/prepare_0.status" 2>/dev/null || true); B=$(cat "$STATE/prepare_1.status" 2>/dev/null || true)
  17   if [[ "$A" == failed || "$B" == failed ]]; then echo preparation_failed > "$OUT/status"; exit 1; fi
  18   if [[ "$A" == completed && "$B" == completed ]]; then break; fi
  19   sleep 30
  20  done
  21  if [[ "$GPU" == 0 ]]; then
  22   python scripts/practice9/build_tw75_manifests.py --selection "$DATA/datasets/practice9/tw75_dataset_v1/selection.json" --fk-manifests "$STATE"/batches/*/manifest.json --output "$STATE/splits"

  24  import json,sys
  25  from pathlib import Path
  26  r=Path(sys.argv[1]);d=json.load(open(r/'splits/val.json'));chosen=[]
  27  for category in ['walking','standing_upper']:
  28   chosen.extend([x for x in d['motions'] if x['category']==category][:3])
  29  if len(chosen)!=6:raise ValueError('Insufficient held-out validation coverage')
  30  (r/'eval').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
  31  for x in chosen:
  32   p=dict(d);p['motions']=[x];(r/'eval'/f'{x["id"]}.json').write_text(json.dumps(p))
  33  (r/'eval_ids.txt').write_text(''.join(x['id']+'\n' for x in chosen))

  35  PY
  36  fi
  37  while [[ ! -f "$STATE/splits/ready" ]]; do

  39   sleep 15
  40  done
  41  export PRACTICE9_CUSTOM_MOTION_MANIFEST="$STATE/splits/train.json"
  42  FRAMES=$(python -c 'import json,sys;print(sum(x["frames"] for x in json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["motions"]))' "$PRACTICE9_CUSTOM_MOTION_MANIFEST")
  43  export P9_TARGET_SMOOTHING_TAU=0 P9_TARGET_LIMIT_VELOCITY=1 P9_TARGET_VELOCITY_SCALE=0.5 P9_PD_STIFFNESS_MULT=1 P9_PD_DAMPING_MULT=1
  44  CHECKPOINT=$(cat "$DATA/datasets/practice9/s2_control_fix/long_velocity/final_checkpoint")
  45  git rev-parse HEAD > "$OUT/source_commit"

  47  echo training > "$OUT/status"
  48  python scripts/rsl_rl/train.py --headless --task Unitree-Custom-Humanoid-30dof-Mimic-HumanML3D --num_envs 1024 --max_iterations 20000 --seed "$SEED" --run_name "tw75_full_s$SEED" --logger tensorboard --resume --load_run "$(basename "$(dirname "$CHECKPOINT")")" --checkpoint "$(basename "$CHECKPOINT")" env.rewards.action_rate_l2.weight=-0.2 env.rewards.joint_acc.weight=-2e-6 env.rewards.motion_joint_vel_cost.weight=-0.2 env.commands.motion.adaptive_uniform_ratio=1.0 "env.commands.motion.max_motion_frames=$FRAMES" agent.save_interval=500 > "$OUT/train.log" 2>&1
  49  RUN=$(find logs/rsl_rl/unitree_custom_humanoid_30dof_mimic_humanml3d -maxdepth 1 -type d -name "*_tw75_full_s$SEED" | sort | tail -1)
  50  CHECKPOINT=$(find "$RUN" -maxdepth 1 -name 'model_*.pt' | sort -V | tail -1)
  51  realpath "$CHECKPOINT" > "$OUT/final_checkpoint"
  52  unset P9_TARGET_SMOOTHING_TAU P9_TARGET_LIMIT_VELOCITY P9_TARGET_VELOCITY_SCALE P9_PD_STIFFNESS_MULT P9_PD_DAMPING_MULT
  53  echo evaluating > "$OUT/status"
  54  mkdir -p "$OUT/videos"
  55  while read -r ID; do
  56   export PRACTICE9_CUSTOM_MOTION_MANIFEST="$STATE/eval/$ID.json"
  57   LENGTH=$(python -c 'import json,sys;print(max(1000,json.load(open(sys.argv[1]))["motions"][0]["frames"]+1))' "$PRACTICE9_CUSTOM_MOTION_MANIFEST")
  58   for EVAL_SEED in 42 123 2026; do
  59    python scripts/rsl_rl/play.py --headless --task Unitree-Custom-Humanoid-30dof-Mimic-HumanML3D --num_envs 1 --checkpoint "$CHECKPOINT" --evaluation_motion_id 0 --evaluation_steps "$LENGTH" --seed "$EVAL_SEED" --disable_observation_noise --evaluation_output "$OUT/$ID.seed$EVAL_SEED.json" --telemetry_output "$OUT/$ID.seed$EVAL_SEED.npz" > "$OUT/$ID.seed$EVAL_SEED.log" 2>&1
  60   done
  61   python scripts/rsl_rl/play.py --headless --task Unitree-Custom-Humanoid-30dof-Mimic-HumanML3D --num_envs 1 --checkpoint "$CHECKPOINT" --evaluation_motion_id 0 --video --video_length 1000 --seed 42 --disable_observation_noise --evaluation_output "$OUT/$ID.video.json" --viewer_follow_asset robot --viewer_follow_tau 0.5 --viewer_eye 0 -1.2 0.15 --viewer_lookat 0 0 -0.03 > "$OUT/$ID.video.log" 2>&1
  62   VIDEO=$(find "$RUN/videos/play" -name '*.mp4' | head -1)
  63   test -s "$VIDEO"; mv "$VIDEO" "$OUT/videos/$ID.mp4"
  64  done < "$STATE/eval_ids.txt"
  65  echo completed > "$OUT/status"

  33  class MotionLoader:
  34      def __init__(self, motion_file: str, body_indexes: Sequence[int], device: str = "cpu"):
  35          assert os.path.isfile(motion_file), f"Invalid file path: {motion_file}"
  36          data = np.load(motion_file)
  37          self.fps = data["fps"]
  38          self.joint_pos = torch.tensor(data["joint_pos"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
  39          self.joint_vel = torch.tensor(data["joint_vel"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
  40          self._body_pos_w = torch.tensor(data["body_pos_w"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
  41          self._body_quat_w = torch.tensor(data["body_quat_w"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
  42          self._body_lin_vel_w = torch.tensor(data["body_lin_vel_w"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
  43          self._body_ang_vel_w = torch.tensor(data["body_ang_vel_w"], dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
  44          self._body_indexes = body_indexes
  45          self.time_step_total = self.joint_pos.shape[0]
  46  
  47      @property
  48      def body_pos_w(self) -> torch.Tensor:
  49          return self._body_pos_w[:, self._body_indexes]
  50  
  51      @property
  52      def body_quat_w(self) -> torch.Tensor:
  53          return self._body_quat_w[:, self._body_indexes]
  54  
  55      @property
  56      def body_lin_vel_w(self) -> torch.Tensor:
  57          return self._body_lin_vel_w[:, self._body_indexes]
  58  
  59      @property
  60      def body_ang_vel_w(self) -> torch.Tensor:
  61          return self._body_ang_vel_w[:, self._body_indexes]
