  64  class MotionCommand(CommandTerm):
  65      cfg: MotionCommandCfg
  66  
  67      def __init__(self, cfg: MotionCommandCfg, env: ManagerBasedRLEnv):
  68          super().__init__(cfg, env)
  69  
  70          self.robot: Articulation = env.scene[cfg.asset_name]
  71          self.robot_anchor_body_index = self.robot.body_names.index(self.cfg.anchor_body_name)
  72          self.motion_anchor_body_index = self.cfg.body_names.index(self.cfg.anchor_body_name)
  73          root_body_name = self.cfg.root_body_name or self.cfg.body_names[0]
  74          self.motion_root_body_index = self.cfg.body_names.index(root_body_name)
  75          self.body_indexes = torch.tensor(
  76              self.robot.find_bodies(self.cfg.body_names, preserve_order=True)[0], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device
  77          )
  78          joint_expressions = self.cfg.joint_names if self.cfg.joint_names is not None else [".*"]
  79          joint_indexes, resolved_joint_names = self.robot.find_joints(joint_expressions, preserve_order=True)
  80          self.joint_indexes = torch.tensor(joint_indexes, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
  81          if self.cfg.joint_names is not None and resolved_joint_names != self.cfg.joint_names:
  82              raise ValueError(
  83                  "Configured motion joint order did not resolve exactly. "
  84                  f"expected={self.cfg.joint_names}, resolved={resolved_joint_names}"
  85              )
  86  
  87          control_fps = 1.0 / (env.cfg.decimation * env.cfg.sim.dt)
  88          self.motion = MotionLibrary(
  89              self.cfg.motion_file,
  90              device=self.device,
  91              tracked_body_names=self.cfg.body_names,
  92              joint_names=self.cfg.joint_names,
  93              legacy_body_indexes=self.body_indexes,
  94              expected_fps=control_fps,
  95              max_frames=self.cfg.max_motion_frames,
  96              require_quality_pass=self.cfg.require_quality_pass,
  97          )
  98          if self.motion.joint_pos.shape[1] != len(self.joint_indexes):
  99              raise ValueError(
 100                  f"Motion has {self.motion.joint_pos.shape[1]} joints, but robot selection "
 101                  f"resolved {len(self.joint_indexes)} joints."
 102              )
 103          self.motion_ids = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
 104          self.time_steps = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
 105          self._evaluation_motion_ids = torch.full((self.num_envs,), -1, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
 106          self.body_pos_relative_w = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, len(cfg.body_names), 3, device=self.device)
 107          self.body_quat_relative_w = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, len(cfg.body_names), 4, device=self.device)
 108          self.body_quat_relative_w[:, :, 0] = 1.0
 109  
 110          self.bin_count = self.motion.build_bins(self.cfg.adaptive_bin_size_s)
 111          self.bin_failed_count = torch.zeros(self.bin_count, dtype=torch.float, device=self.device)
 112          self.bin_visit_count = torch.zeros(self.bin_count, dtype=torch.float, device=self.device)
 113          self._current_bin_failed = torch.zeros(self.bin_count, dtype=torch.float, device=self.device)
 114          self._current_bin_visited = torch.zeros(self.bin_count, dtype=torch.float, device=self.device)
 115          self._sample_active = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, dtype=torch.bool, device=self.device)
 116          self.max_sampling_probability = self.cfg.adaptive_max_probability
 117          if self.max_sampling_probability is not None:
 118              self.max_sampling_probability = max(self.max_sampling_probability, 1.0 / self.bin_count)
 119          if self.motion.num_motions > 1 and self.cfg.adaptive_kernel_size != 1:
 120              raise ValueError("Multi-motion adaptive sampling currently requires adaptive_kernel_size=1")
 121          self.kernel = torch.tensor(
 122              [self.cfg.adaptive_lambda**i for i in range(self.cfg.adaptive_kernel_size)], device=self.device
 123          )
 124          self.kernel = self.kernel / self.kernel.sum()
 125          self.metrics['sampling_effective_bins'] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 126          self.metrics['failure_rate_mean'] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 127          self.metrics['failure_rate_max'] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 128  
 129          self.metrics["error_anchor_pos"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 130          self.metrics["error_anchor_rot"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 131          self.metrics["error_anchor_lin_vel"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 132          self.metrics["error_anchor_ang_vel"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 133          self.metrics["error_body_pos"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 134          self.metrics["error_body_rot"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 135          self.metrics["error_joint_pos"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 136          self.metrics["error_joint_vel"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 137          self.metrics["sampling_entropy"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 138          self.metrics["sampling_top1_prob"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 139          self.metrics["sampling_top1_bin"] = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, device=self.device)
 140  
 141      @property
 142      def frame_indices(self) -> torch.Tensor:
 143          return self.motion.frame_indices(self.motion_ids, self.time_steps)
 144  
 145      def set_evaluation_motion_ids(self, motion_ids: Sequence[int] | torch.Tensor) -> None:
 146          """Pin every environment to one motion and restart it from frame zero on reset.
 147  
 148          This is intentionally an explicit runtime override instead of a training
 149          configuration option. It keeps the adaptive sampler unchanged during
 150          training while allowing deterministic per-clip evaluation.
 151          """
 152          values = torch.as_tensor(motion_ids, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
 153          if values.ndim != 1 or values.shape[0] != self.num_envs:
 154              raise ValueError(
 155                  f"Expected one evaluation motion id per environment ({self.num_envs}), got {tuple(values.shape)}"
 156              )
 157          if torch.any(values < 0) or torch.any(values >= self.motion.num_motions):
 158              minimum = int(values.min().item()) if values.numel() else -1
 159              maximum = int(values.max().item()) if values.numel() else -1
 160              raise ValueError(
 161                  f"Evaluation motion ids must be in [0, {self.motion.num_motions}), got [{minimum}, {maximum}]"
 162              )
 163          self._evaluation_motion_ids.copy_(values)
 164          self._sample_active.zero_()
 165  
 166      def clear_evaluation_motion_ids(self) -> None:
 167          """Return all environments to adaptive motion sampling on their next reset."""
 168          self._evaluation_motion_ids.fill_(-1)

 172          return torch.cat([self.joint_pos, self.joint_vel], dim=1)

 254      def _update_metrics(self):
 255          self.metrics["error_anchor_pos"] = torch.norm(self.anchor_pos_w - self.robot_anchor_pos_w, dim=-1)
 256          self.metrics["error_anchor_rot"] = quat_error_magnitude(self.anchor_quat_w, self.robot_anchor_quat_w)
 257          self.metrics["error_anchor_lin_vel"] = torch.norm(self.anchor_lin_vel_w - self.robot_anchor_lin_vel_w, dim=-1)
 258          self.metrics["error_anchor_ang_vel"] = torch.norm(self.anchor_ang_vel_w - self.robot_anchor_ang_vel_w, dim=-1)
 259  
 260          self.metrics["error_body_pos"] = torch.norm(self.body_pos_relative_w - self.robot_body_pos_w, dim=-1).mean(
 261              dim=-1
 262          )
 263          self.metrics["error_body_rot"] = quat_error_magnitude(self.body_quat_relative_w, self.robot_body_quat_w).mean(
 264              dim=-1
 265          )
 266  
 267          self.metrics["error_body_lin_vel"] = torch.norm(self.body_lin_vel_w - self.robot_body_lin_vel_w, dim=-1).mean(
 268              dim=-1
 269          )
 270          self.metrics["error_body_ang_vel"] = torch.norm(self.body_ang_vel_w - self.robot_body_ang_vel_w, dim=-1).mean(
 271              dim=-1
 272          )
 273  
 274          self.metrics["error_joint_pos"] = torch.norm(self.joint_pos - self.robot_joint_pos, dim=-1)
 275          self.metrics["error_joint_vel"] = torch.norm(self.joint_vel - self.robot_joint_vel, dim=-1)
 276  
 277      def _adaptive_sampling(self, env_ids: Sequence[int]):
 278          episode_failed = self._env.termination_manager.terminated[env_ids]
 279          previous_active = self._sample_active[env_ids]
 280          current_bin_index = self.motion.current_bin_indexes(self.motion_ids, self.time_steps)
 281          if torch.any(previous_active):
 282              completed_bins = current_bin_index[env_ids][previous_active]
 283              self._current_bin_visited[:] = torch.bincount(completed_bins, minlength=self.bin_count)
 284              fail_bins = current_bin_index[env_ids][previous_active & episode_failed]
 285              self._current_bin_failed[:] = torch.bincount(fail_bins, minlength=self.bin_count)
 286  
 287          # Sample
 288          failure_score = failure_rates(
 289              self.bin_failed_count,
 290              self.bin_visit_count,
 291              unvisited_score=self.cfg.adaptive_unvisited_score,
 292          )
 293          if self.cfg.adaptive_kernel_size > 1:
 294              failure_score = torch.nn.functional.pad(
 295                  failure_score.unsqueeze(0).unsqueeze(0),
 296                  (0, self.cfg.adaptive_kernel_size - 1),
 297                  mode="replicate",
 298              )
 299              failure_score = torch.nn.functional.conv1d(
 300                  failure_score, self.kernel.view(1, 1, -1)
 301              ).view(-1)
 302  
 303          sampling_probabilities = balanced_sampling_probabilities(
 304              failure_score,
 305              self.motion.bin_weights,
 306              uniform_ratio=self.cfg.adaptive_uniform_ratio,
 307              max_probability=self.max_sampling_probability,
 308          )
 309          sampled_bins = torch.multinomial(sampling_probabilities, len(env_ids), replacement=True)
 310          fractions = sample_uniform(0.0, 1.0, (len(env_ids),), device=self.device)
 311          sampled_motion_ids, sampled_time_steps = self.motion.sample_from_bins(sampled_bins, fractions)
 312          self.motion_ids[env_ids] = sampled_motion_ids
 313          self.time_steps[env_ids] = sampled_time_steps
 314          self._sample_active[env_ids] = True
 315  
 316          # Metrics
 317          H = -(sampling_probabilities * (sampling_probabilities + 1e-12).log()).sum()
 318          H_norm = H / math.log(self.bin_count) if self.bin_count > 1 else torch.zeros_like(H)
 319          pmax, imax = sampling_probabilities.max(dim=0)
 320          effective_bins = 1.0 / sampling_probabilities.square().sum()
 321          self.metrics['sampling_effective_bins'][:] = effective_bins / self.bin_count
 322          self.metrics['failure_rate_mean'][:] = failure_score.mean()
 323          self.metrics['failure_rate_max'][:] = failure_score.max()
 324          self.metrics["sampling_entropy"][:] = H_norm
 325          self.metrics["sampling_top1_prob"][:] = pmax
 326          self.metrics["sampling_top1_bin"][:] = imax.float() / self.bin_count
 327  
 328      def _resample_command(self, env_ids: Sequence[int]):
 329          if len(env_ids) == 0:
 330              return
 331          env_ids_tensor = torch.as_tensor(env_ids, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
 332          fixed_mask = self._evaluation_motion_ids[env_ids_tensor] >= 0
 333          sampled_env_ids = env_ids_tensor[~fixed_mask]
 334          fixed_env_ids = env_ids_tensor[fixed_mask]
 335  
 336          if sampled_env_ids.numel() > 0:
 337              self._adaptive_sampling(sampled_env_ids)
 338          if fixed_env_ids.numel() > 0:
 339              self.motion_ids[fixed_env_ids] = self._evaluation_motion_ids[fixed_env_ids]
 340              self.time_steps[fixed_env_ids] = 0
 341              self._sample_active[fixed_env_ids] = False
 342  
 343          root_pos = self.body_pos_w[:, self.motion_root_body_index].clone()
 344          root_ori = self.body_quat_w[:, self.motion_root_body_index].clone()
 345          root_lin_vel = self.body_lin_vel_w[:, self.motion_root_body_index].clone()
 346          root_ang_vel = self.body_ang_vel_w[:, self.motion_root_body_index].clone()
 347  
 348          range_list = [self.cfg.pose_range.get(key, (0.0, 0.0)) for key in ["x", "y", "z", "roll", "pitch", "yaw"]]
 349          ranges = torch.tensor(range_list, device=self.device)
 350          rand_samples = sample_uniform(ranges[:, 0], ranges[:, 1], (len(env_ids), 6), device=self.device)
 351          root_pos[env_ids] += rand_samples[:, 0:3]
 352          orientations_delta = quat_from_euler_xyz(rand_samples[:, 3], rand_samples[:, 4], rand_samples[:, 5])
 353          root_ori[env_ids] = quat_mul(orientations_delta, root_ori[env_ids])
 354          range_list = [self.cfg.velocity_range.get(key, (0.0, 0.0)) for key in ["x", "y", "z", "roll", "pitch", "yaw"]]
 355          ranges = torch.tensor(range_list, device=self.device)
 356          rand_samples = sample_uniform(ranges[:, 0], ranges[:, 1], (len(env_ids), 6), device=self.device)
 357          root_lin_vel[env_ids] += rand_samples[:, :3]
 358          root_ang_vel[env_ids] += rand_samples[:, 3:]
 359  
 360          joint_pos = self.joint_pos.clone()
 361          joint_vel = self.joint_vel.clone()
 362  
 363          joint_pos += sample_uniform(*self.cfg.joint_position_range, joint_pos.shape, joint_pos.device)
 364          soft_joint_pos_limits = self.robot.data.soft_joint_pos_limits[env_ids][:, self.joint_indexes]
 365          joint_pos[env_ids] = torch.clip(
 366              joint_pos[env_ids], soft_joint_pos_limits[:, :, 0], soft_joint_pos_limits[:, :, 1]
 367          )
 368          self.robot.write_joint_state_to_sim(
 369              joint_pos[env_ids],
 370              joint_vel[env_ids],
 371              joint_ids=self.joint_indexes,
 372              env_ids=env_ids,
 373          )
 374          self.robot.write_root_state_to_sim(
 375              torch.cat([root_pos[env_ids], root_ori[env_ids], root_lin_vel[env_ids], root_ang_vel[env_ids]], dim=-1),
 376              env_ids=env_ids,
 377          )
 378          # Public env.reset() does not call command.compute() before the first
 379          # transition. Seed these buffers with the newly selected reference.
 380          self.body_pos_relative_w[env_ids] = self.body_pos_w[env_ids]
 381          self.body_quat_relative_w[env_ids] = self.body_quat_w[env_ids]
 382  
 383      def _update_command(self):
 384          self.time_steps += 1
 385          clip_lengths = self.motion.clip_lengths[self.motion_ids]
 386          env_ids = torch.where(self.time_steps >= clip_lengths)[0]
 387          if self.cfg.resample_at_motion_end:
 388              self._resample_command(env_ids)
 389          else:
 390              self.time_steps = torch.minimum(self.time_steps, clip_lengths - 1)
 391  
 392          anchor_pos_w_repeat = self.anchor_pos_w[:, None, :].repeat(1, len(self.cfg.body_names), 1)
 393          anchor_quat_w_repeat = self.anchor_quat_w[:, None, :].repeat(1, len(self.cfg.body_names), 1)
 394          robot_anchor_pos_w_repeat = self.robot_anchor_pos_w[:, None, :].repeat(1, len(self.cfg.body_names), 1)
 395          robot_anchor_quat_w_repeat = self.robot_anchor_quat_w[:, None, :].repeat(1, len(self.cfg.body_names), 1)
 396  
 397          delta_pos_w = robot_anchor_pos_w_repeat
 398          delta_pos_w[..., 2] = anchor_pos_w_repeat[..., 2]
 399          delta_ori_w = yaw_quat(quat_mul(robot_anchor_quat_w_repeat, quat_inv(anchor_quat_w_repeat)))
 400  
 401          self.body_quat_relative_w = quat_mul(delta_ori_w, self.body_quat_w)
 402          self.body_pos_relative_w = delta_pos_w + quat_apply(delta_ori_w, self.body_pos_w - anchor_pos_w_repeat)
 403  
 404          self.bin_failed_count = (
 405              self.cfg.adaptive_alpha * self._current_bin_failed + (1 - self.cfg.adaptive_alpha) * self.bin_failed_count
 406          )
 407          self.bin_visit_count = (
 408              self.cfg.adaptive_alpha * self._current_bin_visited + (1 - self.cfg.adaptive_alpha) * self.bin_visit_count
 409          )
 410          self._current_bin_failed.zero_()
 411          self._current_bin_visited.zero_()
 412  
 413      def _set_debug_vis_impl(self, debug_vis: bool):
 414          if debug_vis:
 415              if not hasattr(self, "current_anchor_visualizer"):
 416                  self.current_anchor_visualizer = VisualizationMarkers(
 417                      self.cfg.anchor_visualizer_cfg.replace(prim_path="/Visuals/Command/current/anchor")
 418                  )
 419                  self.goal_anchor_visualizer = VisualizationMarkers(
 420                      self.cfg.anchor_visualizer_cfg.replace(prim_path="/Visuals/Command/goal/anchor")
 421                  )
 422  
 423                  self.current_body_visualizers = []
 424                  self.goal_body_visualizers = []
 425                  for name in self.cfg.body_names:
 426                      self.current_body_visualizers.append(
 427                          VisualizationMarkers(
 428                              self.cfg.body_visualizer_cfg.replace(prim_path="/Visuals/Command/current/" + name)
 429                          )
 430                      )
 431                      self.goal_body_visualizers.append(
 432                          VisualizationMarkers(
 433                              self.cfg.body_visualizer_cfg.replace(prim_path="/Visuals/Command/goal/" + name)
 434                          )
 435                      )
 436  
 437              self.current_anchor_visualizer.set_visibility(True)
 438              self.goal_anchor_visualizer.set_visibility(True)
 439              for i in range(len(self.cfg.body_names)):
 440                  self.current_body_visualizers[i].set_visibility(True)
 441                  self.goal_body_visualizers[i].set_visibility(True)
 442  
 443          else:
 444              if hasattr(self, "current_anchor_visualizer"):
 445                  self.current_anchor_visualizer.set_visibility(False)
 446                  self.goal_anchor_visualizer.set_visibility(False)
 447                  for i in range(len(self.cfg.body_names)):
 448                      self.current_body_visualizers[i].set_visibility(False)
 449                      self.goal_body_visualizers[i].set_visibility(False)
 450  
 451      def _debug_vis_callback(self, event):
 452          if not self.robot.is_initialized:
 453              return
 454  
 455          self.current_anchor_visualizer.visualize(self.robot_anchor_pos_w, self.robot_anchor_quat_w)
 456          self.goal_anchor_visualizer.visualize(self.anchor_pos_w, self.anchor_quat_w)
 457  
 458          for i in range(len(self.cfg.body_names)):
 459              self.current_body_visualizers[i].visualize(self.robot_body_pos_w[:, i], self.robot_body_quat_w[:, i])
 460              self.goal_body_visualizers[i].visualize(self.body_pos_relative_w[:, i], self.body_quat_relative_w[:, i])

  24  def _string_list(value: np.ndarray | None) -> list[str] | None:
  25      if value is None:
  26          return None
  27      array = np.asarray(value)
  28      if array.ndim != 1:
  29          raise ValueError(f"Name metadata must be one-dimensional, got {array.shape}")
  30      return [str(item) for item in array.tolist()]

  33  class MotionLibrary:
  34      """Eager motion library with explicit clip boundaries.
  35  
  36      This backend intentionally has a frame cap. It is suitable for the first
  37      filtered HumanML3D curriculum and keeps the command interface ready for a
  38      future sharded/cache backend without changing the RL task.
  39      """
  40  
  41      def __init__(
  42          self,
  43          source: str,
  44          *,
  45          device: str,
  46          tracked_body_names: Sequence[str],
  47          joint_names: Sequence[str] | None,
  48          legacy_body_indexes: Sequence[int] | torch.Tensor,
  49          expected_fps: float,
  50          max_frames: int,
  51          require_quality_pass: bool = False,
  52      ):
  53          self.source = Path(source).expanduser().resolve()
  54          self.device = device
  55          self.tracked_body_names = list(tracked_body_names)
  56          self.joint_names = None if joint_names is None else list(joint_names)
  57          if isinstance(legacy_body_indexes, torch.Tensor):
  58              legacy_body_indexes = legacy_body_indexes.detach().cpu().tolist()
  59          self.legacy_body_indexes = list(legacy_body_indexes)
  60          self.expected_fps = float(expected_fps)
  61          self.max_frames = int(max_frames)
  62          self.require_quality_pass = bool(require_quality_pass)
  63          if not np.isfinite(self.expected_fps) or self.expected_fps <= 0.0:
  64              raise ValueError(f"expected_fps must be finite and positive, got {expected_fps}")
  65          if self.max_frames <= 0:
  66              raise ValueError(f"max_frames must be positive, got {max_frames}")
  67  
  68          entries = self._read_entries()
  69          arrays: dict[str, list[torch.Tensor]] = {field: [] for field in MOTION_FIELDS}
  70          clip_ids: list[str] = []
  71          clip_lengths: list[int] = []
  72          clip_weights: list[float] = []
  73          clip_sampling_intervals: list[list[tuple[int, int, float]]] = []
  74          fps_value: float | None = None
  75          total_frames = 0
  76  
  77          for entry in entries:
  78              path = entry["path"]
  79              weight = float(entry["weight"])
  80              motion_id = str(entry["id"])
  81              loaded = self._load_npz(
  82                  path,
  83                  start_frame=entry.get('start_frame', 0),
  84                  end_frame=entry.get('end_frame'),
  85              )
  86              clip_fps = loaded.pop("fps")
  87              if fps_value is None:
  88                  fps_value = clip_fps
  89              elif abs(clip_fps - fps_value) > 1.0e-6:
  90                  raise ValueError(f"Mixed motion FPS: {fps_value} and {clip_fps} in {path}")
  91  
  92              length = int(loaded["joint_pos"].shape[0])
  93              total_frames += length
  94              if total_frames > self.max_frames:
  95                  raise MemoryError(
  96                      f"Motion manifest has {total_frames} frames, over eager limit "
  97                      f"{self.max_frames}. Filter the curriculum or use a sharded backend."
  98                  )
  99  
 100              for field in MOTION_FIELDS:
 101                  arrays[field].append(torch.from_numpy(loaded[field]))
 102              clip_ids.append(motion_id)
 103              clip_lengths.append(length)
 104              clip_weights.append(weight)
 105              clip_sampling_intervals.append(
 106                  self._validate_sampling_intervals(
 107                      entry.get('sampling_intervals'),
 108                      length=length,
 109                      motion_id=motion_id,
 110                  )
 111              )
 112  
 113          if fps_value is None:
 114              raise ValueError(f"No motions found in {self.source}")
 115          if abs(fps_value - self.expected_fps) > 1.0e-4:
 116              raise ValueError(
 117                  f"Motion FPS {fps_value:g} does not match environment control FPS "
 118                  f"{self.expected_fps:g}. Convert motions before training."
 119              )
 120  
 121          self.fps = fps_value
 122          self.clip_ids = clip_ids
 123          self.num_motions = len(clip_ids)
 124          self.clip_lengths = torch.tensor(clip_lengths, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
 125          self.clip_weights = torch.tensor(clip_weights, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
 126          self.clip_sampling_intervals = clip_sampling_intervals
 127          starts = np.cumsum([0, *clip_lengths[:-1]], dtype=np.int64)
 128          self.clip_starts = torch.tensor(starts, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
 129          self.time_step_total = total_frames
 130  
 131          for field in MOTION_FIELDS:
 132              value = torch.cat(arrays[field], dim=0).to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)
 133              setattr(self, field, value)
 134  
 135          self.bin_motion_ids: torch.Tensor | None = None
 136          self.bin_local_starts: torch.Tensor | None = None
 137          self.bin_widths: torch.Tensor | None = None
 138          self.bin_weights: torch.Tensor | None = None
 139          self.clip_bin_offsets: torch.Tensor | None = None
 140          self.frames_per_bin: int | None = None
 141  
 142      def _read_entries(self) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
 143          if self.source.suffix.lower() != ".json":
 144              if not self.source.is_file():
 145                  raise FileNotFoundError(self.source)
 146              return [{"id": self.source.stem, "path": self.source, "weight": 1.0}]
 147  
 148          payload = json.loads(self.source.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
 149          if payload.get("schema_version") != 1:
 150              raise ValueError(f"Unsupported motion manifest schema in {self.source}")
 151          motions = payload.get("motions")
 152          if not isinstance(motions, list) or not motions:
 153              raise ValueError(f"Manifest has no motions: {self.source}")
 154  
 155          entries: list[dict[str, object]] = []
 156          seen_ids: set[str] = set()
 157          for index, item in enumerate(motions):
 158              if not isinstance(item, dict) or "file" not in item:
 159                  raise ValueError(f"Invalid motion entry {index} in {self.source}")
 160              path = Path(str(item["file"])).expanduser()
 161              if not path.is_absolute():
 162                  path = (self.source.parent / path).resolve()
 163              motion_id = str(item.get("id", path.stem))
 164              if motion_id in seen_ids:
 165                  raise ValueError(f"Duplicate motion id: {motion_id}")
 166              seen_ids.add(motion_id)
 167              if self.require_quality_pass and item.get("quality_pass") is not True:
 168                  raise ValueError(
 169                      f"Motion {motion_id} is not marked quality_pass=true; refusing training data."
 170                  )
 171              weight = float(item.get("weight", 1.0))
 172              if not np.isfinite(weight) or weight <= 0.0:
 173                  raise ValueError(f"Invalid sampling weight for {motion_id}: {weight}")
 174              entries.append({"id": motion_id, "path": path, "weight": weight})
 175          for item, entry in zip(motions, entries):
 176              motion_id = str(entry['id'])
 177              start_frame = item.get('start_frame', 0)
 178              end_frame = item.get('end_frame')
 179              if isinstance(start_frame, bool) or not isinstance(start_frame, int) or start_frame < 0:
 180                  raise ValueError(f'Invalid start_frame for {motion_id}: {start_frame}')
 181              if end_frame is not None and (
 182                  isinstance(end_frame, bool) or not isinstance(end_frame, int) or end_frame <= start_frame
 183              ):
 184                  raise ValueError(f'Invalid end_frame for {motion_id}: {end_frame}')
 185              entry.update(
 186                  {
 187                      'start_frame': start_frame,
 188                      'end_frame': end_frame,
 189                      'sampling_intervals': item.get('sampling_intervals'),
 190                  }
 191              )
 192          return entries
 193  
 194      @staticmethod
 195      def _validate_sampling_intervals(
 196          value: object,
 197          *,
 198          length: int,
 199          motion_id: str,
 200      ) -> list[tuple[int, int, float]]:
 201          if value is None:
 202              return []
 203          if not isinstance(value, list):
 204              raise TypeError(f'sampling_intervals for {motion_id} must be a list')
 205          intervals: list[tuple[int, int, float]] = []
 206          previous_end = 0
 207          startable_length = length - 1
 208          for index, item in enumerate(value):
 209              if not isinstance(item, dict):
 210                  raise TypeError(f'Invalid sampling interval {index} for {motion_id}')
 211              start = item.get('start')
 212              end = item.get('end')
 213              multiplier = item.get('multiplier')
 214              if (
 215                  isinstance(start, bool)
 216                  or not isinstance(start, int)
 217                  or isinstance(end, bool)
 218                  or not isinstance(end, int)
 219                  or start < previous_end
 220                  or end <= start
 221                  or end > startable_length
 222              ):
 223                  raise ValueError(f'Invalid sampling interval {index} for {motion_id}: {item}')
 224              multiplier = float(multiplier)
 225              if not np.isfinite(multiplier) or multiplier <= 0.0:
 226                  raise ValueError(f'Invalid sampling multiplier for {motion_id}: {multiplier}')
 227              intervals.append((start, end, multiplier))
 228              previous_end = end
 229          return intervals
 230  
 231      def _load_npz(
 232          self,
 233          path: Path,
 234          *,
 235          start_frame: int = 0,
 236          end_frame: int | None = None,
 237      ) -> dict[str, np.ndarray | float]:
 238          if not path.is_file():
 239              raise FileNotFoundError(path)
 240          with np.load(path, allow_pickle=False) as data:
 241              missing = [field for field in MOTION_FIELDS if field not in data]
 242              if missing or "fps" not in data:
 243                  raise ValueError(f"{path} is missing fields: {missing + ([] if 'fps' in data else ['fps'])}")
 244  
 245              fps_array = np.asarray(data["fps"]).reshape(-1)
 246              if fps_array.size != 1:
 247                  raise ValueError(f"{path}: fps must be scalar, got {fps_array.shape}")
 248              fps = float(fps_array[0])
 249              if not np.isfinite(fps) or fps <= 0.0:
 250                  raise ValueError(f"{path}: fps must be finite and positive, got {fps}")
 251  
 252              joint_pos = np.asarray(data["joint_pos"], dtype=np.float32)
 253              joint_vel = np.asarray(data["joint_vel"], dtype=np.float32)
 254              body_pos = np.asarray(data["body_pos_w"], dtype=np.float32)
 255              body_quat = np.asarray(data["body_quat_w"], dtype=np.float32)
 256              body_lin_vel = np.asarray(data["body_lin_vel_w"], dtype=np.float32)
 257              body_ang_vel = np.asarray(data["body_ang_vel_w"], dtype=np.float32)
 258              stored_contract = str(np.asarray(data["joint_coordinate_contract"]).reshape(-1)[0]) if "joint_coordinate_contract" in data else None
 259              stored_joint_names = _string_list(data.get("joint_names", None))
 260              stored_body_names = _string_list(data.get("body_names", None))
 261  
 262          if joint_pos.ndim != 2:
 263              raise ValueError(f'{path}: joint_pos must be [T,J], got {joint_pos.shape}')
 264          source_length = joint_pos.shape[0]
 265          resolved_end = source_length if end_frame is None else end_frame
 266          if start_frame >= source_length or resolved_end > source_length or resolved_end - start_frame < 2:
 267              raise ValueError(
 268                  f'{path}: invalid frame slice [{start_frame}:{resolved_end}] for length {source_length}'
 269              )
 270          joint_pos = joint_pos[start_frame:resolved_end]
 271          joint_vel = joint_vel[start_frame:resolved_end]
 272          body_pos = body_pos[start_frame:resolved_end]
 273          body_quat = body_quat[start_frame:resolved_end]
 274          body_lin_vel = body_lin_vel[start_frame:resolved_end]
 275          body_ang_vel = body_ang_vel[start_frame:resolved_end]
 276  
 277          if joint_pos.ndim != 2 or joint_pos.shape[0] < 2:
 278              raise ValueError(f"{path}: joint_pos must be [T,J] with T>=2, got {joint_pos.shape}")
 279          length = joint_pos.shape[0]
 280          if joint_vel.shape != joint_pos.shape:
 281              raise ValueError(f"{path}: joint velocity shape {joint_vel.shape} != {joint_pos.shape}")
 282          for name, array, tail in (
 283              ("body_pos_w", body_pos, 3),
 284              ("body_quat_w", body_quat, 4),
 285              ("body_lin_vel_w", body_lin_vel, 3),
 286              ("body_ang_vel_w", body_ang_vel, 3),
 287          ):
 288              if array.ndim != 3 or array.shape[0] != length or array.shape[2] != tail:
 289                  raise ValueError(f"{path}: invalid {name} shape {array.shape}")
 290          body_count = body_pos.shape[1]
 291          for name, array in (
 292              ("body_quat_w", body_quat),
 293              ("body_lin_vel_w", body_lin_vel),
 294              ("body_ang_vel_w", body_ang_vel),
 295          ):
 296              if array.shape[1] != body_count:
 297                  raise ValueError(
 298                      f"{path}: {name} body count {array.shape[1]} != body_pos_w count {body_count}"
 299                  )
 300          if stored_joint_names is not None and len(stored_joint_names) != joint_pos.shape[1]:
 301              raise ValueError(
 302                  f"{path}: joint_names count {len(stored_joint_names)} != joint array width {joint_pos.shape[1]}"
 303              )
 304          if stored_body_names is not None and len(stored_body_names) != body_count:
 305              raise ValueError(
 306                  f"{path}: body_names count {len(stored_body_names)} != body array width {body_count}"
 307              )
 308  
 309          stored_joint_names, joint_pos, joint_vel = convert_motion(
 310              stored_joint_names, joint_pos, joint_vel, self.joint_names, stored_contract
 311          )
 312  
 313          if self.joint_names is not None:
 314              if stored_joint_names is None:
 315                  raise ValueError(f"{path}: custom motions require joint_names metadata")
 316              joint_indexes = self._ordered_indexes(stored_joint_names, self.joint_names, "joint", path)
 317              joint_pos = joint_pos[:, joint_indexes]
 318              joint_vel = joint_vel[:, joint_indexes]
 319  
 320          if stored_body_names is None:
 321              body_indexes = self.legacy_body_indexes
 322              if body_indexes and (min(body_indexes) < 0 or max(body_indexes) >= body_count):
 323                  raise ValueError(
 324                      f"{path}: legacy body indexes {body_indexes} exceed body count {body_count}"
 325                  )
 326          else:
 327              body_indexes = self._ordered_indexes(stored_body_names, self.tracked_body_names, "body", path)
 328          body_pos = body_pos[:, body_indexes]
 329          body_quat = body_quat[:, body_indexes]
 330          body_lin_vel = body_lin_vel[:, body_indexes]
 331          body_ang_vel = body_ang_vel[:, body_indexes]
 332  
 333          values = (joint_pos, joint_vel, body_pos, body_quat, body_lin_vel, body_ang_vel)
 334          if not all(np.isfinite(value).all() for value in values):
 335              raise ValueError(f"{path}: motion contains NaN or Inf")
 336          quat_norm = np.linalg.norm(body_quat, axis=-1)
 337          max_quat_error = float(np.max(np.abs(quat_norm - 1.0)))
 338          if max_quat_error > 5.0e-3:
 339              raise ValueError(f"{path}: body quaternion norm error {max_quat_error:.6g}")
 340  
 341          return {
 342              "fps": fps,
 343              "joint_pos": np.ascontiguousarray(joint_pos),
 344              "joint_vel": np.ascontiguousarray(joint_vel),
 345              "body_pos_w": np.ascontiguousarray(body_pos),
 346              "body_quat_w": np.ascontiguousarray(body_quat),
 347              "body_lin_vel_w": np.ascontiguousarray(body_lin_vel),
 348              "body_ang_vel_w": np.ascontiguousarray(body_ang_vel),
 349          }
 350  
 351      @staticmethod
 352      def _ordered_indexes(
 353          stored: Sequence[str], requested: Sequence[str], kind: str, path: Path
 354      ) -> list[int]:
 355          duplicate = {name for name in stored if stored.count(name) > 1}
 356          if duplicate:
 357              raise ValueError(f"{path}: duplicate {kind} names: {sorted(duplicate)}")
 358          lookup = {name: index for index, name in enumerate(stored)}
 359          missing = [name for name in requested if name not in lookup]
 360          if missing:
 361              raise ValueError(f"{path}: missing requested {kind} names: {missing}")
 362          return [lookup[name] for name in requested]
 363  
 364      def frame_indices(self, motion_ids: torch.Tensor, local_frames: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
 365          return self.clip_starts[motion_ids] + local_frames
 366  
 367      def build_bins(self, bin_size_s: float) -> int:
 368          if not np.isfinite(bin_size_s) or bin_size_s <= 0.0:
 369              raise ValueError(f"adaptive_bin_size_s must be positive, got {bin_size_s}")
 370          self.frames_per_bin = max(1, round(self.fps * bin_size_s))
 371          motion_ids: list[int] = []
 372          local_starts: list[int] = []
 373          widths: list[int] = []
 374          weights: list[float] = []
 375          offsets: list[int] = []
 376          cursor = 0
 377          for motion_id, (length, weight) in enumerate(
 378              zip(self.clip_lengths.detach().cpu().tolist(), self.clip_weights.detach().cpu().tolist())
 379          ):
 380              offsets.append(cursor)
 381              # The last frame is a timeout target, never a normal episode start.
 382              startable_length = max(1, length - 1)
 383              for start in range(0, startable_length, self.frames_per_bin):
 384                  motion_ids.append(motion_id)
 385                  local_starts.append(start)
 386                  width = min(self.frames_per_bin, startable_length - start)
 387                  widths.append(width)
 388                  # Weight by startable frames so partial tail bins are not oversampled.
 389                  weighted_width = float(width)
 390                  end = start + width
 391                  for interval_start, interval_end, multiplier in self.clip_sampling_intervals[motion_id]:
 392                      overlap = max(0, min(end, interval_end) - max(start, interval_start))
 393                      weighted_width += overlap * (multiplier - 1.0)
 394                  weights.append(weight * weighted_width)
 395                  cursor += 1
 396          self.bin_motion_ids = torch.tensor(motion_ids, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
 397          self.bin_local_starts = torch.tensor(local_starts, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
 398          self.bin_widths = torch.tensor(widths, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
 399          self.bin_weights = torch.tensor(weights, dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)
 400          self.clip_bin_offsets = torch.tensor(offsets, dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)
 401          return cursor
 402  
 403      def current_bin_indexes(self, motion_ids: torch.Tensor, local_frames: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
 404          if self.frames_per_bin is None or self.clip_bin_offsets is None:
 405              raise RuntimeError("build_bins() must be called first")
 406          last_startable = self.clip_lengths[motion_ids] - 2
 407          startable_frames = torch.minimum(local_frames, last_startable)
 408          return self.clip_bin_offsets[motion_ids] + torch.div(
 409              startable_frames, self.frames_per_bin, rounding_mode="floor"
 410          )
 411  
 412      def sample_from_bins(self, bin_ids: torch.Tensor, fractions: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
 413          if self.bin_motion_ids is None or self.bin_local_starts is None or self.bin_widths is None:
 414              raise RuntimeError("build_bins() must be called first")
 415          motion_ids = self.bin_motion_ids[bin_ids]
 416          offsets = torch.floor(fractions * self.bin_widths[bin_ids].float()).long()
 417          offsets = torch.minimum(offsets, self.bin_widths[bin_ids] - 1)
 418          return motion_ids, self.bin_local_starts[bin_ids] + offsets

  17  def _get_body_indexes(command: MotionCommand, body_names: list[str] | None) -> list[int]:
  18      return [i for i, name in enumerate(command.cfg.body_names) if (body_names is None) or (name in body_names)]

  21  def motion_global_anchor_position_error_exp(env: ManagerBasedRLEnv, command_name: str, std: float) -> torch.Tensor:
  22      command: MotionCommand = env.command_manager.get_term(command_name)
  23      error = torch.sum(torch.square(command.anchor_pos_w - command.robot_anchor_pos_w), dim=-1)

  78  def feet_contact_time(env: ManagerBasedRLEnv, sensor_cfg: SceneEntityCfg, threshold: float) -> torch.Tensor:
  79      contact_sensor: ContactSensor = env.scene.sensors[sensor_cfg.name]
  80      first_air = contact_sensor.compute_first_air(env.step_dt, env.physics_dt)[:, sensor_cfg.body_ids]
  81      last_contact_time = contact_sensor.data.last_contact_time[:, sensor_cfg.body_ids]
  82      reward = torch.sum((last_contact_time < threshold) * first_air, dim=-1)
  83      return reward
  84  
  85  
  86  def motion_joint_position_error_exp(
  87      env: ManagerBasedRLEnv,
  88      command_name: str,
  89      std: float,
  90      joint_names: list[str] | None = None,
  91  ) -> torch.Tensor:
  92      """Soft joint-reference reward, optionally restricted by explicit names."""
  93      command: MotionCommand = env.command_manager.get_term(command_name)
  94      if joint_names is None:
  95          indexes = list(range(command.joint_pos.shape[1]))
  96      else:
  97          if command.cfg.joint_names is None:
  98              raise ValueError("Named joint tracking requires MotionCommandCfg.joint_names")
  99          lookup = {name: index for index, name in enumerate(command.cfg.joint_names)}
 100          missing = [name for name in joint_names if name not in lookup]
 101          if missing:
 102              raise ValueError(f"Unknown reference joints: {missing}")
 103          indexes = [lookup[name] for name in joint_names]
 104      error = torch.mean(torch.square(command.joint_pos[:, indexes] - command.robot_joint_pos[:, indexes]), dim=-1)
 105      return torch.exp(-error / std**2)
 106  
 107  
 108  def feet_slide(
 109      env: ManagerBasedRLEnv,
 110      sensor_cfg: SceneEntityCfg,
 111      asset_cfg: SceneEntityCfg = SceneEntityCfg("robot"),
 112  ) -> torch.Tensor:
 113      """Penalize horizontal foot velocity only while each foot is in contact."""
 114      contact_sensor: ContactSensor = env.scene.sensors[sensor_cfg.name]
 115      contacts = (
 116          contact_sensor.data.net_forces_w_history[:, :, sensor_cfg.body_ids, :]
 117          .norm(dim=-1)
 118          .max(dim=1)[0]
 119          > 1.0
 120      )
 121      asset: Articulation = env.scene[asset_cfg.name]
 122      body_vel = asset.data.body_lin_vel_w[:, asset_cfg.body_ids, :2]
 123      return torch.sum(body_vel.norm(dim=-1) * contacts, dim=1)

   1  """Joint-order-aligned velocity tracking cost (rad/s squared)."""
   2  def motion_joint_velocity_error_l2(env, command_name: str):
   3      command = env.command_manager.get_term(command_name)
   4      # MotionCommand exposes both tensors in the same reference joint order.
   5      # Penalize excess motion relative to reference, not intentional movement.
   6      return (command.robot_joint_vel - command.joint_vel).square().mean(dim=-1)

SHA256 1a69bcfc6a2d508f8195b8ef5de1e411d87106af84240f177ff3cdd3af8f3651

   1  """Optional physical-unit target shaping, shared by training and replay."""
   2  import math
   3  import torch
   4  from isaaclab.envs.mdp.actions.joint_actions import JointPositionAction
   5  from isaaclab.envs.mdp.actions.actions_cfg import JointPositionActionCfg
   6  from isaaclab.utils import configclass
   7  
   8  
   9  class SmoothJointPositionAction(JointPositionAction):
  10      def __init__(self, cfg, env):
  11          super().__init__(cfg, env)
  12          if not math.isfinite(cfg.smoothing_tau) or cfg.smoothing_tau < 0:
  13              raise ValueError('smoothing_tau must be finite and nonnegative')
  14          if not math.isfinite(cfg.target_velocity_scale) or not 0 < cfg.target_velocity_scale <= 1:
  15              raise ValueError("target_velocity_scale must be in (0,1]")
  16          self._target_dt = env.step_dt
  17          self._alpha = 1.0 if cfg.smoothing_tau == 0 else -math.expm1(-env.step_dt / cfg.smoothing_tau)
  18          self._target_memory = self._asset.data.joint_pos[:, self._joint_ids].clone()
  19          self._target_initialized = torch.zeros(self.num_envs, dtype=torch.bool, device=self.device)
  20  
  21      def process_actions(self, actions):
  22          super().process_actions(actions)
  23          if self.cfg.smoothing_tau == 0 and not self.cfg.limit_target_velocity:
  24              return
  25          missing = ~self._target_initialized
  26          self._target_memory[missing] = self._asset.data.joint_pos[missing][:, self._joint_ids]
  27          self._target_initialized[:] = True
  28          change = self._alpha * (self._processed_actions - self._target_memory)
  29          if self.cfg.limit_target_velocity:
  30              maximum = self._asset.data.joint_vel_limits[:, self._joint_ids] * self._target_dt * self.cfg.target_velocity_scale
  31              change = torch.clamp(change, min=-maximum, max=maximum)
  32          self._target_memory.add_(change)
  33          self._processed_actions = self._target_memory.clone()
  34  
  35      def reset(self, env_ids=None):
  36          super().reset(env_ids)
  37          # Seed after all reset events have placed the robot, on the next command.
  38          self._target_initialized[env_ids] = False
  39  
  40  
  41  @configclass
  42  class SmoothJointPositionActionCfg(JointPositionActionCfg):
  43      class_type: type = SmoothJointPositionAction
  44      smoothing_tau: float = 0.0
  45      limit_target_velocity: bool = False
  46      target_velocity_scale: float = 1.0

 141  @configclass
 142  class ObservationsCfg:
 143      @configclass
 144      class PolicyCfg(ObsGroup):
 145          motion_command = ObsTerm(func=mdp.generated_commands, params={"command_name": "motion"})
 146          motion_anchor_pos_b = ObsTerm(func=mdp.motion_anchor_pos_b, params={"command_name": "motion"})
 147          motion_anchor_ori_b = ObsTerm(
 148              func=mdp.motion_anchor_ori_b,
 149              params={"command_name": "motion"},
 150              noise=Unoise(n_min=-0.03, n_max=0.03),
 151          )
 152          base_lin_vel = ObsTerm(func=mdp.base_lin_vel, noise=Unoise(n_min=-0.10, n_max=0.10))
 153          base_ang_vel = ObsTerm(func=mdp.base_ang_vel, noise=Unoise(n_min=-0.15, n_max=0.15))
 154          anchor_lin_vel_error_b = ObsTerm(
 155              func=mdp.motion_anchor_lin_vel_error_b,
 156              params={"command_name": "motion"},
 157          )
 158          anchor_ang_vel_error_b = ObsTerm(
 159              func=mdp.motion_anchor_ang_vel_error_b,
 160              params={"command_name": "motion"},
 161          )
 162          joint_pos_rel = ObsTerm(
 163              func=mdp.joint_pos_rel,
 164              params={"asset_cfg": JOINT_ENTITY},
 165              noise=Unoise(n_min=-0.01, n_max=0.01),
 166          )
 167          joint_vel_rel = ObsTerm(
 168              func=mdp.joint_vel_rel,
 169              params={"asset_cfg": JOINT_ENTITY},
 170              noise=Unoise(n_min=-0.30, n_max=0.30),
 171          )
 172          last_action = ObsTerm(func=mdp.last_action)
 173  
 174          def __post_init__(self):
 175              self.enable_corruption = True
 176              self.concatenate_terms = True
 177  
 178      @configclass
 179      class PrivilegedCfg(ObsGroup):
 180          command = ObsTerm(func=mdp.generated_commands, params={"command_name": "motion"})
 181          motion_anchor_pos_b = ObsTerm(func=mdp.motion_anchor_pos_b, params={"command_name": "motion"})
 182          motion_anchor_ori_b = ObsTerm(func=mdp.motion_anchor_ori_b, params={"command_name": "motion"})
 183          body_pos = ObsTerm(func=mdp.robot_body_pos_b, params={"command_name": "motion"})
 184          body_ori = ObsTerm(func=mdp.robot_body_ori_b, params={"command_name": "motion"})
 185          base_lin_vel = ObsTerm(func=mdp.base_lin_vel)
 186          base_ang_vel = ObsTerm(func=mdp.base_ang_vel)
 187          joint_pos = ObsTerm(func=mdp.joint_pos_rel, params={"asset_cfg": JOINT_ENTITY})
 188          joint_vel = ObsTerm(func=mdp.joint_vel_rel, params={"asset_cfg": JOINT_ENTITY})
 189          actions = ObsTerm(func=mdp.last_action)
 190  
 191      policy: PolicyCfg = PolicyCfg()
 192      critic: PrivilegedCfg = PrivilegedCfg()

 196  class EventCfg:
 197      physics_material = EventTerm(
 198          func=mdp.randomize_rigid_body_material,
 199          mode="startup",
 200          params={
 201              "asset_cfg": SceneEntityCfg("robot", body_names=".*"),
 202              "static_friction_range": (0.7, 1.3),
 203              "dynamic_friction_range": (0.6, 1.1),
 204              "restitution_range": (0.0, 0.1),
 205              "num_buckets": 32,
 206          },
 207      )
 208      add_joint_default_pos = EventTerm(
 209          func=mdp.randomize_joint_default_pos,
 210          mode="startup",
 211          params={
 212              "asset_cfg": JOINT_ENTITY,
 213              "pos_distribution_params": (-0.005, 0.005),
 214              "operation": "add",
 215          },
 216      )
 217      base_com = EventTerm(
 218          func=mdp.randomize_rigid_body_com,
 219          mode="startup",
 220          params={
 221              "asset_cfg": SceneEntityCfg("robot", body_names=ANCHOR_BODY),
 222              "com_range": {"x": (-0.002, 0.002), "y": (-0.002, 0.002), "z": (-0.002, 0.002)},
 223          },

 228  class RewardsCfg:
 229      # Opt-in experiment; zero preserves the existing reward objective.
 230      motion_joint_vel_cost = RewTerm(
 231          func=mdp.motion_joint_velocity_error_l2, weight=0.0,
 232          params={"command_name": "motion"},
 233      )
 234      joint_acc = RewTerm(func=mdp.joint_acc_l2, weight=-1.0e-7, params={"asset_cfg": JOINT_ENTITY})
 235      joint_torque = RewTerm(func=mdp.joint_torques_l2, weight=-2.0e-4, params={"asset_cfg": JOINT_ENTITY})
 236      action_rate_l2 = RewTerm(func=mdp.action_rate_l2, weight=-0.05)
 237      joint_limit = RewTerm(func=mdp.joint_pos_limits, weight=-5.0, params={"asset_cfg": JOINT_ENTITY})
 238      foot_roll_default = RewTerm(
 239          func=mdp.joint_deviation_l1,
 240          weight=-0.02,
 241          params={
 242              "asset_cfg": SceneEntityCfg(
 243                  "robot",
 244                  joint_names=["left_foot_roll", "right_foot_roll"],
 245                  preserve_order=True,

 248      )

 250      motion_global_anchor_pos = RewTerm(

 254      )
 255      motion_global_anchor_ori = RewTerm(
 256          func=mdp.motion_global_anchor_orientation_error_exp,

 259      )

 273                  "right_thigh", "right_mid_leg", "right_foot",
 274              ],
 275          },
 276      )

 278          func=mdp.motion_relative_body_position_error_exp,

 283              "body_names": FOOT_BODIES,

 290              "command_name": "motion",
 291              "std": 0.18,
 292              "body_names": [
 293                  "left_upper_arm", "left_force_arm", "left_wrist",
 294                  "right_upper_arm", "right_force_arm", "right_wrist",
 295              ],
 296          },
 297      )
 298      motion_body_ori = RewTerm(
 299          func=mdp.motion_relative_body_orientation_error_exp,
 300          weight=0.75,
 301          params={"command_name": "motion", "std": 0.5},
 302      )
 303      motion_body_lin_vel = RewTerm(

 305          weight=0.75,
 306          params={"command_name": "motion", "std": 0.75},
 307      )
 308      motion_body_ang_vel = RewTerm(

 310          weight=0.5,
 311          params={"command_name": "motion", "std": 2.0},

 313      motion_joint_pos = RewTerm(

 315          weight=0.35,
 316          params={"command_name": "motion", "std": 0.5, "joint_names": RETARGETED_JOINT_NAMES},

 318      feet_slide = RewTerm(

 320          weight=-0.2,

 322              "asset_cfg": SceneEntityCfg("robot", body_names=FOOT_BODIES, preserve_order=True),
 323              "sensor_cfg": SceneEntityCfg("contact_forces", body_names=FOOT_BODIES, preserve_order=True),
 324          },

 326      undesired_contacts = RewTerm(
 327          func=mdp.undesired_contacts,
 328          weight=-0.2,
 329          params={
 330              "sensor_cfg": SceneEntityCfg(
 331                  "contact_forces",

 339  @configclass
 340  class TerminationsCfg:
 341      time_out = DoneTerm(func=mdp.time_out, time_out=True)
 342      motion_end = DoneTerm(func=mdp.motion_end, time_out=True, params={"command_name": "motion"})
 343      anchor_pos = DoneTerm(
 344          func=mdp.bad_anchor_pos_z_only,
 345          params={"command_name": "motion", "threshold": 0.12},
 346      )
 347      anchor_ori = DoneTerm(
 348          func=mdp.bad_anchor_ori,
 349          params={"asset_cfg": SceneEntityCfg("robot"), "command_name": "motion", "threshold": 0.8},
 350      )
 351      ee_body_pos = DoneTerm(
 352          func=mdp.bad_motion_body_pos_z_only,
 353          params={
 354              "command_name": "motion",
 355              "threshold": 0.12,
 356              "body_names": FOOT_BODIES + WRIST_BODIES,
 357          },

 361  @configclass
 362  class RobotEnvCfg(ManagerBasedRLEnvCfg):
 363      scene: RobotSceneCfg = RobotSceneCfg(num_envs=1024, env_spacing=1.0)
 364      observations: ObservationsCfg = ObservationsCfg()
 365      actions: ActionsCfg = ActionsCfg()
 366      commands: CommandsCfg = CommandsCfg()
 367      rewards: RewardsCfg = RewardsCfg()
 368      terminations: TerminationsCfg = TerminationsCfg()
 369      events: EventCfg = EventCfg()
 370      curriculum = None
 371  
 372      def __post_init__(self):
 373          self.decimation = 4
 374          self.episode_length_s = 30.0
 375          self.sim.dt = 0.005
 376          self.sim.render_interval = self.decimation
 377          self.sim.physics_material = self.scene.terrain.physics_material
 378          self.sim.physx.gpu_max_rigid_patch_count = 10 * 2**15
 379          for field, variable in (("stiffness", "P9_PD_STIFFNESS_MULT"), ("damping", "P9_PD_DAMPING_MULT")):
 380              multiplier = float(os.environ.get(variable, "1"))
 381              if not math.isfinite(multiplier) or multiplier <= 0:
 382                  raise ValueError(f"{variable} must be finite and positive")
 383              for group in ("leg_major", "waist"):
 384                  actuator = self.scene.robot.actuators[group]
 385                  setattr(actuator, field, getattr(actuator, field) * multiplier)
 386  
 387  
 388  
 389  class RobotPlayEnvCfg(RobotEnvCfg):
 390      def __post_init__(self):
 391          super().__post_init__()
 392          self.scene.num_envs = 1
 393          self.episode_length_s = 1.0e9

SHA256 8011acf38822d0fa5e07f22afd026253e96821cbb02e719d7e50a0e9378fd999

   1  """TW-44 table: aligned angle = sign * original CAD angle + initial reading.
   2  
   3  The pre-change training initial readings were all zero. Offsets are readings
   4  at that same physical pose, not additional physical rotations.
   5  """
   6  import math
   7  
   8  CONTRACT_VERSION = 'tw44_table_v2'
   9  LIMIT_OVERRIDES = {name: (-math.pi / 2, math.pi / 2) for name in (
  10      'left_knee_pitch_joint', 'right_knee_pitch_joint',
  11      'left_shoulder_pitch_joint', 'right_shoulder_pitch_joint')}
  12  
  13  COORDINATES = {
  14      'left_hip_linkage_pitch': ('left_hip_pitch_joint', -1, 0.0),
  15      'left_thigh_roll': ('left_hip_roll_joint', 1, 0.0),
  16      'left_knee_linkage_yaw': ('left_hip_yaw_joint', 1, 0.0),
  17      'left_mid_leg_pitch': ('left_knee_pitch_joint', -1, 0.0),
  18      'left_calf_yaw': ('left_ankle_yaw_joint', -1, 0.0),
  19      'left_ankle_pitch': ('left_ankle_pitch_joint', 1, 0.0),
  20      'right_hip_linkage_pitch': ('right_hip_pitch_joint', -1, 0.0),
  21      'right_thigh_roll': ('right_hip_roll_joint', 1, 0.0),
  22      'right_knee_linkage_yaw': ('right_hip_yaw_joint', 1, 0.0),
  23      'right_mid_leg_pitch': ('right_knee_pitch_joint', 1, 0.0),
  24      'right_calf_yaw': ('right_ankle_yaw_joint', 1, 0.0),
  25      'right_ankle_pitch': ('right_ankle_pitch_joint', 1, 0.0),
  26      'left_shoulder_linkage_pitch': ('left_shoulder_pitch_joint', -1, 0.0),
  27      'left_upper_arm_roll': ('left_shoulder_roll_joint', 1, -math.pi / 2),
  28      'left_elbow_linkage_pitch': ('left_shoulder_yaw_joint', 1, 0.0),
  29      'left_force_arm_yaw': ('left_elbow_pitch_joint', 1, 0.0),
  30      'right_shoulder_linkage_pitch': ('right_shoulder_pitch_joint', -1, 0.0),
  31      'right_upper_arm_roll': ('right_shoulder_roll_joint', 1, math.pi / 2),
  32      'right_elbow_linkage_pitch': ('right_shoulder_yaw_joint', 1, 0.0),
  33      'right_force_arm_yaw': ('right_elbow_pitch_joint', 1, 0.0),
  34  }
  35  
  36  
  37  def rename(name):
  38      return COORDINATES.get(name, (name, 1, 0.0))[0]
  39  
  40  
  41  def convert_motion(names, positions, velocities, requested_names, contract=None):
  42      """Convert legacy CAD columns only when aligned coordinates are requested.
  43  
  44  Body FK arrays remain valid because the conversion preserves physical pose.
  45  Current-version aligned clips and other robot families pass through unchanged.
  46  """
  47      if names is None or requested_names is None:
  48          return names, positions, velocities
  49      legacy = [name for name in COORDINATES if name in names and rename(name) in requested_names]
  50      if not legacy:
  51          if all(new in names for new, _, _ in COORDINATES.values()) and contract != CONTRACT_VERSION:
  52              raise ValueError('Stale aligned motion coordinates; regenerate with ' + CONTRACT_VERSION)
  53          return names, positions, velocities
  54      converted_names = [rename(name) for name in names]
  55      if len(set(converted_names)) != len(converted_names):
  56          raise ValueError('Mixed legacy/aligned joint coordinate names')
  57      positions = positions.copy()
  58      velocities = velocities.copy()
  59      for name in legacy:
  60          column = names.index(name)
  61          _, sign, offset = COORDINATES[name]
  62          positions[:, column] = sign * positions[:, column] + offset
  63          velocities[:, column] *= sign
  64      return converted_names, positions, velocities

  34  // Primal active-set solution of a strictly convex box QP.
  35  // Returns only after feasibility and KKT stationarity checks pass.
  36  Vec solve_box_qp(const Mat& H, const Vec& c, const Vec& lo, const Vec& hi, double* residual) {
  37      const int n=c.size();
  38      if(H.rows()!=n || H.cols()!=n || lo.size()!=n || hi.size()!=n ||
  39         !H.allFinite() || !c.allFinite() || !lo.allFinite() || !hi.allFinite() ||
  40         (lo.array()>hi.array()).any())
  41          throw std::runtime_error("Invalid box QP");
  42      Vec x=Vec::Zero(n).cwiseMax(lo).cwiseMin(hi);
  43      std::vector<int> active(n,0);
  44      for(int i=0;i<n;++i) {
  45          if(lo[i]==hi[i]) active[i]=2;
  46          else if(x[i]<=lo[i]) active[i]=-1;
  47          else if(x[i]>=hi[i]) active[i]=1;
  48      }
  49      const double tol=1e-10;
  50      for(int iteration=0;iteration<2000;++iteration) {
  51          std::vector<int> free;
  52          for(int i=0;i<n;++i) if(!active[i]) free.push_back(i);
  53          Vec gradient=H*x+c, direction=Vec::Zero(n);
  54          if(!free.empty()) {
  55              Mat sub(free.size(),free.size()); Vec rhs(free.size());
  56              for(size_t a=0;a<free.size();++a) {
  57                  rhs[a]=-gradient[free[a]];
  58                  for(size_t b=0;b<free.size();++b) sub(a,b)=H(free[a],free[b]);
  59              }
  60              Eigen::LDLT<Mat> factor(sub);
  61              if(factor.info()!=Eigen::Success || !factor.isPositive())
  62                  throw std::runtime_error("QP Hessian not positive definite");
  63              Vec step=factor.solve(rhs);
  64              for(size_t a=0;a<free.size();++a) direction[free[a]]=step[a];
  65          }
  66          double alpha=1.0; int blocker=-1, side=0;
  67          for(int i:free) {
  68              double t=2.; int s=0;
  69              if(direction[i]>0) { t=(hi[i]-x[i])/direction[i]; s=1; }
  70              if(direction[i]<0) { t=(lo[i]-x[i])/direction[i]; s=-1; }
  71              if(t<alpha) { alpha=std::max(0.,t);blocker=i;side=s; }
  72          }
  73          x+=alpha*direction;
  74          if(blocker>=0) {
  75              active[blocker]=side;x[blocker]=side<0?lo[blocker]:hi[blocker];
  76              continue;
  77          }
  78          gradient=H*x+c;
  79          double worst=tol; int release=-1;
  80          for(int i=0;i<n;++i) {
  81              double violation=active[i]==-1?-gradient[i]:active[i]==1?gradient[i]:0.;
  82              if(violation>worst) {worst=violation;release=i;}
  83          }
  84          if(release>=0) {active[release]=0;continue;}
  85          double err=0.;
  86          for(int i=0;i<n;++i) {
  87              double station=active[i]==2?0.:active[i]==-1?std::max(0.,-gradient[i]):
  88                  active[i]==1?std::max(0.,gradient[i]):std::abs(gradient[i]);
  89              err=std::max(err,station);
  90          }
  91          if((x-lo).minCoeff() < -1e-9 || (hi-x).minCoeff() < -1e-9 || err>1e-7)
  92              throw std::runtime_error("QP failed feasibility/KKT validation");
  93          if(residual) *residual=err;
  94          return x;
  95      }
  96      throw std::runtime_error("QP active-set iteration limit");
  97  }
