  99  struct Robot::Impl {
 100      pinocchio::Model model;
 101      std::unique_ptr<pinocchio::Data> data,filtered_data;
 102      Vec q, ref;
 103      std::vector<int> indices;
 104      std::vector<pinocchio::FrameIndex> frame_ids;
 105      std::array<int,9> smpl = {18,19,20,21,4,5,7,8,15};
 106      std::vector<V3> last;
 107      double kkt=0;
 108      int iterations=1;
 109      ObjectiveMode objective=ObjectiveMode::SE3;
 110      double distance_epsilon_m=0.,arm_vector_scale=0.,filter_aware_strength=0.;
 111      bool filter_adaptive=false,shoulder_guard=false;
 112      TaskTuning tuning;
 113      Vec previous_step;
 114      // A first source frame has no prior velocity estimate.
 115      bool has_previous_step=false;
 116      double gate_alpha=1.,orientation_strength=0.,posture_bias=1.;
 117      std::array<double,9> gates{};
 118      std::array<pinocchio::FrameIndex,2> shoulder_ids{};
 119      pinocchio::FrameIndex neck_id{};
 120      void update() {
 121          pinocchio::computeJointJacobians(model,*data,q);
 122          pinocchio::updateFramePlacements(model,*data);
 123      }
 124      pinocchio::FrameIndex id(const std::string& name) const {
 125          if(!model.existFrame(name)) throw std::runtime_error("Missing frame: "+name);
 126          return model.getFrameId(name);
 127      }
 128      V3 pos(const std::string& name) const {return data->oMf[id(name)].translation();}
 129  };
 130  Robot::Robot(const std::string& urdf, int iterations, ObjectiveMode objective,
 131               double distance_epsilon_m, double arm_vector_scale, double filter_aware_strength, bool filter_adaptive, double gate_alpha, double orientation_strength, double posture_bias, bool shoulder_guard, TaskTuning tuning):p(new Impl) {
 132      for(double v:{tuning.position_scale,tuning.midpoint_scale})
 133          if(!std::isfinite(v) || v<=0. || v>20.)throw std::runtime_error("Task scale must be in (0,20]");
 134      if(!std::isfinite(tuning.acceleration_cost) || tuning.acceleration_cost<0. || tuning.acceleration_cost>10.)
 135          throw std::runtime_error("Acceleration cost must be in [0,10]");
 136      if(tuning.acceleration_cost>0. && iterations!=1)throw std::runtime_error("Acceleration penalty requires one iteration");
 137      if(!std::isfinite(tuning.mean_distance_epsilon) || tuning.mean_distance_epsilon<0. || tuning.mean_distance_epsilon>1.)
 138          throw std::runtime_error("Mean distance epsilon must be in [0,1] meters");
 139      if(tuning.head_axis_only && (!tuning.human_head || objective!=ObjectiveMode::Cartesian || orientation_strength!=0.))
 140          throw std::runtime_error("Head axis mode requires human head, Cartesian tasks and raw orientation");
 141      if(tuning.ankle_aware && (objective!=ObjectiveMode::Cartesian || !(filter_aware_strength>0.)))
 142          throw std::runtime_error("Ankle-aware mode requires Cartesian filter-aware tasks");
 143      if(tuning.neck_axis && !tuning.head_axis_only)throw std::runtime_error("Neck axis requires head axis mode");
 144      if(!std::isfinite(tuning.output_acceleration_cost) || tuning.output_acceleration_cost<0. || tuning.output_acceleration_cost>100. ||
 145         (tuning.output_acceleration_cost>0. && (!(filter_aware_strength>0.) || iterations!=1)))
 146          throw std::runtime_error("Output acceleration requires one-iteration filter-aware mode and cost in [0,100]");
 147      if(!std::isfinite(tuning.neck_posture_bias) || tuning.neck_posture_bias<0. || tuning.neck_posture_bias>1.)
 148          throw std::runtime_error("Neck posture bias must be in [0,1]");
 149      p->tuning=tuning;
 150      p->shoulder_guard=shoulder_guard;
 151      if(!std::isfinite(posture_bias) || posture_bias<0. || posture_bias>1.)throw std::runtime_error("Posture bias must be in [0,1]");
 152      p->posture_bias=posture_bias;
 153      if(!std::isfinite(orientation_strength) || orientation_strength<0. || orientation_strength>1. ||
 154         (orientation_strength>0. && !(filter_aware_strength>0.)))
 155          throw std::runtime_error("Orientation strength requires filter prediction and must be in [0,1]");
 156      p->orientation_strength=orientation_strength;
 157      if(!std::isfinite(gate_alpha) || gate_alpha<=0. || gate_alpha>1. || (!filter_adaptive && gate_alpha!=1.))
 158          throw std::runtime_error("Gate alpha must be in (0,1] and smoothing requires adaptive mode");
 159      p->gate_alpha=gate_alpha;
 160      if(filter_adaptive && !(filter_aware_strength>0.))throw std::runtime_error("Adaptive filter requires positive strength");
 161      p->filter_adaptive=filter_adaptive;
 162      if(!std::isfinite(filter_aware_strength) || filter_aware_strength<0. || filter_aware_strength>1.)
 163          throw std::runtime_error("Filter-aware strength must be in [0,1]");
 164      if(filter_aware_strength>0. && (iterations!=1 || distance_epsilon_m!=0. || arm_vector_scale!=0.))
 165          throw std::runtime_error("Filter-aware experiment requires one iteration and no other experimental tasks");
 166      p->filter_aware_strength=filter_aware_strength;
 167      if(!std::isfinite(arm_vector_scale) || arm_vector_scale<0.)
 168          throw std::runtime_error("Arm vector scale must be finite and nonnegative");
 169      p->arm_vector_scale=arm_vector_scale;
 170      if(!std::isfinite(distance_epsilon_m) || distance_epsilon_m<0.)
 171          throw std::runtime_error("Distance epsilon must be finite and nonnegative");
 172      if(distance_epsilon_m>0. && (objective!=ObjectiveMode::Cartesian || iterations!=1))
 173          throw std::runtime_error("Distance loss requires Cartesian objective and exactly one iteration");
 174      p->distance_epsilon_m=distance_epsilon_m;
 175      p->objective=objective;
 176      if(iterations<1 || iterations>20)throw std::runtime_error("IK iterations must be in [1,20]");
 177      p->iterations=iterations;
 178      pinocchio::urdf::buildModel(urdf,p->model);
 179      if(p->model.nq!=28 || p->model.nv!=28)
 180          throw std::runtime_error("This IK_V2 port requires the 28 scalar-joint fixed-base model");
 181      p->ref=pinocchio::neutral(p->model);
 182      for(int i=0;i<28;++i) {
 183          if(!p->model.existJointName(names[i])) throw std::runtime_error("Missing joint: "+names[i]);
 184          const auto& j=p->model.joints[p->model.getJointId(names[i])];
 185          if(j.nq()!=1 || j.nv()!=1 || j.idx_q()!=j.idx_v())
 186              throw std::runtime_error("Expected scalar joint coordinates");
 187          int index=j.idx_q();p->indices.push_back(index);
 188          // Limits are loaded from the derived training URDF.
 189          
 190          if(i==16) p->ref[index]=static_cast<float>(-1.57079632679);
 191          if(i==21) p->ref[index]=static_cast<float>(1.57079632679);
 192      }
 193      p->data=std::make_unique<pinocchio::Data>(p->model);
 194      if(filter_aware_strength>0.)p->filtered_data=std::make_unique<pinocchio::Data>(p->model);
 195      for(const auto& name:{"chest","left_force_arm","right_force_arm","left_hand","right_hand",
 196                          "left_calf","right_calf","left_foot","right_foot","head"})
 197          p->frame_ids.push_back(p->id(name));
 198      p->shoulder_ids={p->id("left_upper_arm"),p->id("right_upper_arm")};
 199      p->neck_id=p->id("neck_linkage");
 200      reset();
 201  }
 202  Robot::~Robot()=default;
 203  void Robot::reset(){p->q=p->ref;p->has_previous_step=false;p->previous_step=Vec::Zero(28);p->last.clear();p->gates.fill(0.);p->kkt=0;p->update();}
 204  void Robot::clear_targets(){p->last.clear();p->gates.fill(0.);p->has_previous_step=false;p->previous_step=Vec::Zero(28);}
 205  void Robot::seed(const Vec& named) {
 206      if(named.size()!=28 || !named.allFinite()) throw std::runtime_error("Seed must have 28 finite angles");
 207      for(int i=0;i<28;++i)p->q[p->indices[i]]=named[i];
 208      p->has_previous_step=false;p->previous_step=Vec::Zero(28);p->last.clear();p->gates.fill(0.);p->kkt=0;p->update();
 209  }
 210  V3 Robot::waist() const{return p->pos("waist");}
 211  Vec Robot::named(const Vec& q) const{Vec x(28);for(int i=0;i<28;++i)x[i]=q[p->indices[i]];return x;}
 212  Vec Robot::clamp(const Vec& q) const{Vec x=q;for(int i=0;i<28;++i)x[i]=std::clamp(x[i],p->model.lowerPositionLimit[p->indices[i]],p->model.upperPositionLimit[p->indices[i]]);return x;}
 213  Vec Robot::clamp_degrees(const Vec& q) const {
 214      Vec out=q;const float factor=180.0f/static_cast<float>(3.14159265358979323846);
 215      for(int i=0;i<28;++i)out[i]=std::clamp(out[i],double(p->model.lowerPositionLimit[p->indices[i]]*factor),double(p->model.upperPositionLimit[p->indices[i]]*factor));
 216      return out;
 217  }
 218  double Robot::max_kkt() const{return p->kkt;}
 219  Frame Robot::targets(const Frame& j) const {
 220      Frame n=j;
 221      n[12]=p->pos("neck_linkage");n[15]=n[12]+norm(j[15]-j[12])*0.022419877341323689;
 222      for(int side=0;side<2;++side) {
 223          int hip=1+side,knee=4+side,ankle=7+side,shoulder=16+side,elbow=18+side,wrist=20+side;
 224          n[hip]=p->pos(side?"right_thigh":"left_thigh");
 225          n[knee]=n[hip]+norm(j[knee]-j[hip])*(side ? 0.10176699207613756 : 0.10175799515713191);
 226          n[ankle]=n[knee]+norm(j[ankle]-j[knee])*(side ? 0.10513069629924765 : 0.10513186872353147);
 227          n[shoulder]=p->pos(side?"right_upper_arm":"left_upper_arm");
 228          n[elbow]=n[shoulder]+norm(j[elbow]-j[shoulder])*(side ? 0.072794471802542324 : 0.071759075135702705);
 229          n[wrist]=n[elbow]+norm(j[wrist]-j[elbow])*(side ? 0.026286395854108718 : 0.027073234014428342);
 230      }
 231      return n;
 232  }
 233  Vec Robot::solve(const Frame& j,const Frame& target,const M3* head,
 234                   const Vec* filter_offset,double filter_gain,const std::array<double,2>* ankle_override,
 235                   const Vec* output_acceleration_target) {
 236      if(p->filter_aware_strength>0. &&
 237         (!filter_offset || filter_offset->size()!=28 || !filter_offset->allFinite() ||
 238          !std::isfinite(filter_gain) || filter_gain<=0. || filter_gain>1.))
 239          throw std::runtime_error("Invalid filter prediction");
 240      if(p->tuning.ankle_aware && (!ankle_override ||
 241         !std::isfinite((*ankle_override)[0]) || !std::isfinite((*ankle_override)[1]) ||
 242         std::abs((*ankle_override)[0])>double(.79f) || std::abs((*ankle_override)[1])>double(.79f)))
 243          throw std::runtime_error("Invalid ankle override");
 244      if(output_acceleration_target && (output_acceleration_target->size()!=28 || !output_acceleration_target->allFinite()))
 245          throw std::runtime_error("Invalid output acceleration target");
 246      const bool predict=p->filter_aware_strength>0. && (filter_gain<1. || p->tuning.ankle_aware);
 247      if(predict) {
 248          Vec q_filtered=filter_gain*p->q+*filter_offset;
 249          if(p->tuning.ankle_aware)for(int side=0;side<2;++side)
 250              q_filtered[p->indices[side?11:5]]=(*ankle_override)[side];
 251          pinocchio::computeJointJacobians(p->model,*p->filtered_data,q_filtered);
 252          pinocchio::updateFramePlacements(p->model,*p->filtered_data);
 253      }
 254      for(int i=0;i<28;++i)
 255          if(p->q[i]<p->model.lowerPositionLimit[i]-1e-6 || p->q[i]>p->model.upperPositionLimit[i]+1e-6)
 256              throw std::runtime_error("Configuration outside joint limits");
 257      V3 up=norm((j[13]+j[14])/2.-j[3]),right=norm(j[13]-j[14]);
 258      V3 forward=norm(right.cross(up));right=norm(up.cross(forward));
 259      M3 chest;chest.col(0)=forward;chest.col(1)=right;chest.col(2)=up;
 260      std::vector<V3> limited;
 261      for(int k=0;k<9;++k) {
 262          V3 pos=target[p->smpl[k]];
 263          if(!p->last.empty()) {
 264              V3 d=pos-p->last[k];
 265              if(d.norm()>.045)pos=p->last[k]+d/d.norm()*.045;
 266          }
 267          limited.push_back(pos);
 268      }
 269      p->last=limited;
 270      // Freeze targets, speed-limit history and the original per-frame joint box.
 271      const Vec frame_low=p->q+.5*pinocchio::difference(p->model,p->q,p->model.lowerPositionLimit);
 272      const Vec frame_high=p->q+.5*pinocchio::difference(p->model,p->q,p->model.upperPositionLimit);
 273      for(int iteration=0;iteration<p->iterations;++iteration) {
 274          Mat H=Mat::Identity(28,28)*1e-3; Vec c=Vec::Zero(28);
 275          for(int k=0;k<10;++k) {
 276              auto id=p->frame_ids[k];
 277              const auto& pose=p->data->oMf[id];
 278              M3 rot=M3::Identity();V3 pos;
 279              double wc=0.,wr=0.;
 280              if(k==0){rot=chest;pos=pose.translation();wr=50.;}
 281              else {
 282                  pos=limited[k-1];
 283                  wc=(k==1||k==2||k==5||k==6)?5.:(k==9?1.:10.);
 284                  wc*=p->tuning.position_scale;
 285                  if(k==1||k==2||k==5||k==6)wc*=p->tuning.midpoint_scale;
 286                  wr=(k==7||k==8)?1.:(k==9?5.:0.);
 287                  if(k==9) {
 288                      if(head)rot=chest*(*head);
 289                      else if(p->tuning.human_head)rot=human_head_rotation(j,chest);
 290                  }
 291              }
 292  if(k==0){ M3 frame_offset;frame_offset<<-1,-3.2310891488651735e-15,0,3.2310891488651735e-15,-1,0,0,0,1;rot=rot*frame_offset; }
 293  if(k==7){ M3 frame_offset;frame_offset<<1,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1;rot=rot*frame_offset; }
 294  if(k==8){ M3 frame_offset;frame_offset<<1,0,0,0,1,0,0,0,1;rot=rot*frame_offset; }
 295  if(k==9){ M3 frame_offset;frame_offset<<1,6.4621782977303469e-15,0,-6.4621782977303469e-15,1,0,0,0,1;rot=rot*frame_offset; }
 296              pinocchio::SE3 goal(rot,pos);
 297              Eigen::Matrix<double,6,Eigen::Dynamic> local(6,28);local.setZero();
 298              pinocchio::getFrameJacobian(p->model,*p->data,id,pinocchio::LOCAL,local);
 299              Mat J;
 300              Eigen::Matrix<double,6,1> e;
 301              // Chest is orientation-only: preserve its original computation.
 302              if(p->objective==ObjectiveMode::Cartesian && k!=0) {
 303                  separated_task(pose,goal,local,e,J);
 304              } else {
 305                  Eigen::Matrix<double,6,6> logJ;
 306                  pinocchio::Jlog6(goal.actInv(pose),logJ);
 307                  J=-logJ*local;
 308                  e=pinocchio::log6(pose.actInv(goal)).toVector();
 309              }
 310              if(k==9 && !head && p->tuning.head_axis_only) {
 311                  if(p->tuning.neck_axis) {
 312                      const auto& anchor=p->data->oMf[p->neck_id];
 313                      const V3 bone=pose.translation()-anchor.translation();
 314                      const double length=bone.norm();
 315                      if(length<1e-8)throw std::runtime_error("Degenerate robot neck segment");
 316                      const V3 axis=bone/length;
 317                      Eigen::Matrix<double,6,Eigen::Dynamic> nj(6,28);nj.setZero();
 318                      pinocchio::getFrameJacobian(p->model,*p->data,p->neck_id,pinocchio::LOCAL,nj);
 319                      e.tail<3>()=axis-rot.col(2);
 320                      J.bottomRows(3)=((M3::Identity()-axis*axis.transpose())/length)*
 321                          (pose.rotation()*local.topRows(3)-anchor.rotation()*nj.topRows(3));
 322                  } else {
 323                      e.tail<3>()=pose.rotation().col(2)-rot.col(2);
 324                      M3 cross;cross<<0,-1,0,1,0,0,0,0,0;
 325                      J.bottomRows(3)=-pose.rotation()*cross*local.bottomRows(3);
 326                  }
 327              }
 328              // One frozen coefficient per position task, one QP per source frame.
 329              // Use unweighted Cartesian distance; orientation and posture are unchanged.
 330              if(k!=0 && p->distance_epsilon_m>0.)
 331                  wc*=std::sqrt(distance_loss_weight(e.head(3).norm(),p->distance_epsilon_m));
 332              if(k!=0 && p->tuning.mean_distance_epsilon>0.)
 333                  wc*=std::sqrt(distance_loss_weight((pose.translation()-pos).norm(),p->tuning.mean_distance_epsilon));
 334              if(predict && k!=0) {
 335                  // Blend the original position objective with the same objective
 336                  // evaluated at predicted filtered output; orientation stays raw.
 337                  const auto& filtered_pose=p->filtered_data->oMf[id];
 338                  Eigen::Matrix<double,6,Eigen::Dynamic> fj(6,28);fj.setZero();
 339                  pinocchio::getFrameJacobian(p->model,*p->filtered_data,id,pinocchio::LOCAL,fj);
 340                  if(p->tuning.ankle_aware){fj.col(p->indices[5]).setZero();fj.col(p->indices[11]).setZero();}
 341                  Eigen::Matrix<double,6,1> fe;Mat F;
 342                  if(p->objective==ObjectiveMode::Cartesian) {
 343                      separated_task(filtered_pose,goal,fj,fe,F);
 344                  } else {
 345                      Eigen::Matrix<double,6,6> logJ;
 346                      pinocchio::Jlog6(goal.actInv(filtered_pose),logJ);
 347                      F=-logJ*fj;
 348                      fe=pinocchio::log6(filtered_pose.actInv(goal)).toVector();
 349                  }
 350                  double strength=p->filter_aware_strength;
 351                  if(p->filter_adaptive) {
 352                      const double lag=(filtered_pose.translation()-pose.translation()).norm();
 353                      const double excess=(filtered_pose.translation()-pos).norm()-(pose.translation()-pos).norm();
 354                      double gate=std::clamp(excess/(lag+1e-9),0.,1.);
 355                      if(p->gate_alpha<1.) {
 356                          gate=p->gates[k-1]+p->gate_alpha*(gate-p->gates[k-1]);
 357                          p->gates[k-1]=gate;
 358                      }
 359                      strength*=gate;
 360                  }
 361                  const double cost=wc*std::sqrt(strength);
 362                  Mat A=cost*filter_gain*F.topRows(3);
 363                  V3 b=cost*fe.head(3);
 364                  H.noalias()+=A.transpose()*A;
 365                  c.noalias()+=A.transpose()*b;
 366                  wc*=std::sqrt(1.-strength);
 367              }
 368              const double orientation_blend=(p->tuning.ankle_aware && (k==7 || k==8))?1.:p->orientation_strength;
 369              if(predict && orientation_blend>0. && (k==0 || k==9 || (p->tuning.ankle_aware && (k==7 || k==8)))) {
 370                  const auto& fp=p->filtered_data->oMf[id];
 371                  Eigen::Matrix<double,6,Eigen::Dynamic> fj(6,28);fj.setZero();
 372                  pinocchio::getFrameJacobian(p->model,*p->filtered_data,id,pinocchio::LOCAL,fj);
 373                  if(p->tuning.ankle_aware){fj.col(p->indices[5]).setZero();fj.col(p->indices[11]).setZero();}
 374                  Eigen::Matrix<double,6,6> logJ;
 375                  pinocchio::Jlog6(goal.actInv(fp),logJ);
 376                  Mat F=-logJ*fj;
 377                  const V3 fe=pinocchio::log6(fp.actInv(goal)).toVector().tail<3>();
 378                  const double cost=wr*std::sqrt(orientation_blend);
 379                  Mat A=cost*filter_gain*F.bottomRows(3);
 380                  V3 b=cost*fe;
 381                  H.noalias()+=A.transpose()*A;
 382                  c.noalias()+=A.transpose()*b;
 383                  wr*=std::sqrt(1.-orientation_blend);
 384              }
 385              J.topRows(3)*=wc;J.bottomRows(3)*=wr;e.head(3)*=wc;e.tail(3)*=wr;
 386              H.noalias()+=J.transpose()*J;
 387              c.noalias()+=J.transpose()*e;
 388          }
 389          if(p->arm_vector_scale>0.) {
 390              // Same world-frame limb vectors as the existing geometric mapping.
 391              // Both endpoints move with q; unlike a frozen absolute shoulder anchor.
 392              for(int side=0;side<2;++side) {
 393                  const std::array<pinocchio::FrameIndex,3> chain={
 394                      p->shoulder_ids[side],p->frame_ids[1+side],p->frame_ids[3+side]};
 395                  const std::array<int,3> source={16+side,18+side,20+side};
 396                  Eigen::Matrix<double,6,Eigen::Dynamic> ja(6,28),jb(6,28);
 397                  ja.setZero();
 398                  pinocchio::getFrameJacobian(p->model,*p->data,chain[0],pinocchio::LOCAL_WORLD_ALIGNED,ja);
 399                  for(int segment=0;segment<2;++segment) {
 400                      jb.setZero();
 401                      pinocchio::getFrameJacobian(p->model,*p->data,chain[segment+1],pinocchio::LOCAL_WORLD_ALIGNED,jb);
 402                      V3 e=(p->data->oMf[chain[segment+1]].translation()-p->data->oMf[chain[segment]].translation())
 403                           -(target[source[segment+1]]-target[source[segment]]);
 404                      const double cost=(segment==0?5.:10.)*p->arm_vector_scale;
 405                      Mat J=cost*(jb.topRows(3)-ja.topRows(3));
 406                      e*=cost;
 407                      H.noalias()+=J.transpose()*J;
 408                      c.noalias()+=J.transpose()*e;
 409                      ja=jb;
 410                  }
 411              }
 412          }
 413          // Keep total regularization curvature unchanged. Split .01 between
 414          // a fixed-reference prior (bias) and zero-increment damping (1-bias).
 415          // Only the former contributes a linear pull toward the reference pose.
 416          H.diagonal().array()+=.01;
 417          Vec prior=pinocchio::difference(p->model,p->ref,p->q);
 418          if(p->tuning.neck_posture_bias!=1.)for(int named:{25,26,27})prior[p->indices[named]]*=p->tuning.neck_posture_bias;
 419          if(p->shoulder_guard) {
 420              Vec gradient=(.01*p->posture_bias)*prior;
 421              for(int named:{15,16,17,20,21,22})gradient[p->indices[named]]=.01*prior[p->indices[named]];
 422              c+=gradient;
 423          } else c+=(.01*p->posture_bias)*prior;
 424          if(p->tuning.acceleration_cost>0. && p->has_previous_step) {
 425              H.diagonal().array()+=p->tuning.acceleration_cost;
 426              c.noalias()-=p->tuning.acceleration_cost*p->previous_step;
 427          }
 428          if(p->tuning.output_acceleration_cost>0. && output_acceleration_target) {
 429              // Exact weighted3 output acceleration proxy at 20Hz source timestamps.
 430              // Overwritten ankle pitch is not a controllable output variable.
 431              for(int named=0;named<28;++named)if(named!=5 && named!=11) {
 432                  const int index=p->indices[named];
 433                  const double residual=filter_gain*p->q[index]+(*filter_offset)[index]-(*output_acceleration_target)[named];
 434                  H(index,index)+=p->tuning.output_acceleration_cost*filter_gain*filter_gain;
 435                  c[index]+=p->tuning.output_acceleration_cost*filter_gain*residual;
 436              }
 437          }
 438          Vec lo=.5*pinocchio::difference(p->model,p->q,p->model.lowerPositionLimit);
 439          Vec hi=.5*pinocchio::difference(p->model,p->q,p->model.upperPositionLimit);
 440          if(iteration>0){lo=lo.cwiseMax(frame_low-p->q);hi=hi.cwiseMin(frame_high-p->q);}
 441          double kkt=0;Vec dq=solve_box_qp(H,c,lo,hi,&kkt);p->kkt=std::max(p->kkt,kkt);
 442          if(p->tuning.acceleration_cost>0.){p->previous_step=dq;p->has_previous_step=true;}
 443          Vec v=dq/.05;
 444          p->q=pinocchio::integrate(p->model,p->q,v*.05);p->update();
 445      }
 446      return p->q;
 447  }
 448  }

SHA256 bc1497d07b707831cf28afb9e882b13392d28105a056307ec606b12f474fae4b

   1  #include "core.hpp"
   2  #include "filter_prediction.hpp"
   3  #include <algorithm>
   4  #include <cmath>
   5  #include <stdexcept>
   6  namespace tw {
   7  static constexpr double pi=3.14159265358979323846;
   8  static V3 normalize(const V3& v){return v/(v.norm()+1e-6);}
   9  static double unwrap_delta(double delta) {
  10      double wrapped=std::fmod(delta+pi,2*pi);
  11      if(wrapped<0)wrapped+=2*pi;
  12      wrapped-=pi;
  13      if(wrapped==-pi && delta>0)wrapped=pi;
  14      return std::abs(delta)<pi?delta:wrapped;
  15  }
  16  Motion preprocess(const Motion& input,const V3& waist) {
  17      if(input.size()<9)throw std::runtime_error("At least 9 source frames required for Savitzky-Golay");
  18      const size_t joints=input[0].size();
  19      if(joints<22)throw std::runtime_error("At least 22 source joints required");
  20      Motion a=input;
  21      for(size_t i=0;i<a.size();++i) {
  22          if(input[i].size()!=joints)throw std::runtime_error("Inconsistent joint count");
  23          V3 pelvis(input[i][0][2],input[i][0][0],input[i][0][1]);
  24          for(size_t j=0;j<joints;++j) {
  25              if(!input[i][j].allFinite())throw std::runtime_error("Non-finite source");
  26              a[i][j]=(V3(input[i][j][2],input[i][j][0],input[i][j][1])-pelvis)*.24658347237104405+waist;
  27          }
  28      }
  29      V3 hip=a[0][2]-a[0][1];double ref=std::atan2(hip[1],hip[0]);
  30      for(auto& f:a) {
  31          hip=f[2]-f[1];double angle=ref-std::atan2(hip[1],hip[0]);
  32          M3 rot=Eigen::AngleAxisd(angle,V3::UnitZ()).toRotationMatrix();
  33          V3 root=f[0];
  34          for(auto& v:f)v=rot*(v-root)+root;
  35      }
  36      // Cached least-squares quadratic projection for the 9-sample window.
  37      static const Mat weights=[](){
  38          Mat design(9,3);
  39          for(int i=0;i<9;++i)design.row(i)<<1.,double(i),double(i*i);
  40          Mat inverse=design.colPivHouseholderQr().solve(Mat::Identity(9,9));
  41          return Mat(design*inverse);
  42      }();
  43      Motion smooth=a;
  44      for(size_t i=0;i<a.size();++i) {
  45          int start=std::clamp(int(i)-4,0,int(a.size())-9), row=int(i)-start;
  46          for(size_t j=0;j<joints;++j) {
  47              V3 sum=V3::Zero();
  48              for(int k=0;k<9;++k)sum+=weights(row,k)*a[start+k][j];
  49              smooth[i][j]=sum;
  50          }
  51      }
  52      return smooth;
  53  }
  54  std::vector<M3> preprocess_head(const Motion& input,const std::vector<M3>& heads) {
  55      if(heads.empty())return {};
  56      if(heads.size()!=input.size())throw std::runtime_error("Head rotation length mismatch");
  57      M3 rot;rot<<0,0,1,1,0,0,0,1,0;
  58      std::vector<V3> vectors;
  59      double displacement=0.;
  60      for(size_t i=0;i<heads.size();++i) {
  61          if(!heads[i].allFinite() || (heads[i].transpose()*heads[i]-M3::Identity()).norm()>1e-5 ||
  62             std::abs(heads[i].determinant()-1)>1e-5)
  63              throw std::runtime_error("Head rotation must be a finite SO(3) matrix");
  64          Eigen::Quaterniond q(rot*heads[i]*rot.transpose());q.normalize();
  65          if(q.w()<0)q.coeffs()*=-1;
  66          double n=q.vec().norm();V3 v=n<1e-15?2*q.vec():V3(q.vec()*(2*std::atan2(n,q.w())/n));
  67          vectors.push_back(v);
  68          if(i)displacement=std::max(displacement,(input[i][0]-input[i-1][0]).norm());
  69      }
  70      V3 prev=vectors[0];
  71      for(size_t i=1;i<vectors.size();++i) {
  72          V3 raw=vectors[i];
  73          for(int j=0;j<3;++j)vectors[i][j]=vectors[i-1][j]+unwrap_delta(raw[j]-prev[j]);
  74          prev=raw;
  75      }
  76      std::vector<M3> result;
  77      for(auto v:vectors) {
  78          if(displacement>.05)v[2]=0;
  79          double angle=v.norm();
  80          result.push_back(angle<1e-15?M3::Identity():Eigen::AngleAxisd(angle,v/angle).toRotationMatrix());
  81      }
  82      return result;
  83  }
  84  Mat cubic_resample(const Mat& y,int count) {
  85      int n=y.rows(),d=y.cols();
  86      if(n<4 || count<2)throw std::runtime_error("Cubic interpolation requires >=4 samples");
  87      // Not-a-knot cubic spline, like scipy interp1d(kind='cubic').
  88      int k=n-2;
  89      Vec diag=Vec::Constant(k,4.),upper=Vec::Ones(k-1),lower=Vec::Ones(k-1);
  90      diag[0]=diag[k-1]=6.;upper[0]=0.;lower[k-2]=0.;
  91      Mat rhs(k,d);
  92      for(int i=0;i<k;++i)rhs.row(i)=6*(y.row(i+2)-2*y.row(i+1)+y.row(i));
  93      for(int i=1;i<k;++i) {
  94          double f=lower[i-1]/diag[i-1];diag[i]-=f*upper[i-1];rhs.row(i)-=f*rhs.row(i-1);
  95      }
  96      Mat second=Mat::Zero(n,d);second.row(n-2)=rhs.row(k-1)/diag[k-1];
  97      for(int i=k-2;i>=0;--i)second.row(i+1)=(rhs.row(i)-upper[i]*second.row(i+2))/diag[i];
  98      second.row(0)=2*second.row(1)-second.row(2);
  99      second.row(n-1)=2*second.row(n-2)-second.row(n-3);
 100      Mat out(count,d);
 101      for(int i=0;i<count;++i) {
 102          double x=double(i)*(n-1)/(count-1);int j=std::min(int(x),n-2);double u=x-j,v=1-u;
 103          out.row(i)=v*y.row(j)+u*y.row(j+1)+((v*v*v-v)*second.row(j)+(u*u*u-u)*second.row(j+1))/6.;
 104      }
 105      return out;
 106  }
 107  void Retargeter::reset_postprocess() {
 108      history.clear();previous_filtered.resize(0);previous_unwrapped.resize(0);previous_dof.resize(0);
 109      previous_previous_dof.resize(0);
 110      has_filter=has_ankle=has_previous=false;source_count=0;next_time=0;
 111  }
 112  void Retargeter::reset(){robot.reset();reset_postprocess();}
 113  void Retargeter::seed(const Vec& q){robot.seed(q);reset_postprocess();}
 114  Vec Retargeter::filter(const Vec& q,bool batch) {
 115      history.push_back(q);if(history.size()>3)history.pop_front();
 116      Vec filtered=Vec::Zero(q.size());
 117      for(int i=0;i<3;++i) {
 118          int j=std::max(0,i-(3-int(history.size())));
 119          if(batch)filtered+=(double(i+1)/6.)*history[j];
 120          else filtered+=double(i+1)*history[j];
 121      }
 122      if(!batch)filtered/=6.;
 123  
 124      Vec unwrapped=filtered;
 125      if(has_filter)
 126          for(int j=0;j<q.size();++j)
 127              unwrapped[j]=previous_unwrapped[j]+unwrap_delta(filtered[j]-previous_filtered[j]);
 128      previous_filtered=filtered;previous_unwrapped=unwrapped;has_filter=true;
 129      return unwrapped;
 130  }
 131  Vec Retargeter::postprocess(const Vec& q,const Frame& f,bool clip) {
 132      Vec dof=robot.named(q);
 133      for(int side=0;side<2;++side) {
 134          double angle=std::acos(std::clamp(normalize(f[7+side]-f[4+side]).dot(
 135                                        normalize(f[10+side]-f[7+side])),-1.,1.));
 136          if(!has_ankle)ankle_neutral[side]=angle;
 137          dof[side?11:5]=std::clamp(angle-ankle_neutral[side],double(-.79f),double(.79f));
 138      }
 139      has_ankle=true;
 140      return clip?robot.clamp(dof):dof;
 141  }
 142  Output Retargeter::process(const Motion& input,bool batch,const std::vector<M3>& heads) {
 143  
 144      if(filter_aware && batch)throw std::runtime_error("Filter-aware mode currently supports stream only");
 145      Output out;out.preprocessed=preprocess(input,robot.waist());
 146      auto rotations=preprocess_head(input,heads);
 147      if(batch){reset_postprocess();robot.clear_targets();}
 148      std::vector<Vec> values;
 149      out.raw.resize(input.size(),28);
 150      for(size_t i=0;i<input.size();++i) {
 151          const auto& source=out.preprocessed[i];
 152          Frame targets=robot.targets(source);out.targets.push_back(targets);
 153          Vec raw;
 154          std::array<double,2> override_angles{};
 155          if(ankle_aware)for(int side=0;side<2;++side) {
 156              double angle=std::acos(std::clamp(normalize(source[7+side]-source[4+side]).dot(
 157                                            normalize(source[10+side]-source[7+side])),-1.,1.));
 158              const double neutral=has_ankle?ankle_neutral[side]:angle;
 159              override_angles[side]=std::clamp(angle-neutral,double(-.79f),double(.79f));
 160          }
 161          if(filter_aware) {
 162              auto prediction=weighted3_prediction(history,28);
 163              Vec acceleration_target;
 164              if(track_output_acceleration && previous_previous_dof.size()==28)acceleration_target=2.*previous_dof-previous_previous_dof;
 165              raw=robot.solve(source,targets,rotations.empty()?nullptr:&rotations[i],
 166                              &prediction.offset,prediction.gain,ankle_aware?&override_angles:nullptr,
 167                              acceleration_target.size()==28?&acceleration_target:nullptr);
 168          } else {
 169              raw=robot.solve(source,targets,rotations.empty()?nullptr:&rotations[i]);
 170          }
 171          out.raw.row(i)=raw.transpose();
 172          Vec dof=postprocess(filter(raw,batch),source,!batch);
 173          if(batch) {values.push_back(dof*(180./pi));continue;}
 174          double current_time=source_count/20.;++source_count;
 175          if(!has_previous) {
 176              previous_dof=dof;next_time=current_time+1./90.;has_previous=true;
 177              values.push_back(dof);continue;
 178          }
 179          double previous_time=(source_count-2)/20.;
 180          while(next_time<=current_time+1e-12) {
 181              double ratio=(next_time-previous_time)/(current_time-previous_time);
 182              values.push_back(previous_dof+ratio*(dof-previous_dof));
 183              next_time+=1./90.;
 184          }
 185          if(track_output_acceleration)previous_previous_dof=previous_dof;
 186          previous_dof=dof;
 187      }
 188      Mat dofs(values.size(),28);
 189      for(size_t i=0;i<values.size();++i)dofs.row(i)=values[i].transpose();
 190      if(batch) {
 191          int count=int((input.size()-1)*4.5)+1;
 192          dofs=cubic_resample(dofs,count);
 193          for(int i=0;i<dofs.rows();++i)dofs.row(i)=robot.clamp_degrees(dofs.row(i).transpose()).transpose();
 194          dofs*=pi/180.;
 195      }
 196      out.dof=std::move(dofs);out.max_kkt=robot.max_kkt();
 197      if(!out.dof.allFinite())throw std::runtime_error("Non-finite output");
 198      return out;
 199  }
 200  }

  24              if(arg=="--posture-shoulder-guard"){shoulder_guard=true;continue;}
  25              if(arg=="--continuous"){continuous=true;continue;}
  26              if(i+1>=argc)throw std::runtime_error("Missing value for "+arg);
  27              std::string value=argv[++i];
  28              if(arg=="--urdf")urdf=value;else if(arg=="--input")input=value;
  29              else if(arg=="--list")list=value;else if(arg=="--out")output=value;
  30              else if(arg=="--head")head=value;else if(arg=="--repeat")repeat=std::stoi(value);
  31              else if(arg=="--iterations")iterations=std::stoi(value);
  32  
  33              else if(arg=="--neck-posture-bias") {
  34                  size_t used=0;tuning.neck_posture_bias=std::stod(value,&used);
  35                  if(used!=value.size())throw std::runtime_error("Invalid neck posture bias");
  36              }
  37              else if(arg=="--output-acceleration-cost") {
  38                  size_t used=0;tuning.output_acceleration_cost=std::stod(value,&used);
  39                  if(used!=value.size())throw std::runtime_error("Invalid output acceleration cost");
  40              }
  41              else if(arg=="--mean-distance-mm") {
  42                  size_t used=0;tuning.mean_distance_epsilon=std::stod(value,&used)/1000.;
  43                  if(used!=value.size())throw std::runtime_error("Invalid mean distance epsilon");
  44              }
  45              else if(arg=="--acceleration-cost") {
  46                  size_t used=0;tuning.acceleration_cost=std::stod(value,&used);
  47                  if(used!=value.size())throw std::runtime_error("Invalid acceleration cost");
  48              }
  49              else if(arg=="--position-scale" || arg=="--midpoint-scale") {
  50                  size_t used=0;double v=std::stod(value,&used);
  51                  if(used!=value.size())throw std::runtime_error("Invalid task scale");
  52                  if(arg=="--position-scale")tuning.position_scale=v;else tuning.midpoint_scale=v;
  53              }
  54              else if(arg=="--posture-bias") {
  55                  size_t used=0;posture_bias=std::stod(value,&used);
  56                  if(used!=value.size())throw std::runtime_error("Invalid posture bias");
  57              }
  58              else if(arg=="--filter-orientation-strength") {
  59                  size_t used=0;orientation_strength=std::stod(value,&used);
  60                  if(used!=value.size())throw std::runtime_error("Invalid orientation strength");
  61              }
  62              else if(arg=="--filter-gate-alpha") {
  63                  size_t used=0;gate_alpha=std::stod(value,&used);
  64                  if(used!=value.size())throw std::runtime_error("Invalid gate alpha");
  65              }
  66              else if(arg=="--filter-aware-strength") {
  67                  size_t used=0;double strength=std::stod(value,&used);
  68                  if(used!=value.size() || !std::isfinite(strength) || strength<0. || strength>1.)
  69                      throw std::runtime_error("Filter-aware strength must be in [0,1]");
  70                  filter_aware_strength=strength;
  71              }
  72              else if(arg=="--arm-vector-scale") {
  73                  size_t used=0;double scale=std::stod(value,&used);
  74                  if(used!=value.size() || !std::isfinite(scale) || scale<0. || scale>10.)
  75                      throw std::runtime_error("Arm vector scale must be in [0,10]");
  76                  arm_vector_scale=scale;
  77              }
  78              else if(arg=="--distance-epsilon-mm") {
  79                  size_t used=0;double mm=std::stod(value,&used);
  80                  if(used!=value.size() || !std::isfinite(mm) || mm<=0. || mm>1000.)
  81                      throw std::runtime_error("Distance epsilon must be in (0,1000] mm");
  82                  distance_epsilon_m=mm/1000.;
  83                  if(distance_epsilon_m<=0.)throw std::runtime_error("Distance epsilon underflow");
  84              }
  85              else if(arg=="--objective") {
  86                  if(value!="se3" && value!="cartesian")throw std::runtime_error("Invalid objective");
  87                  objective=value;
  88              }
  89              else if(arg=="--mode") {if(value!="stream"&&value!="batch")throw std::runtime_error("Invalid mode");batch=value=="batch";}
  90              else throw std::runtime_error("Unknown option "+arg);
  91          }
  92          if(urdf.empty() || output.empty() || (input.empty()==list.empty()) || repeat<1 ||
  93             (continuous && batch) || (!head.empty() && input.empty()))
  94              throw std::runtime_error("Usage: tw_retarget --urdf model.urdf (--input file.npy | --list paths.txt) --out directory [--mode stream|batch] [--repeat N] [--trace] [--head rotations.npy] [--continuous]");
  95          std::vector<std::string> paths;
  96          if(!input.empty())paths.push_back(input);
  97          else {std::ifstream f(list);if(!f)throw std::runtime_error("Cannot open list");
  98              std::string line;while(std::getline(f,line)){if(!line.empty()&&line.back()=='\r')line.pop_back();if(!line.empty())paths.push_back(line);}}
  99          if(paths.empty())throw std::runtime_error("Empty input list");
 100          std::vector<tw::Motion> motions;
 101          for(auto& path:paths)motions.push_back(tw::load_motion(tw::read_npy(path)));
 102          std::vector<tw::M3> heads;
 103          if(!head.empty()) {
 104              auto a=tw::read_npy(head);
 105              if(a.shape!=std::vector<size_t>{motions[0].size(),3,3})throw std::runtime_error("Expected head [T,3,3]");
 106              for(size_t i=0;i<a.shape[0];++i){tw::M3 m;for(int j=0;j<3;++j)for(int k=0;k<3;++k)m(j,k)=a.data[i*9+j*3+k];heads.push_back(m);}
 107          }
 108          // Prevent output names from overwriting any source input, including symlinks.
 109          std::set<std::string> reserved,stems;
 110          for(const auto& path:paths)reserved.insert(fs::weakly_canonical(path).string());
 111          if(!head.empty())reserved.insert(fs::weakly_canonical(head).string());
 112          for(const auto& path:paths) {
 113              auto stem=fs::path(path).stem().string();
 114              if(!stems.insert(stem).second)throw std::runtime_error("Duplicate input stem: "+stem);
 115              std::vector<std::string> suffixes={".npy"};
 116              if(trace){suffixes.push_back("_raw.npy");suffixes.push_back("_pre.npy");suffixes.push_back("_targets.npy");}
 117              for(const auto& suffix:suffixes)
 118                  if(reserved.count(fs::weakly_canonical(fs::path(output)/(stem+suffix)).string()))
 119                      throw std::runtime_error("Refusing to overwrite a source input");
 120          }
 121          tw::Retargeter engine(urdf,iterations,objective=="cartesian"?tw::ObjectiveMode::Cartesian:tw::ObjectiveMode::SE3,
 122                               distance_epsilon_m,arm_vector_scale,filter_aware_strength,filter_adaptive,gate_alpha,orientation_strength,posture_bias,shoulder_guard,tuning);fs::create_directories(output);
 123          std::vector<std::vector<double>> timings(repeat,std::vector<double>(paths.size()));
 124          double kkt=0;size_t frames=0;
 125          for(auto& m:motions)frames+=m.size();
 126          // Warm-up outside all measured intervals.
 127          engine.reset();engine.process(motions[0],batch,heads);
 128          for(int r=0;r<repeat;++r) {
 129              engine.reset();
 130              for(size_t i=0;i<paths.size();++i) {
 131                  if(!continuous)engine.reset();
 132                  auto start=std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
 133                  auto result=engine.process(motions[i],batch,heads);
 134                  timings[r][i]=std::chrono::duration<double,std::milli>(std::chrono::steady_clock::now()-start).count();
 135                  kkt=std::max(kkt,result.max_kkt);
 136                  if(r==repeat-1) {
 137                      std::string stem=fs::path(paths[i]).stem().string();
 138                      tw::write_matrix((fs::path(output)/(stem+".npy")).string(),result.dof);
 139                      if(trace) {
 140                          tw::write_matrix((fs::path(output)/(stem+"_raw.npy")).string(),result.raw);
 141                          tw::write_motion((fs::path(output)/(stem+"_pre.npy")).string(),result.preprocessed);
 142                          tw::write_motion((fs::path(output)/(stem+"_targets.npy")).string(),result.targets);
 143                      }
 144                  }
 145              }
 146          }
 147          std::ostringstream report;report<<std::setprecision(17)<<"{\"ik_iterations\":"<<iterations<<",\"input_frames\":"<<frames<<",\"motions\":"<<paths.size()<<",\"max_kkt\":"<<kkt<<",\"ms_per_input_frame\":[";
 148          for(int r=0;r<repeat;++r){double total=0;for(auto x:timings[r])total+=x;if(r)report<<",";report<<total/frames;}
 149          report<<"],\"objective\":\""<<objective<<"\",\"distance_epsilon_mm\":"<<distance_epsilon_m*1000.
 150                <<",\"arm_vector_scale\":"<<arm_vector_scale<<",\"filter_aware_strength\":"<<filter_aware_strength<<"}";
 151          std::ofstream log(fs::path(output)/"timing.json");log<<report.str()<<"\n";if(!log)throw std::runtime_error("Cannot write timing report");
 152          std::cout<<report.str()<<std::endl;
 153          return 0;
 154      } catch(const std::exception& e){std::cerr<<"ERROR: "<<e.what()<<std::endl;return 1;}
 155  }

 101  import isaaclab_tasks  # noqa: F401
 102  from isaaclab.envs import (
 103      DirectMARLEnv,
 104      DirectMARLEnvCfg,
 105      DirectRLEnvCfg,
 106      ManagerBasedRLEnvCfg,
 107      multi_agent_to_single_agent,

 111  from isaaclab_rl.rsl_rl import RslRlOnPolicyRunnerCfg, RslRlVecEnvWrapper, handle_deprecated_rsl_rl_cfg
 112  from isaaclab_tasks.utils import get_checkpoint_path

 118  torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = True
 119  torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = True
 120  torch.backends.cudnn.deterministic = False
 121  torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False
 122  
 123  
 124  @hydra_task_config(args_cli.task, "rsl_rl_cfg_entry_point")
 125  def main(env_cfg: ManagerBasedRLEnvCfg | DirectRLEnvCfg | DirectMARLEnvCfg, agent_cfg: RslRlOnPolicyRunnerCfg):
 126      """Train with RSL-RL agent."""
 127      # override configurations with non-hydra CLI arguments
 128      agent_cfg = cli_args.update_rsl_rl_cfg(agent_cfg, args_cli)
 129      env_cfg.scene.num_envs = args_cli.num_envs if args_cli.num_envs is not None else env_cfg.scene.num_envs
 130      agent_cfg.max_iterations = (
 131          args_cli.max_iterations if args_cli.max_iterations is not None else agent_cfg.max_iterations
 132      )
 133      if args_cli.save_interval is not None:
 134          agent_cfg.save_interval = args_cli.save_interval
 135  
 136      # Convert legacy policy configs to the actor/critic format expected by newer rsl-rl releases.
 137      agent_cfg = handle_deprecated_rsl_rl_cfg(agent_cfg, installed_version)
 138  
 139      # set the environment seed
 140      # note: certain randomizations occur in the environment initialization so we set the seed here
 141      env_cfg.seed = agent_cfg.seed
 142      env_cfg.sim.device = args_cli.device if args_cli.device is not None else env_cfg.sim.device
 143  
 144      # multi-gpu training configuration
 145      if args_cli.distributed:
 146          env_cfg.sim.device = f"cuda:{app_launcher.local_rank}"
 147          agent_cfg.device = f"cuda:{app_launcher.local_rank}"
 148  
 149          # set seed to have diversity in different threads
 150          seed = agent_cfg.seed + app_launcher.local_rank
 151          env_cfg.seed = seed
 152          agent_cfg.seed = seed
 153  
 154      # specify directory for logging experiments
 155      log_root_path = os.path.join("logs", "rsl_rl", agent_cfg.experiment_name)
 156      log_root_path = os.path.abspath(log_root_path)
 157      print(f"[INFO] Logging experiment in directory: {log_root_path}")
 158      # specify directory for logging runs: {time-stamp}_{run_name}
 159      log_dir = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d_%H-%M-%S")
 160      # This way, the Ray Tune workflow can extract experiment name.
 161      print(f"Exact experiment name requested from command line: {log_dir}")
 162      if agent_cfg.run_name:
 163          log_dir += f"_{agent_cfg.run_name}"
 164      log_dir = os.path.join(log_root_path, log_dir)
 165  
 166      # create isaac environment
 167      env = gym.make(args_cli.task, cfg=env_cfg, render_mode="rgb_array" if args_cli.video else None)
 168  
 169      # convert to single-agent instance if required by the RL algorithm
 170      if isinstance(env.unwrapped, DirectMARLEnv):
 171          env = multi_agent_to_single_agent(env)
 172  
 173      # save resume path before creating a new log_dir
 174      if agent_cfg.resume or agent_cfg.algorithm.class_name == "Distillation":
 175          resume_path = get_checkpoint_path(log_root_path, agent_cfg.load_run, agent_cfg.load_checkpoint)
 176  
 177      # wrap for video recording
 178      if args_cli.video:
 179          video_kwargs = {
 180              "video_folder": os.path.join(log_dir, "videos", "train"),
 181              "step_trigger": lambda step: step % args_cli.video_interval == 0,
 182              "video_length": args_cli.video_length,
 183              "disable_logger": True,
 184          }
 185          print("[INFO] Recording videos during training.")
 186          print_dict(video_kwargs, nesting=4)
 187          env = gym.wrappers.RecordVideo(env, **video_kwargs)
 188  
 189      # wrap around environment for rsl-rl
 190      env = RslRlVecEnvWrapper(env, clip_actions=agent_cfg.clip_actions)
 191  
 192      # create runner from rsl-rl
 193      runner = OnPolicyRunner(env, agent_cfg.to_dict(), log_dir=log_dir, device=agent_cfg.device)
 194      # write git state to logs
 195      runner.add_git_repo_to_log(__file__)
 196      # load the checkpoint
 197      if agent_cfg.resume or agent_cfg.algorithm.class_name == "Distillation":
 198          print(f"[INFO]: Loading model checkpoint from: {resume_path}")
 199          # load previously trained model
 200          runner.load(resume_path)
 201  
 202      # dump the configuration into log-directory
 203      dump_yaml(os.path.join(log_dir, "params", "env.yaml"), env_cfg)
 204      dump_yaml(os.path.join(log_dir, "params", "agent.yaml"), agent_cfg)
 205      export_deploy_cfg(env.unwrapped, log_dir)
 206      from control_runtime import save as save_control_runtime
 207      save_control_runtime(env_cfg, log_dir)
 208      # copy the environment configuration file to the log directory
 209      shutil.copy(
 210          inspect.getfile(env_cfg.__class__),
 211          os.path.join(log_dir, "params", os.path.basename(inspect.getfile(env_cfg.__class__))),
 212      )
 213  
 214      # run training
 215      runner.learn(num_learning_iterations=agent_cfg.max_iterations, init_at_random_ep_len=True)
 216  
 217      # close the simulator
 218      env.close()
 219  
 220  
 221  if __name__ == "__main__":
 222      # run the main function
 223      main()
 224      # close sim app
 225      simulation_app.close()

   6  """Script to play a checkpoint if an RL agent from RSL-RL."""

   8  """Launch Isaac Sim Simulator first."""

  81  """Rest everything follows."""
  82  
  83  import gymnasium as gym
  84  import os
  85  import time
  86  import torch
  87  
  88  from rsl_rl.runners import OnPolicyRunner
  89  
  90  import isaaclab_tasks  # noqa: F401
  91  from isaaclab.envs import DirectMARLEnv, multi_agent_to_single_agent
  92  from isaaclab.utils.assets import retrieve_file_path
  93  from isaaclab.utils.dict import print_dict
  94  from isaaclab_rl.rsl_rl import (
  95      RslRlOnPolicyRunnerCfg,
  96      RslRlVecEnvWrapper,
  97      export_policy_as_jit,
  98      export_policy_as_onnx,
  99      handle_deprecated_rsl_rl_cfg,
 100      handle_deprecated_rsl_rl_checkpoint,
 101  )
 102  from isaaclab_rl.utils.pretrained_checkpoint import get_published_pretrained_checkpoint
 103  from isaaclab_tasks.utils import get_checkpoint_path
 104  
 105  import unitree_rl_lab.tasks  # noqa: F401
 106  from unitree_rl_lab.utils.parser_cfg import parse_env_cfg
 107  
 108  
 109  def main():
 110      """Play with RSL-RL agent."""
 111      # parse configuration
 112      env_cfg = parse_env_cfg(
 113          args_cli.task,
 114          device=args_cli.device,
 115          num_envs=args_cli.num_envs,
 116          use_fabric=not args_cli.disable_fabric,
 117          entry_point_key="play_env_cfg_entry_point",
 118      )
 119      if args_cli.disable_observation_noise:
 120          env_cfg.observations.policy.enable_corruption = False
 121      if args_cli.seed is not None:
 122          env_cfg.seed = args_cli.seed
 123      if args_cli.viewer_eye is not None:
 124          env_cfg.viewer.eye = tuple(args_cli.viewer_eye)
 125      if args_cli.viewer_lookat is not None:
 126          env_cfg.viewer.lookat = tuple(args_cli.viewer_lookat)
 127      if args_cli.viewer_follow_asset is not None:
 128          env_cfg.viewer.origin_type = "world"
 129          env_cfg.viewer.asset_name = args_cli.viewer_follow_asset
 130          env_cfg.viewer.env_index = 0
 131      if args_cli.evaluation_motion_id is not None:
 132          env_cfg.commands.motion.adaptive_max_probability = None
 133          env_cfg.commands.motion.adaptive_uniform_ratio = 1.0
 134      agent_cfg: RslRlOnPolicyRunnerCfg = cli_args.parse_rsl_rl_cfg(args_cli.task, args_cli)
 135      agent_cfg = handle_deprecated_rsl_rl_cfg(agent_cfg, installed_version)
 136  
 137      # specify directory for logging experiments
 138      log_root_path = os.path.join("logs", "rsl_rl", agent_cfg.experiment_name)
 139      log_root_path = os.path.abspath(log_root_path)
 140      print(f"[INFO] Loading experiment from directory: {log_root_path}")
 141      if args_cli.use_pretrained_checkpoint:
 142          resume_path = get_published_pretrained_checkpoint("rsl_rl", args_cli.task)
 143          if not resume_path:
 144              print("[INFO] Unfortunately a pre-trained checkpoint is currently unavailable for this task.")
 145              return
 146      elif args_cli.checkpoint:
 147          resume_path = retrieve_file_path(args_cli.checkpoint)
 148      else:
 149          resume_path = get_checkpoint_path(log_root_path, agent_cfg.load_run, agent_cfg.load_checkpoint)
 150      resume_path = handle_deprecated_rsl_rl_checkpoint(resume_path, installed_version)
 151  
 152      from control_runtime import restore as restore_control_runtime, snapshot as control_snapshot
 153      restore_control_runtime(env_cfg, resume_path)
 154      log_dir = os.path.dirname(resume_path)
 155  
 156      # create isaac environment
 157      env = gym.make(args_cli.task, cfg=env_cfg, render_mode="rgb_array" if args_cli.video else None)
 158  
 159      # convert to single-agent instance if required by the RL algorithm
 160      if isinstance(env.unwrapped, DirectMARLEnv):
 161          env = multi_agent_to_single_agent(env)
 162  
 163      if args_cli.evaluation_motion_id is not None:
 164          env.unwrapped.command_manager.get_term("motion").set_evaluation_motion_ids(
 165              [args_cli.evaluation_motion_id] * env.unwrapped.num_envs
 166          )
 167      # wrap for video recording
 168      if args_cli.video:
 169          video_kwargs = {
 170              "video_folder": os.path.join(log_dir, "videos", "play"),
 171              "step_trigger": lambda step: step == 0,
 172              "video_length": args_cli.video_length,
 173              "disable_logger": True,
 174          }
 175          print("[INFO] Recording videos during training.")
 176          print_dict(video_kwargs, nesting=4)
 177          env = gym.wrappers.RecordVideo(env, **video_kwargs)
 178  
 179      # wrap around environment for rsl-rl
 180      env = RslRlVecEnvWrapper(env, clip_actions=agent_cfg.clip_actions)
 181  
 182      print(f"[INFO]: Loading model checkpoint from: {resume_path}")
 183      # load previously trained model
 184      if not hasattr(agent_cfg, "class_name") or agent_cfg.class_name == "OnPolicyRunner":
 185          runner = OnPolicyRunner(env, agent_cfg.to_dict(), log_dir=None, device=agent_cfg.device)
 186      elif agent_cfg.class_name == "DistillationRunner":
 187          from rsl_rl.runners import DistillationRunner
 188  
 189          runner = DistillationRunner(env, agent_cfg.to_dict(), log_dir=None, device=agent_cfg.device)
 190      else:
 191          raise ValueError(f"Unsupported runner class: {agent_cfg.class_name}")
 192      runner.load(resume_path)
 193  
 194      # obtain the trained policy for inference
 195      policy = runner.get_inference_policy(device=env.unwrapped.device)
 196  
 197      export_model_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(resume_path), "exported")
 198  
 199      if packaging_version.parse(version("rsl-rl-lib")) >= packaging_version.parse("4.0.0"):
 200          runner.export_policy_to_jit(path=export_model_dir, filename="policy.pt")
 201          runner.export_policy_to_onnx(path=export_model_dir, filename="policy.onnx")
 202          policy_nn = None
 203      else:
 204          # extract the neural network module
 205          # we do this in a try-except to maintain backwards compatibility.
 206          try:
 207              # version 2.3 onwards
 208              policy_nn = runner.alg.policy
 209          except AttributeError:
 210              # version 2.2 and below
 211              policy_nn = runner.alg.actor_critic
 212  
 213          # extract the normalizer
 214          if hasattr(policy_nn, "actor_obs_normalizer"):
 215              normalizer = policy_nn.actor_obs_normalizer
 216          elif hasattr(policy_nn, "student_obs_normalizer"):
 217              normalizer = policy_nn.student_obs_normalizer
 218          else:
 219              normalizer = None
 220  
 221          # export policy to onnx/jit
 222          export_policy_as_jit(policy_nn, normalizer=normalizer, path=export_model_dir, filename="policy.pt")
 223          export_policy_as_onnx(policy_nn, normalizer=normalizer, path=export_model_dir, filename="policy.onnx")
 224  
 225      dt = env.unwrapped.step_dt
 226  
 227      # reset environment
 228      obs = env.get_observations()
 229      if version("rsl-rl-lib").startswith("2.3."):
 230          obs, _ = env.get_observations()
 231      timestep = 0
 232      evaluation = {"steps": 0, "termination_counts": {}, "motion_metrics": {}}
 233      telemetry = None
 234      if args_cli.telemetry_output:
 235          from mimic_telemetry import MimicTelemetry
 236          telemetry = MimicTelemetry(env.unwrapped, args_cli.telemetry_output)
 237      camera_trace = []
 238      camera_follow = None
 239      if args_cli.viewer_follow_asset is not None:
 240          from camera_follow import SmoothCameraFollow
 241          camera_asset = env.unwrapped.scene[args_cli.viewer_follow_asset]
 242          camera_follow = SmoothCameraFollow(camera_asset.data.root_pos_w[0].cpu().numpy(), dt, args_cli.viewer_follow_tau)
 243          camera_reset = False
 244      # simulate environment
 245      while simulation_app.is_running():
 246          start_time = time.time()
 247          if camera_follow is not None:
 248              origin = camera_follow.update(camera_asset.data.root_pos_w[0].cpu().numpy(), reset=camera_reset)
 249              env.unwrapped.sim.set_camera_view(eye=origin + env_cfg.viewer.eye, target=origin + env_cfg.viewer.lookat)
 250              if args_cli.evaluation_output:
 251                  camera_trace.append((origin.copy(), camera_asset.data.root_pos_w[0].cpu().numpy().copy(), camera_reset))
 252          # run everything in inference mode
 253          with torch.inference_mode():
 254              # agent stepping
 255              actions = policy(obs)
 256              if telemetry is not None:
 257                  telemetry.begin(actions)
 258              # env stepping
 259              obs, _, dones, _ = env.step(actions)
 260              if camera_follow is not None:
 261                  camera_reset = bool(dones[0].item())
 262              if telemetry is not None:
 263                  telemetry.end(dones)
 264              if args_cli.evaluation_output:
 265                  evaluation["steps"] += 1
 266                  manager = env.unwrapped.termination_manager
 267                  for name in manager.active_terms:
 268                      count = int(manager.get_term(name).sum().item())
 269                      evaluation["termination_counts"][name] = evaluation["termination_counts"].get(name, 0) + count
 270                  for name, value in env.unwrapped.command_manager.get_term("motion").metrics.items():
 271                      if name.startswith("error_"):
 272                          evaluation["motion_metrics"][name] = evaluation["motion_metrics"].get(name, 0.0) + float(value.mean().item())
 273              if packaging_version.parse(version("rsl-rl-lib")) >= packaging_version.parse("4.0.0"):
 274                  policy.reset(dones)
 275              elif policy_nn is not None:
 276                  policy_nn.reset(dones)
 277          if args_cli.video or args_cli.evaluation_steps:
 278              timestep += 1
 279              # Exit the play loop after recording one video
 280              if timestep == (args_cli.evaluation_steps or args_cli.video_length):
 281                  break
 282  
 283          # time delay for real-time evaluation
 284          sleep_time = dt - (time.time() - start_time)
 285          if args_cli.real_time and sleep_time > 0:
 286              time.sleep(sleep_time)
 287  
 288      if telemetry is not None:
 289          telemetry.save()
 290      if args_cli.evaluation_output:
 291          import json
 292          from pathlib import Path
 293          if camera_trace:
 294              import numpy as np
 295              np.savez_compressed(str(args_cli.evaluation_output) + ".camera.npz", camera_origin=np.array([x[0] for x in camera_trace]), robot_origin=np.array([x[1] for x in camera_trace]), reset=np.array([x[2] for x in camera_trace]), dt=dt)
 296          steps = max(evaluation["steps"], 1)
 297          evaluation["motion_metrics"] = {name: value / steps for name, value in evaluation["motion_metrics"].items()}
 298          evaluation.update(control_runtime=control_snapshot(env_cfg), camera_follow_tau=args_cli.viewer_follow_tau, checkpoint=resume_path, motion_id=args_cli.evaluation_motion_id,
 299                            seconds=evaluation["steps"] * dt, num_envs=env.unwrapped.num_envs)
 300          Path(args_cli.evaluation_output).write_text(json.dumps(evaluation, indent=2) + "\n")
 301      # close the simulator
 302      env.close()
 303  
 304  
 305  if __name__ == "__main__":
 306      # run the main function
 307      main()
 308      # close sim app
 309      simulation_app.close()

   1  """Persist custom action/actuator settings alongside each checkpoint run."""
   2  import json
   3  import os
   4  from pathlib import Path
   5  
   6  
   7  def snapshot(cfg):
   8      action=getattr(cfg.actions,'JointPositionAction',None)
   9      if action is None or not hasattr(action,'smoothing_tau'): return None
  10      return dict(version=1,target_velocity_scale=action.target_velocity_scale,smoothing_tau=action.smoothing_tau,limit_target_velocity=action.limit_target_velocity,
  11          actuators={name:dict(stiffness=a.stiffness,damping=a.damping) for name,a in cfg.scene.robot.actuators.items()})
  12  
  13  
  14  def save(cfg,run_dir):
  15      payload=snapshot(cfg)
  16      if payload is not None:
  17          path=Path(run_dir)/'params/control_runtime.json';path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
  18          path.write_text(json.dumps(payload,indent=2)+'\n')
  19  
  20  
  21  def restore(cfg,checkpoint):
  22      path=Path(checkpoint).parent/'params/control_runtime.json'
  23      if not path.exists(): return
  24      payload=json.loads(path.read_text())
  25      if payload['version']!=1: raise ValueError('Unsupported control runtime version')
  26      action=cfg.actions.JointPositionAction
  27      for field,key in [('target_velocity_scale','P9_TARGET_VELOCITY_SCALE'),('smoothing_tau','P9_TARGET_SMOOTHING_TAU'),('limit_target_velocity','P9_TARGET_LIMIT_VELOCITY')]:
  28          if key not in os.environ: setattr(action,field,payload[field])
  29      for name,values in payload['actuators'].items():
  30          for field,key in [('stiffness','P9_PD_STIFFNESS_MULT'),('damping','P9_PD_DAMPING_MULT')]:
  31              if key not in os.environ: setattr(cfg.scene.robot.actuators[name],field,values[field])

SHA256 39358a0a60219fdb360df7acddf80f6a382837aa58e2ec0ccf875df4afee4825

   1  # Copyright (c) 2021-2026, ETH Zurich and NVIDIA CORPORATION
   2  # All rights reserved.
   3  #
   4  # SPDX-License-Identifier: BSD-3-Clause
   5  
   6  
   7  from __future__ import annotations
   8  
   9  import os
  10  import time
  11  import torch
  12  
  13  from rsl_rl.algorithms import PPO
  14  from rsl_rl.env import VecEnv
  15  from rsl_rl.models import MLPModel
  16  from rsl_rl.utils import check_nan, resolve_callable
  17  from rsl_rl.utils.logger import Logger
  18  
  19  
  20  class OnPolicyRunner:
  21      """On-policy runner for reinforcement learning algorithms."""
  22  
  23      alg: PPO
  24      """The actor-critic algorithm."""
  25  
  26      def __init__(self, env: VecEnv, train_cfg: dict, log_dir: str | None = None, device: str = "cpu") -> None:
  27          """Construct the runner, algorithm, and logging stack."""
  28          self.env = env
  29          self.cfg = train_cfg
  30          self.device = device
  31  
  32          # Setup multi-GPU training if enabled
  33          self._configure_multi_gpu()
  34  
  35          # Query observations from the environment for algorithm construction
  36          obs = self.env.get_observations()
  37  
  38          # Create the algorithm
  39          alg_class: type[PPO] = resolve_callable(self.cfg["algorithm"]["class_name"])  # type: ignore
  40          self.alg = alg_class.construct_algorithm(obs, self.env, self.cfg, self.device)
  41  
  42          # Create the logger
  43          self.logger = Logger(
  44              log_dir=log_dir,
  45              cfg=self.cfg,
  46              env_cfg=self.env.cfg,
  47              num_envs=self.env.num_envs,
  48              is_distributed=self.is_distributed,
  49              gpu_world_size=self.gpu_world_size,
  50              gpu_global_rank=self.gpu_global_rank,
  51              device=self.device,
  52          )
  53  
  54          self.current_learning_iteration = 0
  55  
  56      def learn(self, num_learning_iterations: int, init_at_random_ep_len: bool = False) -> None:
  57          """Run the learning loop for the specified number of iterations."""
  58          # Randomize initial episode lengths (for exploration)
  59          if init_at_random_ep_len:
  60              self.env.episode_length_buf = torch.randint_like(
  61                  self.env.episode_length_buf, high=int(self.env.max_episode_length)
  62              )
  63  
  64          # Start learning
  65          obs = self.env.get_observations().to(self.device)
  66          self.alg.train_mode()  # switch to train mode (for dropout for example)
  67  
  68          # Ensure all parameters are in-synced
  69          if self.is_distributed:
  70              print(f"Synchronizing parameters for rank {self.gpu_global_rank}...")
  71              self.alg.broadcast_parameters()
  72  
  73          # Initialize the logging writer
  74          self.logger.init_logging_writer()
  75  
  76          # Start training
  77          start_it = self.current_learning_iteration
  78          total_it = start_it + num_learning_iterations
  79          for it in range(start_it, total_it):
  80              start = time.time()
  81              # Rollout
  82              with torch.inference_mode():
  83                  for _ in range(self.cfg["num_steps_per_env"]):
  84                      # Sample actions
  85                      actions = self.alg.act(obs)
  86                      # Step the environment
  87                      obs, rewards, dones, extras = self.env.step(actions.to(self.env.device))
  88                      # Check for NaN values from the environment
  89                      if self.cfg.get("check_for_nan", True):
  90                          check_nan(obs, rewards, dones)
  91                      # Move to device
  92                      obs, rewards, dones = (obs.to(self.device), rewards.to(self.device), dones.to(self.device))
  93                      # Process the step
  94                      self.alg.process_env_step(obs, rewards, dones, extras)
  95                      # Extract intrinsic rewards if RND is used (only for logging)
  96                      intrinsic_rewards = self.alg.intrinsic_rewards if self.cfg["algorithm"]["rnd_cfg"] else None
  97                      # Book keeping
  98                      self.logger.process_env_step(rewards, dones, extras, intrinsic_rewards)
  99  
 100                  stop = time.time()
 101                  collect_time = stop - start
 102                  start = stop
 103  
 104                  # Compute returns
 105                  self.alg.compute_returns(obs)
 106  
 107              # Update policy
 108              loss_dict = self.alg.update()
 109  
 110              stop = time.time()
 111              learn_time = stop - start
 112              self.current_learning_iteration = it
 113  
 114              # Log information
 115              self.logger.log(
 116                  it=it,
 117                  start_it=start_it,
 118                  total_it=total_it,
 119                  collect_time=collect_time,
 120                  learn_time=learn_time,
 121                  loss_dict=loss_dict,
 122                  learning_rate=self.alg.learning_rate,
 123                  action_std=self.alg.get_policy().output_std,
 124                  rnd_weight=self.alg.rnd.weight if self.cfg["algorithm"]["rnd_cfg"] else None,
 125              )
 126  
 127              # Save model
 128              if self.logger.writer is not None and it % self.cfg["save_interval"] == 0:
 129                  self.save(os.path.join(self.logger.log_dir, f"model_{it}.pt"))  # type: ignore
 130  
 131          # Save the final model after training and stop the logging writer
 132          if self.logger.writer is not None:
 133              self.save(os.path.join(self.logger.log_dir, f"model_{self.current_learning_iteration}.pt"))  # type: ignore
 134              self.logger.stop_logging_writer()
 135  
 136      def save(self, path: str, infos: dict | None = None) -> None:
 137          """Save the models and training state to a given path and upload them if external logging is used."""
 138          saved_dict = self.alg.save()
 139          saved_dict["iter"] = self.current_learning_iteration
 140          saved_dict["infos"] = infos
 141          torch.save(saved_dict, path)
 142          # Upload model to external logging services
 143          self.logger.save_model(path, self.current_learning_iteration)
 144  
 145      def load(
 146          self, path: str, load_cfg: dict | None = None, strict: bool = True, map_location: str | None = None
 147      ) -> dict:
 148          """Load the models and training state from a given path.
 149  
 150          Args:
 151              path (str): Path to load the model from.
 152              load_cfg (dict | None): Optional dictionary that defines what models and states to load. If None, all
 153                  models and states are loaded.
 154              strict (bool): Whether state_dict loading should be strict.
 155              map_location (str | None): Device mapping for loading the model.
 156          """
 157          loaded_dict = torch.load(path, weights_only=False, map_location=map_location)
 158          load_iteration = self.alg.load(loaded_dict, load_cfg, strict)
 159          if load_iteration:
 160              self.current_learning_iteration = loaded_dict["iter"]
 161          return loaded_dict["infos"]
 162  
 163      def get_inference_policy(self, device: str | None = None) -> MLPModel:
 164          """Return the policy on the requested device for inference."""
 165          self.alg.eval_mode()  # Switch to evaluation mode (e.g. for dropout)
 166          return self.alg.get_policy().to(device)  # type: ignore
 167  
 168      def export_policy_to_jit(self, path: str, filename: str = "policy.pt") -> None:
 169          """Export the model to a Torch JIT file."""
 170          jit_model = self.alg.get_policy().as_jit()
 171          jit_model.to("cpu")
 172  
 173          if not os.path.exists(path):
 174              os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
 175          save_path = os.path.join(path, filename)
 176  
 177          # Trace and save the model
 178          traced_model = torch.jit.script(jit_model)
 179          traced_model.save(save_path)
 180  
 181      def export_policy_to_onnx(self, path: str, filename: str = "policy.onnx", verbose: bool = False) -> None:
 182          """Export the model into an ONNX file."""
 183          onnx_model = self.alg.get_policy().as_onnx(verbose=verbose)
 184          onnx_model.to("cpu")
 185          onnx_model.eval()
 186  
 187          if not os.path.exists(path):
 188              os.makedirs(path, exist_ok=True)
 189          save_path = os.path.join(path, filename)
 190  
 191          # Trace and save the model
 192          torch.onnx.export(
 193              onnx_model,
 194              onnx_model.get_dummy_inputs(),  # type: ignore
 195              save_path,
 196              export_params=True,
 197              opset_version=18,
 198              verbose=verbose,
 199              input_names=onnx_model.input_names,  # type: ignore
 200              output_names=onnx_model.output_names,  # type: ignore
 201          )
 202  
 203      def add_git_repo_to_log(self, repo_file_path: str) -> None:
 204          """Register a repository path whose git status should be logged."""
 205          self.logger.git_status_repos.append(repo_file_path)
 206  
 207      def _configure_multi_gpu(self) -> None:
 208          """Configure multi-gpu training."""
 209          # Check if distributed training is enabled
 210          self.gpu_world_size = int(os.getenv("WORLD_SIZE", "1"))
 211          self.is_distributed = self.gpu_world_size > 1
 212  
 213          # If not distributed training, set local and global rank to 0 and return
 214          if not self.is_distributed:
 215              self.gpu_local_rank = 0
 216              self.gpu_global_rank = 0
 217              self.cfg["multi_gpu"] = None
 218              return
 219  
 220          # Get rank and world size
 221          self.gpu_local_rank = int(os.getenv("LOCAL_RANK", "0"))
 222          self.gpu_global_rank = int(os.getenv("RANK", "0"))
 223  
 224          # Make a configuration dictionary
 225          self.cfg["multi_gpu"] = {
 226              "global_rank": self.gpu_global_rank,  # Rank of the main process
 227              "local_rank": self.gpu_local_rank,  # Rank of the current process
 228              "world_size": self.gpu_world_size,  # Total number of processes
 229          }
 230  
 231          # Check if user has device specified for local rank
 232          if self.device != f"cuda:{self.gpu_local_rank}":
 233              raise ValueError(
 234                  f"Device '{self.device}' does not match expected device for local rank '{self.gpu_local_rank}'."
 235              )
 236          # Validate multi-GPU configuration
 237          if self.gpu_local_rank >= self.gpu_world_size:
 238              raise ValueError(
 239                  f"Local rank '{self.gpu_local_rank}' is greater than or equal to world size '{self.gpu_world_size}'."
 240              )
 241          if self.gpu_global_rank >= self.gpu_world_size:
 242              raise ValueError(
 243                  f"Global rank '{self.gpu_global_rank}' is greater than or equal to world size '{self.gpu_world_size}'."
 244              )
 245  
 246          # Initialize torch distributed
 247          torch.distributed.init_process_group(backend="nccl", rank=self.gpu_global_rank, world_size=self.gpu_world_size)
 248          # Set device to the local rank
 249          torch.cuda.set_device(self.gpu_local_rank)
