SHA256 a2d35e7ad7b884c80b7434e7d2ce785a6da1e18d93c96f1179fbd3a208669f8c

   1  # Copyright (c) 2021-2026, ETH Zurich and NVIDIA CORPORATION
   2  # All rights reserved.
   3  #
   4  # SPDX-License-Identifier: BSD-3-Clause
   5  
   6  
   7  from __future__ import annotations
   8  
   9  import torch
  10  import torch.nn as nn
  11  import torch.optim as optim
  12  from itertools import chain
  13  from tensordict import TensorDict
  14  
  15  from rsl_rl.env import VecEnv
  16  from rsl_rl.extensions import RandomNetworkDistillation, resolve_rnd_config, resolve_symmetry_config
  17  from rsl_rl.models import MLPModel
  18  from rsl_rl.storage import RolloutStorage
  19  from rsl_rl.utils import resolve_callable, resolve_obs_groups, resolve_optimizer
  20  
  21  
  22  class PPO:
  23      """Proximal Policy Optimization algorithm.
  24  
  25      Reference:
  26          - Schulman et al. "Proximal policy optimization algorithms." arXiv preprint arXiv:1707.06347 (2017).
  27      """
  28  
  29      actor: MLPModel
  30      """The actor model."""
  31  
  32      critic: MLPModel
  33      """The critic model."""
  34  
  35      def __init__(
  36          self,
  37          actor: MLPModel,
  38          critic: MLPModel,
  39          storage: RolloutStorage,
  40          num_learning_epochs: int = 5,
  41          num_mini_batches: int = 4,
  42          clip_param: float = 0.2,
  43          gamma: float = 0.99,
  44          lam: float = 0.95,
  45          value_loss_coef: float = 1.0,
  46          entropy_coef: float = 0.01,
  47          learning_rate: float = 0.001,
  48          max_grad_norm: float = 1.0,
  49          optimizer: str = "adam",
  50          use_clipped_value_loss: bool = True,
  51          schedule: str = "adaptive",
  52          desired_kl: float = 0.01,
  53          normalize_advantage_per_mini_batch: bool = False,
  54          device: str = "cpu",
  55          # RND parameters
  56          rnd_cfg: dict | None = None,
  57          # Symmetry parameters
  58          symmetry_cfg: dict | None = None,
  59          # Distributed training parameters
  60          multi_gpu_cfg: dict | None = None,
  61      ) -> None:
  62          """Initialize the algorithm with models, storage, and optimization settings."""
  63          # Device-related parameters
  64          self.device = device
  65          self.is_multi_gpu = multi_gpu_cfg is not None
  66  
  67          # Multi-GPU parameters
  68          if multi_gpu_cfg is not None:
  69              self.gpu_global_rank = multi_gpu_cfg["global_rank"]
  70              self.gpu_world_size = multi_gpu_cfg["world_size"]
  71          else:
  72              self.gpu_global_rank = 0
  73              self.gpu_world_size = 1
  74  
  75          # RND components
  76          if rnd_cfg:
  77              # Extract parameters used in ppo
  78              rnd_lr = rnd_cfg.pop("learning_rate", 1e-3)
  79              # Create RND module
  80              self.rnd = RandomNetworkDistillation(device=self.device, **rnd_cfg)
  81              # Create RND optimizer
  82              params = self.rnd.predictor.parameters()
  83              self.rnd_optimizer = optim.Adam(params, lr=rnd_lr)
  84          else:
  85              self.rnd = None
  86              self.rnd_optimizer = None
  87  
  88          # Symmetry components
  89          if symmetry_cfg is not None:
  90              # Check if symmetry is enabled
  91              use_symmetry = symmetry_cfg["use_data_augmentation"] or symmetry_cfg["use_mirror_loss"]
  92              # Print that we are not using symmetry
  93              if not use_symmetry:
  94                  print("Symmetry not used for learning. We will use it for logging instead.")
  95              # Resolve the data augmentation function (supports string names or direct callables)
  96              symmetry_cfg["data_augmentation_func"] = resolve_callable(symmetry_cfg["data_augmentation_func"])
  97              # Check valid configuration
  98              if not callable(symmetry_cfg["data_augmentation_func"]):
  99                  raise ValueError(
 100                      f"Symmetry configuration exists but the function is not callable: "
 101                      f"{symmetry_cfg['data_augmentation_func']}"
 102                  )
 103              # Check if the policy is compatible with symmetry
 104              if actor.is_recurrent or critic.is_recurrent:
 105                  raise ValueError("Symmetry augmentation is not supported for recurrent policies.")
 106              # Store symmetry configuration
 107              self.symmetry = symmetry_cfg
 108          else:
 109              self.symmetry = None
 110  
 111          # PPO components
 112          self.actor = actor.to(self.device)
 113          self.critic = critic.to(self.device)
 114  
 115          # Create the optimizer
 116          self.optimizer = resolve_optimizer(optimizer)(
 117              chain(self.actor.parameters(), self.critic.parameters()), lr=learning_rate
 118          )  # type: ignore
 119  
 120          # Add storage
 121          self.storage = storage
 122          self.transition = RolloutStorage.Transition()
 123  
 124          # PPO parameters
 125          self.clip_param = clip_param
 126          self.num_learning_epochs = num_learning_epochs
 127          self.num_mini_batches = num_mini_batches
 128          self.value_loss_coef = value_loss_coef
 129          self.entropy_coef = entropy_coef
 130          self.gamma = gamma
 131          self.lam = lam
 132          self.max_grad_norm = max_grad_norm
 133          self.use_clipped_value_loss = use_clipped_value_loss
 134          self.desired_kl = desired_kl
 135          self.schedule = schedule
 136          self.learning_rate = learning_rate
 137          self.normalize_advantage_per_mini_batch = normalize_advantage_per_mini_batch
 138  
 139      def act(self, obs: TensorDict) -> torch.Tensor:
 140          """Sample actions and store transition data."""
 141          # Record the hidden states for recurrent policies
 142          self.transition.hidden_states = (self.actor.get_hidden_state(), self.critic.get_hidden_state())
 143          # Compute the actions and values
 144          self.transition.actions = self.actor(obs, stochastic_output=True).detach()
 145          self.transition.values = self.critic(obs).detach()
 146          self.transition.actions_log_prob = self.actor.get_output_log_prob(self.transition.actions).detach()  # type: ignore
 147          self.transition.distribution_params = tuple(p.detach() for p in self.actor.output_distribution_params)
 148          # Record observations before env.step()
 149          self.transition.observations = obs
 150          return self.transition.actions  # type: ignore
 151  
 152      def process_env_step(
 153          self, obs: TensorDict, rewards: torch.Tensor, dones: torch.Tensor, extras: dict[str, torch.Tensor]
 154      ) -> None:
 155          """Record one environment step and update the normalizers."""
 156          # Update the normalizers
 157          self.actor.update_normalization(obs)
 158          self.critic.update_normalization(obs)
 159          if self.rnd:
 160              self.rnd.update_normalization(obs)
 161  
 162          # Record the rewards and dones
 163          # Note: We clone here because later on we bootstrap the rewards based on timeouts
 164          self.transition.rewards = rewards.clone()
 165          self.transition.dones = dones
 166  
 167          # Compute the intrinsic rewards and add to extrinsic rewards
 168          if self.rnd:
 169              # Compute the intrinsic rewards
 170              self.intrinsic_rewards = self.rnd.get_intrinsic_reward(obs)
 171              # Add intrinsic rewards to extrinsic rewards
 172              self.transition.rewards += self.intrinsic_rewards
 173  
 174          # Bootstrapping on time outs
 175          if "time_outs" in extras:
 176              self.transition.rewards += self.gamma * torch.squeeze(
 177                  self.transition.values * extras["time_outs"].unsqueeze(1).to(self.device),  # type: ignore
 178                  1,
 179              )
 180  
 181          # Record the transition
 182          self.storage.add_transition(self.transition)
 183          self.transition.clear()
 184          self.actor.reset(dones)
 185          self.critic.reset(dones)
 186  
 187      def compute_returns(self, obs: TensorDict) -> None:
 188          """Compute return and advantage targets from stored transitions."""
 189          st = self.storage
 190          # Compute value for the last step
 191          last_values = self.critic(obs).detach()
 192          # Compute returns and advantages
 193          advantage = 0
 194          for step in reversed(range(st.num_transitions_per_env)):
 195              # If we are at the last step, bootstrap the return value
 196              next_values = last_values if step == st.num_transitions_per_env - 1 else st.values[step + 1]
 197              # 1 if we are not in a terminal state, 0 otherwise
 198              next_is_not_terminal = 1.0 - st.dones[step].float()
 199              # TD error: r_t + gamma * V(s_{t+1}) - V(s_t)
 200              delta = st.rewards[step] + next_is_not_terminal * self.gamma * next_values - st.values[step]
 201              # Advantage: A(s_t, a_t) = delta_t + gamma * lambda * A(s_{t+1}, a_{t+1})
 202              advantage = delta + next_is_not_terminal * self.gamma * self.lam * advantage
 203              # Return: R_t = A(s_t, a_t) + V(s_t)
 204              st.returns[step] = advantage + st.values[step]
 205          # Compute the advantages
 206          st.advantages = st.returns - st.values
 207          # Normalize the advantages if per minibatch normalization is not used
 208          if not self.normalize_advantage_per_mini_batch:
 209              st.advantages = (st.advantages - st.advantages.mean()) / (st.advantages.std() + 1e-8)
 210  
 211      def update(self) -> dict[str, float]:
 212          """Run optimization epochs over stored batches and return mean losses."""
 213          mean_value_loss = 0
 214          mean_surrogate_loss = 0
 215          mean_entropy = 0
 216          # RND loss
 217          mean_rnd_loss = 0 if self.rnd else None
 218          # Symmetry loss
 219          mean_symmetry_loss = 0 if self.symmetry else None
 220  
 221          # Get mini batch generator
 222          if self.actor.is_recurrent or self.critic.is_recurrent:
 223              generator = self.storage.recurrent_mini_batch_generator(self.num_mini_batches, self.num_learning_epochs)
 224          else:
 225              generator = self.storage.mini_batch_generator(self.num_mini_batches, self.num_learning_epochs)
 226  
 227          # Iterate over batches
 228          for batch in generator:
 229              original_batch_size = batch.observations.batch_size[0]
 230  
 231              # Check if we should normalize advantages per mini batch
 232              if self.normalize_advantage_per_mini_batch:
 233                  with torch.no_grad():
 234                      batch.advantages = (batch.advantages - batch.advantages.mean()) / (batch.advantages.std() + 1e-8)  # type: ignore
 235  
 236              # Perform symmetric augmentation
 237              if self.symmetry and self.symmetry["use_data_augmentation"]:
 238                  # Augmentation using symmetry
 239                  data_augmentation_func = self.symmetry["data_augmentation_func"]
 240                  # Returned shape: [batch_size * num_aug, ...]
 241                  batch.observations, batch.actions = data_augmentation_func(
 242                      env=self.symmetry["_env"],
 243                      obs=batch.observations,
 244                      actions=batch.actions,
 245                  )
 246                  # Compute number of augmentations per sample
 247                  num_aug = int(batch.observations.batch_size[0] / original_batch_size)
 248                  # Repeat the rest of the batch
 249                  batch.old_actions_log_prob = batch.old_actions_log_prob.repeat(num_aug, 1)
 250                  batch.values = batch.values.repeat(num_aug, 1)
 251                  batch.advantages = batch.advantages.repeat(num_aug, 1)
 252                  batch.returns = batch.returns.repeat(num_aug, 1)
 253  
 254              # Recompute actions log prob and entropy for current batch of transitions
 255              # Note: We need to do this because we updated the policy with the new parameters
 256              self.actor(
 257                  batch.observations,
 258                  masks=batch.masks,
 259                  hidden_state=batch.hidden_states[0],
 260                  stochastic_output=True,
 261              )
 262              actions_log_prob = self.actor.get_output_log_prob(batch.actions)  # type: ignore
 263              values = self.critic(batch.observations, masks=batch.masks, hidden_state=batch.hidden_states[1])
 264              # Note: We only keep the distribution parameters and entropy of the first augmentation (the original one)
 265              distribution_params = tuple(p[:original_batch_size] for p in self.actor.output_distribution_params)
 266              entropy = self.actor.output_entropy[:original_batch_size]
 267  
 268              # Compute KL divergence and adapt the learning rate
 269              if self.desired_kl is not None and self.schedule == "adaptive":
 270                  with torch.inference_mode():
 271                      kl = self.actor.get_kl_divergence(batch.old_distribution_params, distribution_params)  # type: ignore
 272                      kl_mean = torch.mean(kl)
 273  
 274                      # Reduce the KL divergence across all GPUs
 275                      if self.is_multi_gpu:
 276                          torch.distributed.all_reduce(kl_mean, op=torch.distributed.ReduceOp.SUM)
 277                          kl_mean /= self.gpu_world_size
 278  
 279                      # Update the learning rate only on the main process
 280                      if self.gpu_global_rank == 0:
 281                          if kl_mean > self.desired_kl * 2.0:
 282                              self.learning_rate = max(1e-5, self.learning_rate / 1.5)
 283                          elif kl_mean < self.desired_kl / 2.0 and kl_mean > 0.0:
 284                              self.learning_rate = min(1e-2, self.learning_rate * 1.5)
 285  
 286                      # Update the learning rate for all GPUs
 287                      if self.is_multi_gpu:
 288                          lr_tensor = torch.tensor(self.learning_rate, device=self.device)
 289                          torch.distributed.broadcast(lr_tensor, src=0)
 290                          self.learning_rate = lr_tensor.item()
 291  
 292                      # Update the learning rate for all parameter groups
 293                      for param_group in self.optimizer.param_groups:
 294                          param_group["lr"] = self.learning_rate
 295  
 296              # Surrogate loss
 297              ratio = torch.exp(actions_log_prob - torch.squeeze(batch.old_actions_log_prob))  # type: ignore
 298              surrogate = -torch.squeeze(batch.advantages) * ratio  # type: ignore
 299              surrogate_clipped = -torch.squeeze(batch.advantages) * torch.clamp(  # type: ignore
 300                  ratio, 1.0 - self.clip_param, 1.0 + self.clip_param
 301              )
 302              surrogate_loss = torch.max(surrogate, surrogate_clipped).mean()
 303  
 304              # Value function loss
 305              if self.use_clipped_value_loss:
 306                  value_clipped = batch.values + (values - batch.values).clamp(-self.clip_param, self.clip_param)
 307                  value_losses = (values - batch.returns).pow(2)
 308                  value_losses_clipped = (value_clipped - batch.returns).pow(2)
 309                  value_loss = torch.max(value_losses, value_losses_clipped).mean()
 310              else:
 311                  value_loss = (batch.returns - values).pow(2).mean()
 312  
 313              loss = surrogate_loss + self.value_loss_coef * value_loss - self.entropy_coef * entropy.mean()
 314  
 315              # Symmetry loss
 316              if self.symmetry:
 317                  # Obtain the symmetric actions
 318                  # Note: If we did augmentation before then we don't need to augment again
 319                  if not self.symmetry["use_data_augmentation"]:
 320                      data_augmentation_func = self.symmetry["data_augmentation_func"]
 321                      batch.observations, _ = data_augmentation_func(
 322                          obs=batch.observations, actions=None, env=self.symmetry["_env"]
 323                      )
 324  
 325                  # Actions predicted by the actor for symmetrically-augmented observations
 326                  mean_actions = self.actor(batch.observations.detach().clone())
 327  
 328                  # Compute the symmetrically augmented actions
 329                  # Note: We are assuming the first augmentation is the original one. We do not use the batch.actions from
 330                  # earlier since that action was sampled from the distribution. However, the symmetry loss is computed
 331                  # using the mean of the distribution.
 332                  action_mean_orig = mean_actions[:original_batch_size]
 333                  _, actions_mean_symm = data_augmentation_func(
 334                      obs=None, actions=action_mean_orig, env=self.symmetry["_env"]
 335                  )
 336  
 337                  # Compute the loss
 338                  mse_loss = torch.nn.MSELoss()
 339                  symmetry_loss = mse_loss(
 340                      mean_actions[original_batch_size:], actions_mean_symm.detach()[original_batch_size:]
 341                  )
 342                  # Add the loss to the total loss
 343                  if self.symmetry["use_mirror_loss"]:
 344                      loss += self.symmetry["mirror_loss_coeff"] * symmetry_loss
 345                  else:
 346                      symmetry_loss = symmetry_loss.detach()
 347  
 348              # RND loss
 349              if self.rnd:
 350                  # Extract the rnd_state
 351                  with torch.no_grad():
 352                      rnd_state = self.rnd.get_rnd_state(batch.observations[:original_batch_size])  # type: ignore
 353                      rnd_state = self.rnd.state_normalizer(rnd_state)
 354                  # Predict the embedding and the target
 355                  predicted_embedding = self.rnd.predictor(rnd_state)
 356                  target_embedding = self.rnd.target(rnd_state).detach()
 357                  # Compute the loss as the mean squared error
 358                  mseloss = torch.nn.MSELoss()
 359                  rnd_loss = mseloss(predicted_embedding, target_embedding)
 360  
 361              # Compute the gradients for PPO
 362              self.optimizer.zero_grad()
 363              loss.backward()
 364              # Compute the gradients for RND
 365              if self.rnd:
 366                  self.rnd_optimizer.zero_grad()
 367                  rnd_loss.backward()
 368  
 369              # Collect gradients from all GPUs
 370              if self.is_multi_gpu:
 371                  self.reduce_parameters()
 372  
 373              # Apply the gradients for PPO
 374              nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.actor.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)
 375              nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.critic.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)
 376              self.optimizer.step()
 377              # Apply the gradients for RND
 378              if self.rnd_optimizer:
 379                  self.rnd_optimizer.step()
 380  
 381              # Store the losses
 382              mean_value_loss += value_loss.item()
 383              mean_surrogate_loss += surrogate_loss.item()
 384              mean_entropy += entropy.mean().item()
 385              # RND loss
 386              if mean_rnd_loss is not None:
 387                  mean_rnd_loss += rnd_loss.item()
 388              # Symmetry loss
 389              if mean_symmetry_loss is not None:
 390                  mean_symmetry_loss += symmetry_loss.item()
 391  
 392          # Divide the losses by the number of updates
 393          num_updates = self.num_learning_epochs * self.num_mini_batches
 394          mean_value_loss /= num_updates
 395          mean_surrogate_loss /= num_updates
 396          mean_entropy /= num_updates
 397          if mean_rnd_loss is not None:
 398              mean_rnd_loss /= num_updates
 399          if mean_symmetry_loss is not None:
 400              mean_symmetry_loss /= num_updates
 401  
 402          # Clear the storage
 403          self.storage.clear()
 404  
 405          # Construct the loss dictionary
 406          loss_dict = {
 407              "value": mean_value_loss,
 408              "surrogate": mean_surrogate_loss,
 409              "entropy": mean_entropy,
 410          }
 411          if self.rnd:
 412              loss_dict["rnd"] = mean_rnd_loss
 413          if self.symmetry:
 414              loss_dict["symmetry"] = mean_symmetry_loss
 415  
 416          return loss_dict
 417  
 418      def train_mode(self) -> None:
 419          """Set train mode for learnable models."""
 420          self.actor.train()
 421          self.critic.train()
 422          if self.rnd:
 423              self.rnd.train()
 424  
 425      def eval_mode(self) -> None:
 426          """Set evaluation mode for learnable models."""
 427          self.actor.eval()
 428          self.critic.eval()
 429          if self.rnd:
 430              self.rnd.eval()
 431  
 432      def save(self) -> dict:
 433          """Return a dict of all models for saving."""
 434          saved_dict = {
 435              "actor_state_dict": self.actor.state_dict(),
 436              "critic_state_dict": self.critic.state_dict(),
 437              "optimizer_state_dict": self.optimizer.state_dict(),
 438          }
 439          if self.rnd:
 440              saved_dict["rnd_state_dict"] = self.rnd.state_dict()
 441              saved_dict["rnd_optimizer_state_dict"] = self.rnd_optimizer.state_dict()
 442          return saved_dict
 443  
 444      def load(self, loaded_dict: dict, load_cfg: dict | None, strict: bool) -> bool:
 445          """Load specified models from a saved dict."""
 446          # If no load_cfg is provided, load all models and states
 447          if load_cfg is None:
 448              load_cfg = {
 449                  "actor": True,
 450                  "critic": True,
 451                  "optimizer": True,
 452                  "iteration": True,
 453                  "rnd": True,
 454              }
 455  
 456          # Load the specified models
 457          if load_cfg.get("actor"):
 458              self.actor.load_state_dict(loaded_dict["actor_state_dict"], strict=strict)
 459          if load_cfg.get("critic"):
 460              self.critic.load_state_dict(loaded_dict["critic_state_dict"], strict=strict)
 461          if load_cfg.get("optimizer"):
 462              self.optimizer.load_state_dict(loaded_dict["optimizer_state_dict"])
 463          if load_cfg.get("rnd") and self.rnd:
 464              self.rnd.load_state_dict(loaded_dict["rnd_state_dict"], strict=strict)
 465              self.rnd_optimizer.load_state_dict(loaded_dict["rnd_optimizer_state_dict"])
 466          return load_cfg.get("iteration", False)
 467  
 468      def get_policy(self) -> MLPModel:
 469          """Get the policy model."""
 470          return self.actor
 471  
 472      @staticmethod
 473      def construct_algorithm(obs: TensorDict, env: VecEnv, cfg: dict, device: str) -> PPO:
 474          """Construct the PPO algorithm."""
 475          # Resolve class callables
 476          alg_class: type[PPO] = resolve_callable(cfg["algorithm"].pop("class_name"))  # type: ignore
 477          actor_class: type[MLPModel] = resolve_callable(cfg["actor"].pop("class_name"))  # type: ignore
 478          critic_class: type[MLPModel] = resolve_callable(cfg["critic"].pop("class_name"))  # type: ignore
 479  
 480          # Resolve observation groups
 481          default_sets = ["actor", "critic"]
 482          if "rnd_cfg" in cfg["algorithm"] and cfg["algorithm"]["rnd_cfg"] is not None:
 483              default_sets.append("rnd_state")
 484          cfg["obs_groups"] = resolve_obs_groups(obs, cfg["obs_groups"], default_sets)
 485  
 486          # Resolve RND config if used
 487          cfg["algorithm"] = resolve_rnd_config(cfg["algorithm"], obs, cfg["obs_groups"], env)
 488  
 489          # Resolve symmetry config if used
 490          cfg["algorithm"] = resolve_symmetry_config(cfg["algorithm"], env)
 491  
 492          # Initialize the policy
 493          actor: MLPModel = actor_class(obs, cfg["obs_groups"], "actor", env.num_actions, **cfg["actor"]).to(device)
 494          print(f"Actor Model: {actor}")
 495          if cfg["algorithm"].pop("share_cnn_encoders", None):  # Share CNN encoders between actor and critic
 496              cfg["critic"]["cnns"] = actor.cnns  # type: ignore
 497          critic: MLPModel = critic_class(obs, cfg["obs_groups"], "critic", 1, **cfg["critic"]).to(device)
 498          print(f"Critic Model: {critic}")
 499  
 500          # Initialize the storage
 501          storage = RolloutStorage("rl", env.num_envs, cfg["num_steps_per_env"], obs, [env.num_actions], device)
 502  
 503          # Initialize the algorithm
 504          alg: PPO = alg_class(actor, critic, storage, device=device, **cfg["algorithm"], multi_gpu_cfg=cfg["multi_gpu"])
 505  
 506          return alg
 507  
 508      def broadcast_parameters(self) -> None:
 509          """Broadcast model parameters to all GPUs."""
 510          # Obtain the model parameters on current GPU
 511          model_params = [self.actor.state_dict(), self.critic.state_dict()]
 512          if self.rnd:
 513              model_params.append(self.rnd.predictor.state_dict())
 514          # Broadcast the model parameters
 515          torch.distributed.broadcast_object_list(model_params, src=0)
 516          # Load the model parameters on all GPUs from source GPU
 517          self.actor.load_state_dict(model_params[0])
 518          self.critic.load_state_dict(model_params[1])
 519          if self.rnd:
 520              self.rnd.predictor.load_state_dict(model_params[2])
 521  
 522      def reduce_parameters(self) -> None:
 523          """Collect gradients from all GPUs and average them.
 524  
 525          This function is called after the backward pass to synchronize the gradients across all GPUs.
 526          """
 527          # Create a tensor to store the gradients
 528          all_params = chain(self.actor.parameters(), self.critic.parameters())
 529          if self.rnd:
 530              all_params = chain(all_params, self.rnd.parameters())
 531          all_params = list(all_params)
 532          grads = [param.grad.view(-1) for param in all_params if param.grad is not None]
 533          all_grads = torch.cat(grads)
 534          # Average the gradients across all GPUs
 535          torch.distributed.all_reduce(all_grads, op=torch.distributed.ReduceOp.SUM)
 536          all_grads /= self.gpu_world_size
 537          # Update the gradients for all parameters with the reduced gradients
 538          offset = 0
 539          for param in all_params:
 540              if param.grad is not None:
 541                  numel = param.numel()
 542                  # Copy data back from shared buffer
 543                  param.grad.data.copy_(all_grads[offset : offset + numel].view_as(param.grad.data))
 544                  # Update the offset for the next parameter
 545                  offset += numel

正文是否与实现一致？

TW-81文档交付不代表TW-75或S2训练验收完成。
